## ג (id: BDB01542)

ג, גּ, *Gîmel*, third letter; in postB Heb. \= numeral 3 (and so margin of printed MT); Gîmel (with diaresis) \= 3000; no evidence of this usage in OT times.

> \[Str1341] גֵּא v. sub. גאה. below



## גָּאָה (id: BDB01544)

\[Str1342] † גָּאָה **vb.** rise up (Aram. Pe. (Targum [1 S 2:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:5)) \& oft. Ethp. אֶתְגָּאֵי, ܒܕ *be boastful, proud*, ef. also NH)—**Qal** *Pf.* ´ג [Ex 15:1](https://ref.ly/Exod15:1), [15:21](https://ref.ly/Exod15:21), גָּאוּ Ez [47:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:5); *Impf.* יִגְאֶה [Jb 8:11](https://ref.ly/Job8:11), [10:16](https://ref.ly/Job10:16); *Inf.* abs. גָּאֹה [Ex 15:1](https://ref.ly/Exod15:1), [15:21](https://ref.ly/Exod15:21); —**1\.** *rise up*, of waters Ez [47:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:5). **2\.** *grow up*, of plants [Jb 8:11](https://ref.ly/Job8:11). **3\.** *be lifted up, exalted*, of head [Jb 10:16](https://ref.ly/Job10:16), of ´י in triumph [Ex 15:1](https://ref.ly/Exod15:1), [15:21](https://ref.ly/Exod15:21).

> \[Str1341] † גֵּא **adj.** proud, scribal error for גֵּאֶה [Is 16:6](https://ref.ly/Isa16:6) (as in [Je 48:29](https://ref.ly/Jer48:29)).

> \[Str1343] † גֵּאֶה **adj.** proud, [Jb 40:11](https://ref.ly/Job40:11), [40:12](https://ref.ly/Job40:12), [Is 2:12](https://ref.ly/Isa2:12), [Je 48:29](https://ref.ly/Jer48:29); pl. גֵּאִים [Psalm 94:2](https://ref.ly/Ps94:2), [140:6](https://ref.ly/Ps140:6), [Pr 15:25](https://ref.ly/Prov15:25), [16:19](https://ref.ly/Prov16:19); cstr. גְּאֵי [Psalm 123:4](https://ref.ly/Ps123:4) Qr גאי יונים (\> Kt גאייונים), yet cf. De al.

> \[Str1344] † גֵּאָה **n.f.** pride, [Pr 8:13](https://ref.ly/Prov8:13).

> \[Str1346] † גַּאֲוָה **n.f.** majesty, pride (cf. Syr. ܓܐܝܘܬܐ)—[Jb 41:7](https://ref.ly/Job41:7) \+ 6 t.; cstr. גַּאֲוַת [Pr 29:23](https://ref.ly/Prov29:23) \+ 2 t.; sf. גַּאֲוָתִי [Is 13:3](https://ref.ly/Isa13:3) \+ 8 t.;—**1\.** *rising up, swelling* of the sea [Psalm 46:4](https://ref.ly/Ps46:4). **2\.** *majesty*, of Israel [Dt 33:29](https://ref.ly/Deut33:29), Moab [Is 16:6](https://ref.ly/Isa16:6) \= [Je 48:29](https://ref.ly/Jer48:29), scales of crocodile [Jb 41:7](https://ref.ly/Job41:7), of God [Dt 33:26](https://ref.ly/Deut33:26), [Psalm 68:35](https://ref.ly/Ps68:35). **3\.** *pride, haughtiness* [Psalm 10:2](https://ref.ly/Ps10:2), [31:19](https://ref.ly/Ps31:19), [31:24](https://ref.ly/Ps31:24), [36:12](https://ref.ly/Ps36:12), [73:6](https://ref.ly/Ps73:6), [Pr 14:3](https://ref.ly/Prov14:3), [29:23](https://ref.ly/Prov29:23), [Is 9:8](https://ref.ly/Isa9:8), [13:11](https://ref.ly/Isa13:11), [25:11](https://ref.ly/Isa25:11); עליזי גאותי *my proudly exulting ones* [Is 13:3](https://ref.ly/Isa13:3) cf. [Zp 3:11](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:11).

> \[Str1347] † גָּאוֺן **n.m.**:[Ho 5:5](https://ref.ly/Hos5:5) exaltation—[Jb 40:10](https://ref.ly/Job40:10) \+ 5 t.; cstr. גְּאוֺן [Lv 26:19](https://ref.ly/Lev26:19) \+ 31 t.; sf. גְּאוֺנְךָ etc. [Ex 15:7](https://ref.ly/Exod15:7) \+ 9 t.; pl. sf. גְּאוָֺ\+ניִח Ez [16:56](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:56); —**1\.** *exaltation, majesty, excellence*, **a.** of nations, their wealth, power, magnificence of buildings, e.g. Egypt Ez [32:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:12), Chaldeans [Is 13:11](https://ref.ly/Isa13:11), [13:19](https://ref.ly/Isa13:19), [14:11](https://ref.ly/Isa14:11), Philistines [Zc 9:6](https://ref.ly/Zech9:6), Assyria [Zc 10:11](https://ref.ly/Zech10:11), Jacob [Psalm 47:5](https://ref.ly/Ps47:5), [Am 6:8](https://ref.ly/Amos6:8), [8:7](https://ref.ly/Amos8:7), [Na 2:3](https://ref.ly/Nah2:3), Israel [Ho 5:5](https://ref.ly/Hos5:5), [7:10](https://ref.ly/Hos7:10) (prob. appellation of ´י), [Na 2:3](https://ref.ly/Nah2:3), Judah [Je 13:9](https://ref.ly/Jer13:9), Jerusalem v:[9](https://ref.ly/Jer13:9) Ez [16:56](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:56); גְּאוֺן עֻזָּהּ *pride of her strength* Ez [30:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:6), [30:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:18), [33:28](https://ref.ly/Ezek33:28); גאון עזים Ez [7:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek7:24) (but LXX Ew Hi Co rd. עזם); גאון עזכם [Lv 26:19](https://ref.ly/Lev26:19), Ez [24:21](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:21); the fruit of land of Judah will become לגאון ולתפארת *majestic and beautiful* [Is 4:2](https://ref.ly/Isa4:2); גאון כל צבי *the majesty of all the splendour* (of Tyre) [Is 23:9](https://ref.ly/Isa23:9); Zion is to become גאון עולם *an everlasting excellency* [Is 60:15](https://ref.ly/Isa60:15). **b.** of God [Ex 15:7](https://ref.ly/Exod15:7), [Is 24:14](https://ref.ly/Isa24:14), [Mi 5:3](https://ref.ly/Mic5:3); הֲדַר גְּאֹנוֺ [Is 2:10](https://ref.ly/Isa2:10), [2:19](https://ref.ly/Isa2:19), [2:21](https://ref.ly/Isa2:21); יַרְעֵם בְּקוֺל גְּאוֺנוֺ [Jb 37:4](https://ref.ly/Job37:4); עֲדֵה נָא גָּאוֺן וָגֹבַהּ [Jb 40:10](https://ref.ly/Job40:10). **c.** גאון הירדן *majesty of Jordan*, referring to the green and shady banks, clothed with willows, tamarisks, and cane, in which the lions made their covert [Je 49:19](https://ref.ly/Jer49:19), [50:44](https://ref.ly/Jer50:44), [Zc 11:3](https://ref.ly/Zech11:3), and therefore dangerous [Je 12:5](https://ref.ly/Jer12:5) (Ew thinks of the *swelling* of its agitated waters); גְּאוֺן גַּלֶּיךָ *majesty of thy waves* [Jb 38:11](https://ref.ly/Job38:11). **2\.** *pride* (bad sense) [Jb 35:12](https://ref.ly/Job35:12), [Psalm 59:13](https://ref.ly/Ps59:13), [Pr 8:13](https://ref.ly/Prov8:13), [16:18](https://ref.ly/Prov16:18), Ez [7:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek7:20), [16:49](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:49), [Zp 2:10](https://ref.ly/Zeph2:10); of Moab [Is 16:6](https://ref.ly/Isa16:6), [16:6](https://ref.ly/Isa16:6) \= [Je 48:29](https://ref.ly/Jer48:29), [48:29](https://ref.ly/Jer48:29).

> \[Str1348] † גֵּאוּת **n.f.** majesty, [Psalm 93:1](https://ref.ly/Ps93:1) \+ 7 t.;—**1\.** *lifting up* גֵּאוּת עָשָׁן *column of smoke* [Is 9:17](https://ref.ly/Isa9:17); גֵּאוּת הַיָּם *swelling of the sea* [Psalm 89:10](https://ref.ly/Ps89:10). **2\.** *majesty* of God [Psalm 93:1](https://ref.ly/Ps93:1), [Is 26:10](https://ref.ly/Isa26:10); גֵּאוּת עָשָה *he hath done majestically* [Is 12:5](https://ref.ly/Isa12:5); עֲטֶרֶת גֵּאוּת *crown of majesty* [Is 28:1](https://ref.ly/Isa28:1), [28:3](https://ref.ly/Isa28:3) (Samaria, on a round hill majestically commanding the country). **3\.** *pride* דִּבְּרוּ בְגֵאוּת *they speak proudly* [Psalm 17:10](https://ref.ly/Ps17:10); so for נְאוֺת [74:20](https://ref.ly/Ps74:20) BI Che.

> \[Str1345] † גְּאוּאֵל **n.pr.m.** (*majesty of EĒl*) the spy of the tribe of Gad [Nu 13:15](https://ref.ly/Num13:15).

> \[Str1349] † \[גַּאֲיוֺן] **adj.** proud, גאייונים [Psalm 123:4](https://ref.ly/Ps123:4) (Kt cf. Baer's note, yet rd. prob. גַּאֲיוֺנִים; but Qr better, גְּאֵי יוֺנִים *proudest oppressors*, v. גֵּאֶה).

> גאייונים v. foregoing, and also גֵּאֶה.

> \[Str1466] גֵּוָה **n.f.** pride (contr. for גַּאֲוָה Ew§ 62 b, 73 b cf. Aram. [Dn 4:34](https://ref.ly/Dan4:34) \& Targum)—**1\.** *pride* [Jb 33:17](https://ref.ly/Job33:17), [Je 13:17](https://ref.ly/Jer13:17). **2\.** *lifting up* [Jb 22:29](https://ref.ly/Job22:29) an exclamation, *up!* Ew Di De al.; but *pride* Hi Dr§ 154 n. (ed. 3\).

> גֵּאָיוֺת v. גַּיְא.

## I גָּאַל (id: BDB01556)

\[Str1350] † I. גָּאַל **vb.** redeem, act as kinsman (NH, Niph. *be redeemed;* also גּוֺאֵל, גְּאוּלָּה)—**Qal** *Pf.* ´ג, etc. [Is 44:23](https://ref.ly/Isa44:23) \+ 14 t.; *Impf.* יִגְאַל, etc. [Lv 25:33](https://ref.ly/Lev25:33) \+ 18 t.; *Imv.* גְּאַל, etc. [Ru 4:6](https://ref.ly/Ruth4:6) \+ 3 t.; *Inf.* abs. גָּאֹל [Lv 27:13](https://ref.ly/Lev27:13) \+ 2 t.; cstr. לִגְאוֺל [Ru 4:4](https://ref.ly/Ruth4:4) \+ 2 t.; sf. לְגָאֳלֵח [Ru 3:13](https://ref.ly/Ruth3:13); *Pt.* גֹּאֵל [Lv 25:26](https://ref.ly/Lev25:26) \+ 24 t., גּוֺאֵל [Is 59:20](https://ref.ly/Isa59:20), [Psalm 103:4](https://ref.ly/Ps103:4); sf. גֹּאַלְךָ [Is 48:17](https://ref.ly/Isa48:17), גֹּאֲלֵח [Is 54:8](https://ref.ly/Isa54:8) (sf. 19 t.); pass. pl. גְּאוּלִים, etc. [Is 35:9](https://ref.ly/Isa35:9) \+ 4 t.;—**1\.** *act as kinsman, do the part of next of kin* (chiefly in D H P Ru), גֹּאֵל *kinsman* [Lv 25:25](https://ref.ly/Lev25:25) (H) [Nu 5:8](https://ref.ly/Num5:8), [35:12](https://ref.ly/Num35:12) (P) [Ru 2:20](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:20), [3:9](https://ref.ly/Ruth3:9), [3:12](https://ref.ly/Ruth3:12), [4:1](https://ref.ly/Ruth4:1), [4:3](https://ref.ly/Ruth4:3), [4:6](https://ref.ly/Ruth4:6), [4:8](https://ref.ly/Ruth4:8), [4:14](https://ref.ly/Ruth4:14), [1 K 16:11](https://ref.ly/1Kgs16:11). **a.** in taking a kinsman's widow אם יגאלך טוב יגאל ואם לא יחפץ לגאלך וגאלתיך *if he will do thee the kinsman's part* (raise up children by the widow) *well, let him do the kinsman's part; but if he is not pleased to do thee the kinsman's part then I will do thee the kinsman's part* [Ru 3:13](https://ref.ly/Ruth3:13); **b.** in redeeming from bondage [Lv 25:48](https://ref.ly/Lev25:48), [25:49](https://ref.ly/Lev25:49) (H); **c.** in redeeming a field [Lv 25:26](https://ref.ly/Lev25:26), [25:33](https://ref.ly/Lev25:33) (H) [Ru 4:4](https://ref.ly/Ruth4:4), [4:6](https://ref.ly/Ruth4:6); **d.** claim as kinsman [Jb 3:5](https://ref.ly/Job3:5); **e.** גֹּאֵל הַדָּם *the avenger of blood* [Nu 35:19](https://ref.ly/Num35:19), [35:21](https://ref.ly/Num35:21), [35:24](https://ref.ly/Num35:24), [35:25](https://ref.ly/Num35:25), [35:27](https://ref.ly/Num35:27), [35:27](https://ref.ly/Num35:27), [Jos 20:3](https://ref.ly/Josh20:3), [20:5](https://ref.ly/Josh20:5) (?; not in LXX) v:[9](https://ref.ly/Josh20:9) (P). [Dt 19:6](https://ref.ly/Deut19:6), [19:12](https://ref.ly/Deut19:12) (D) [2 S 14:11](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:11). **2\.** *redeem*, by payment of value assessed, of consecrated things, by the original owner [Lv 27:13](https://ref.ly/Lev27:13), [27:15](https://ref.ly/Lev27:15), [27:19](https://ref.ly/Lev27:19), [27:20](https://ref.ly/Lev27:20), [27:31](https://ref.ly/Lev27:31) (P). **3\.** *redeem*, with God as subj. implying personal relationship, chiefly in poetry:—**a.** individuals, from death [Psalm 103:4](https://ref.ly/Ps103:4), [La 3:58](https://ref.ly/Lam3:58), [Ho 13:14](https://ref.ly/Hos13:14), מכל רע [Gn 48:16](https://ref.ly/Gen48:16) (E poetry), נפשׁ [Psalm 69:19](https://ref.ly/Ps69:19), [72:14](https://ref.ly/Ps72:14), orphans [Pr 23:11](https://ref.ly/Prov23:11), [Je 50:34](https://ref.ly/Jer50:34), ריבה ריבי וגאלני [Psalm 119:154](https://ref.ly/Ps119:154), גֹּאֲלִי [Jb 19:25](https://ref.ly/Job19:25), צוּרִי וְגֹאֲלִי [Psalm 19:15](https://ref.ly/Ps19:15). **b.** Israel, from Egyptian bondage [Ex 6:6](https://ref.ly/Exod6:6) (P?) [15:13](https://ref.ly/Exod15:13) (song) [Psalm 74:2](https://ref.ly/Ps74:2), [77:16](https://ref.ly/Ps77:16), [78:35](https://ref.ly/Ps78:35), מיד אויב [Psalm 106:10](https://ref.ly/Ps106:10). **c.** from exile (chiefly Is:3, the vb. not in Is:1) [Is 43:1](https://ref.ly/Isa43:1), [44:22](https://ref.ly/Isa44:22), [44:23](https://ref.ly/Isa44:23), [48:20](https://ref.ly/Isa48:20), [52:9](https://ref.ly/Isa52:9), [63:9](https://ref.ly/Isa63:9), [Mi 4:10](https://ref.ly/Mic4:10), מיד [Psalm 107:2](https://ref.ly/Ps107:2), [Je 31:11](https://ref.ly/Jer31:11); Yahweh is גֹּאֵל [Is 41:14](https://ref.ly/Isa41:14), [43:14](https://ref.ly/Isa43:14), [44:6](https://ref.ly/Isa44:6), [44:24](https://ref.ly/Isa44:24), [47:4](https://ref.ly/Isa47:4), [48:17](https://ref.ly/Isa48:17), [49:7](https://ref.ly/Isa49:7), [49:26](https://ref.ly/Isa49:26), [54:5](https://ref.ly/Isa54:5), [54:8](https://ref.ly/Isa54:8), [59:20](https://ref.ly/Isa59:20), [60:16](https://ref.ly/Isa60:16), [63:16](https://ref.ly/Isa63:16); and the people גְּאוּלִים [Is 35:9](https://ref.ly/Isa35:9), [51:10](https://ref.ly/Isa51:10), [62:12](https://ref.ly/Isa62:12), [63:4](https://ref.ly/Isa63:4) (cf. גְּאוּלַי infr.), [Psalm 107:2](https://ref.ly/Ps107:2). **Niph.** *Pf.* נִגְאָל [Lv 25:49](https://ref.ly/Lev25:49); *Impf.* יִגָּאֵל [Lv 25:30](https://ref.ly/Lev25:30) \+ 5 t.; תִּגָּאֵָלוּ [Is 52:3](https://ref.ly/Isa52:3); —**1\.** refl. *redeem oneself* [Lv 25:49](https://ref.ly/Lev25:49) (H). **2\.** pass. *be redeemed*, **a.** field [Lv 25:30](https://ref.ly/Lev25:30) (H), slave [Lv 25:54](https://ref.ly/Lev25:54) (H); **b.** consecrated things [Lv 27:20](https://ref.ly/Lev27:20), [27:27](https://ref.ly/Lev27:27), [27:28](https://ref.ly/Lev27:28), [27:33](https://ref.ly/Lev27:33) (P); **c.** Jerusalem by Yahweh [Is 52:3](https://ref.ly/Isa52:3).

> † גְּאוּלַי [Is 63:4](https://ref.ly/Isa63:4), in ´שְׁנַת ג, **n.abstr.** *redemption*, acc. to LXX SyrVer Vulgate Ges Hi De MV Che Di RVm; then either pl. abstr. sf. *year of* (*my*) *redemption* (so most); or abstr. form. in י—, after Syr. analogy, LagSymm. ii. 101 f. Sem. i. 19, 68\. BN 192 (LXX om. *my*); but \< Pt. pass. pl. sf. *my ransomed* (*released*) *ones* Ew Br Brd AV RV cf. sub גאל supra.

> \[Str1353] † גְּאֻלָּה:14 **n.f.** kin (?), redemption—[Lv 25:24](https://ref.ly/Lev25:24) \+ 5 t.; cstr. גְּאֻלַּת [Lv 25:32](https://ref.ly/Lev25:32); sf. גְּאֻלָּתִי [Ru 4:6](https://ref.ly/Ruth4:6), גְּאֻלָּתֶָךָ Ez [11:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek11:15), גְּאֻלָּתוֺ [Lv 25:26](https://ref.ly/Lev25:26) \+ 4 t.;—**1\.** *kin*, אַנְשֵׁי גְּאֻלָּתֶָךָ *men of thy kindred* Ez [11:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek11:15), RV Thes Hi al.; but LXX SyrVer Ew Co גלותך ´א *thy fellow\-exiles*. **2\.** *redemption*, of field [Lv 25:24](https://ref.ly/Lev25:24) (H) [Ru 4:7](https://ref.ly/Ruth4:7). **3\.** *right of redemption* [Lv 25:29](https://ref.ly/Lev25:29), [25:31](https://ref.ly/Lev25:31), [25:32](https://ref.ly/Lev25:32), [25:48](https://ref.ly/Lev25:48) (H) [Ru 4:6](https://ref.ly/Ruth4:6), [Je 32:8](https://ref.ly/Jer32:8) \= משׁפט הגאלה [Je 32:7](https://ref.ly/Jer32:7). **4\.** *price of redemption* [Lv 25:26](https://ref.ly/Lev25:26), [25:51](https://ref.ly/Lev25:51), [25:52](https://ref.ly/Lev25:52) (H).

> \[Str3008] † יִגְאָל **n.pr.m.** (*He redeems*)—**1\.** one of the spies [Nu 13:7](https://ref.ly/Num13:7). **2\.** one of David's heroes [2 S 23:36](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:36). **3\.** descendant of Zerubbabel [1 Ch 3:22](https://ref.ly/1Chr3:22).

## II גָּאַל (id: BDB01560)

\[Str1351] † II. \[גָּאַל] **vb.** defile, late (cf. גָּעַל)—**Niph.** *Pf.* 3 mpl. נְגֹאֲלוּ [Is 59:3](https://ref.ly/Isa59:3), [La 4:14](https://ref.ly/Lam4:14), on form v. Ges§ 51, 2, Köi. 265; *Pt.* נִגְאָלָה [Zp 3:1](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:1); —*be defiled*, hands with blood בַּדָּם, [Is 59:3](https://ref.ly/Isa59:3) cf. [La 4:14](https://ref.ly/Lam4:14); pt. as subst. *defiled, polluted one* [Zp 3:1](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:1) of Jerusalem (‖מֹֽרְאָה; appos. הָעִיר הַיּוֺנָה). **Pi.** *Pf.* 1 pl. sf. גֵּאַלְנוּךָ [Mal 1:7](https://ref.ly/Mal1:7) *pollute, desecrate*, obj. ´י (desecrated in his altar). **Pu.** *Impf.* וַיְגֹאֲלוּ מִן־הַכְּהֻנָּה [Ezr 2:62](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:62) \= [Ne 7:64](https://ref.ly/Neh7:64) cstr. pregn. *and they were desecrated out of the priesthood*, i.e. deposed, as desecrated one; *Pt.* מְגֹאָל of bread laid on Yahweh's altar [Mal 1:7](https://ref.ly/Mal1:7); of Yahweh's altar (table, שֻׁלְחָן) v:[12](https://ref.ly/Mal1:12). **Hiph.** *Pf.* 1 s. אֶגְאָלְתִּי [Is 63:3](https://ref.ly/Isa63:3) (on Aram. form v. Köi. 27 Ges§ 53 R. 6 but) rd. perh. גֵּאָלְתִּי *Pi.,* v. GFMThLZ 1887, 292 cf. also Gesl.c. N. Ol:§ 255 b Sta§ 159 b, 3; —*I have polluted*, i.e. *stained all my raiment*. **Hithp.** *Impf.* יִתְגָּאַל [Dn 1:8](https://ref.ly/Dan1:8); יִתְגָּאָל v:[8](https://ref.ly/Dan1:8); —*defile himself*.

> \[Str1352] † \[גֹּ֫אֶל] **n.\[m.]** defiling, defilement, גָּֽאֳלֵי הַכְּהֻנָּה [Ne 13:29](https://ref.ly/Neh13:29).

> \[Str1354] גַּב back, etc., v. sub גבב below

> \[Str1461] \[גָּב], גָּבִים v. גוב.

> \[Str1357] I. \[גֵּב] *locust*, v. sub גבה. below

> \[Str1356] II. \[גֵּב] pit, III. \[גֵּב] *beam*, v. sub גוב. p. 155

> \[Str1359] גֹּב, גּוֺב **n.pr.loc.** v. sub גבב. below

## גבא (id: BDB01567)

גבא (cf. Ar. جَبَأَ, جَبِئَ *restrain* or *withhold oneself;* perh. جَبَا *collect* (water in a cistern, also tribute), so Lane جَبَى 1\. ad fin., Fl NHWBi. 431; v. also NH גבי, גָּבָה, Aram. גְּבָא, ܓܒܳܐ *collect* debts, taxes, etc.)

> \[Str1360] † גֶּ֫בֶא **n.m.**:Ez [47:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:11) cistern, pool (cf. Ar. جَابِيَةٌ *watering\-trough*)—**1\.** *cistern* מַיִם מִֶ\+גּבֶא [Is 30:14](https://ref.ly/Isa30:14). **2\.** *pool, marsh* גְּבָיָאו Ez [47:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:11) (‖כִּצּאֹתָ֯ו).

* **Associated Passages:** Ezekiel 47:11; Isaiah 30:14


## גבב (id: BDB01569)

גבב (prob. *be curved, convex, elevated*, Aram. גִּבְּבָא *hill; be* or *make hollow, dig*, Ar. جَبَّ *cut off* or *out*, جُبٌّ, Aram. גֹּב, Eth. ግብ፡ As. *gubbu*,—all \= *cistern*; cf. e.g. As. *gubbâni ša mê, cisterns of water*, AsrbAnnals Col. viii. 102, KBii. 220; v. n.pr. גֹּב infr.)

> \[Str1354] † גַּב **n.m.**:? cf. Ez [43:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek43:13) (**f.**:Ez [1:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:18)) anything convex, curved, gibbous, e.g. back, chiefly late;—abs. ָ\+גּב Ez [16:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:24); cstr. גַּב Ez [43:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek43:13) (rd. גֹּבַהּ LXX Ew Sm Co); sf. גַּבִּי [Psalm 129:3](https://ref.ly/Ps129:3), גַּבֵךְ Ez [16:31](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:31), [16:39](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:39); pl. cstr. גַּבֹּת [Lv 14:9](https://ref.ly/Lev14:9), גַּבֵּי [Jb 13:12](https://ref.ly/Job13:12), [15:26](https://ref.ly/Job15:26); sf. גַּבֵּיהֶם [1 K 7:33](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:33), גַּבֵּהֶם Ez [10:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek10:12), גַּבֵּיהֶן Ez [1:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:18), גַֹּבֹּתָם Ez [1:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:18); —**1\.** *back*, of man (fig. of Isr.) [Psalm 129:3](https://ref.ly/Ps129:3); appar. of cherubim Ez [10:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek10:12), but Hi Sm emend v. so that ´גּ ref. to wheels, *rim* v. **6** infr. **2\.** *mound*, for illicit worship Ez [16:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:24), [16:31](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:31), [16:39](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:39) (all ‖רמה); LXX Vulgate *lupanar, brothel*, after analogy of *fornix*, but this without sufficient proof, \& needless. **3\.** *boss*, or convex projection, of shield [Jb 15:26](https://ref.ly/Job15:26) (fig.) *he runneth against him... with the stout bosses of his shields* (i.e. wicked against ´י); so ظهر in Ar. in similar phrase; cf. also Ar. جَوْبٌ, *shield;* also French *bouclier* fr. *boucle*. **4\.** *bulwarks, breastworks*, fig. for arguments לְגַבֵּי־חֹמֶר גַּבֵּיכֶם [Jb 13:12](https://ref.ly/Job13:12) *breastworks of clay are your breastworks*. **5\.** *brow*, only גַּבֹּת עֵינָיו [Lv 14:9](https://ref.ly/Lev14:9) *his eyebrows*. **6\.** *rim* of wheel, *felloe* [1 K 7:33](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:33), Ez [1:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:18), [1:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:18); so perh. [10:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek10:12) v. **1** supr.—Ez [43:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek43:13); *elevation*, i.e. basement of altar, Da after MT., but v. גֹּבַהּ, cf. supr.

> \[Str1359] † גֹּב [2 S 21:18](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:18), גּוֺב v:[19](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:19) **n.pr.loc.** (cf. Aram. גֻּבָּא *den*, and ܓܘܽܒܳܐ, Ar. جُبٌّ, Eth. ግብ፡ As. *gubbu, well, cistern*, v. גבב)—field of battle with Philistines [2 S 21:18](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:18) \= גֶּזֶר in ‖ [1 Ch 20:4](https://ref.ly/1Chr20:4) (so here Th Ew; cf. [Jos 10:33](https://ref.ly/Josh10:33)), but \= גַּת LXX SyrVer [2 S 21:18](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:18) (LXXL Γαζεθ); [2 S 21:19](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:19) (om. ‖ [1 Ch 20:5](https://ref.ly/1Chr20:5)), LXX Ρομ, Ροβ; Klo *Gath;* in v:[16](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:16) We Dr (q. v.) rd. בְּגֹב for בְּנֹב; site of Gob (si vera l.) unknown.

> \[Str1373] † גַּבַּי סַלָּי appar. **n.pr.m.** (cf. Aram. ܓܰܒܳܝܳܐ, Talm. גַּבָּי, *tax\-gatherer*) a Benjamite [Ne 11:8](https://ref.ly/Neh11:8); but text dub. cf. [1 Ch 9:8](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:8) \& SmListen 7\.

> \[Str1405] † גִּבְּתוֺן **n.pr.loc.** (*mound, height*, cf. Targum גִּבְבָא *ridge*) Philistine city [1 K 15:27](https://ref.ly/1Kgs15:27), [15:27](https://ref.ly/1Kgs15:27), [16:15](https://ref.ly/1Kgs16:15), [16:17](https://ref.ly/1Kgs16:17); assigned to Dan [Jos 19:44](https://ref.ly/Josh19:44), and to Levites [21:23](https://ref.ly/Josh21:23): site unknown; cf. LagOnom. 246, 2nd ed. 255\.

## גבה (id: BDB01574)

גבה (*collect*, Ar. جَبَا (\=جَبَأَ?, cf. גבא supr.), NH גְּבי, גָּבָה, Aram. גְּבָא, ܓܒܳܐ; ‖ form גבא q.v.)

> \[Str1357] † I. \[גֵּב] **n.\[m.]** locust (name from *swarm, collection*, Eth. ጉብኣኤ፡ cf. also Eth. አንብጥኣ፡ *locust* √ \= Ar. نَبَطَ *scaturivit, manavit*), only pl. גֵּבִים [Is 33:4](https://ref.ly/Isa33:4) (in sim. of leaping).—גֵּב II, III, v. sub גוב.

> \[Str1462] † גּוֺב **n.\[m.]** locusts, [Na 3:17](https://ref.ly/Nah3:17); cf. גֹּבַי. below

> \[Str1462] † גֹּבַי, גּוֺבָי **n.m.**:[Am. 7:1](https://ref.ly/Amos7:1), cf. v. 2 **coll.** locusts (*swarm, multitude;* Aram. גּוּבָא, pl. גוּבָאֵי; on format. v. Ol:§ 216 d), גֹּבַי symbol of Yahweh's judgment on Isr. [Am 7:1](https://ref.ly/Amos7:1); in sim. of disappearance of Assyrian leaders at destruction of Nineveh גּוֺב גּוֺבָי [Na 3:17](https://ref.ly/Nah3:17) (*locust\-*) *swarm of locusts* (‖אַרְבֶּה); but del. גּוֺב as dittogr. We al.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 33:4; Nahum 3:17; Amos 7:1


## גָּבַהּ (id: BDB01578)

\[Str1361] † גָּבַהּ **vb.** be high, exalted (NH *id.* (Hiph.), Aram. גְּבַהּ, cf. Ar. جَبْهَةٌ *forehead*, جَبَةٌ *prominence of forehead;* compare perhaps also As. *gabâni, heights* (?) LotzTP 133\)—**Qal** *Pf.* ´ג [2 Ch 26:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr26:16) \+ 5 t.; גָּֽבְהָא֯ Ez [31:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek31:5) (א \= ה); גָּבַהְתָּ Ez [31:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek31:10), גָּבְֽהוּ [Jb 35:5](https://ref.ly/Job35:5) \+ 3 t.; *Impf.* יִגְבַּהּ [Pr 18:12](https://ref.ly/Prov18:12) \+ 5 t.; sf. יִגְבְּהוּ Ez [31:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek31:14), יִגְבָּהוּ [Jb 36:7](https://ref.ly/Job36:7), תִּגְבָּהוּ [Je 13:15](https://ref.ly/Jer13:15), תִּגְבְּהֶינָה Ez [16:50](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:50); *Inf.* כִּגְבֹהַּ [Psalm 103:11](https://ref.ly/Ps103:11), לְגָבְהָה [Zp 3:11](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:11); —**1\.** *be high, lofty, tall*, e.g. tree Ez [19:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek19:11), [31:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek31:5), [31:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek31:10), [31:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek31:14), heavens [Jb 35:5](https://ref.ly/Job35:5), [Is 55:9](https://ref.ly/Isa55:9), [Psalm 103:11](https://ref.ly/Ps103:11), man [1 S 10:23](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:23). **2\.** *be exalted*, of man in dignity and honour [Jb 36:7](https://ref.ly/Job36:7), of servant of Yahweh [Is 52:13](https://ref.ly/Isa52:13), God [Is 5:16](https://ref.ly/Isa5:16), God's ways [Is 55:9](https://ref.ly/Isa55:9). **3\.** *lofty* גָּבַהּ לִבּוֺ:—**a.** in a good sense, *encouraged* in the ways of Yahweh [2 Ch 17:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr17:6); **b.** elsewhere in a bad sense, *be haughty* [Psalm 131:1](https://ref.ly/Ps131:1), [Pr 18:12](https://ref.ly/Prov18:12), [2 Ch 26:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr26:16), [32:25](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:25), Ez [28:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek28:2), [28:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek28:5), [28:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek28:17), and so without לב [Is 3:16](https://ref.ly/Isa3:16), [Je 13:15](https://ref.ly/Jer13:15), Ez [16:50](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:50), [Zp 3:11](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:11). **Hiph.** *Pf.* הִגְבַּהְתִּי Ez [17:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:24); *Impf.* יַגְבִּיהַּ [Jb 39:27](https://ref.ly/Job39:27), [Je 49:16](https://ref.ly/Jer49:16), Ob:[4](https://ref.ly/Obad1:4); וַיַּגְבִּיהֶהָ [2 Ch 33:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr33:14); יַגְבִּיהוּ [Jb 5:7](https://ref.ly/Job5:7); *Inf.* הַגְבֵּהַּ [Is 7:11](https://ref.ly/Isa7:11), Ez [21:31](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:31); *Pt.* מַגְבִּיהַּ [Pr 17:19](https://ref.ly/Prov17:19), [Psalm 113:5](https://ref.ly/Ps113:5); —*make high, exalt,* e.g. trees Ez [17:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:24), wall [2 Ch 33:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr33:14), gate [Pr 17:19](https://ref.ly/Prov17:19), nest [Je 49:16](https://ref.ly/Jer49:16), Ob:[4](https://ref.ly/Obad1:4), dwelling [Psalm 103:5](https://ref.ly/Ps103:5), a request [Is 7:11](https://ref.ly/Isa7:11), the lowly Ez [21:31](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:31); יַגְבִּיהוּ עוּף *make their flight high, soar aloft* [Jb 5:7](https://ref.ly/Job5:7), without עוף [Jb 39:27](https://ref.ly/Job39:27).

> \[Str1364,1362] † גָּבֹהַּ **adj.** high, exalted—[1 S 9:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam9:2) \+ 15 t.; גָּבוֺהַּ [Psalm 138:6](https://ref.ly/Ps138:6); cstr. גְּבֹהַּ [1 S 16:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam16:7); גְּבַהּ (Ew§ 213 d) [Psalm 101:5](https://ref.ly/Ps101:5) \+ 3 t.; pl. גְּבֹהִים [Ec 5:7](https://ref.ly/Eccl5:7) \+ 5 t.; f. גְּבֹהָה [Dt 3:5](https://ref.ly/Deut3:5) \+ 6 t.; pl. גְּבֹהוֺת [Dn 8:3](https://ref.ly/Dan8:3) \+ 2 t.; גְּבֹהֹת [Dt 28:52](https://ref.ly/Deut28:52); —**1\.** *high, lofty, tall,* e.g. tree Ez [17:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:24), tower [Is 2:15](https://ref.ly/Isa2:15), [Zp 1:16](https://ref.ly/Zeph1:16), mountain [Gn 7:19](https://ref.ly/Gen7:19), [Psalm 104:18](https://ref.ly/Ps104:18), [Is 40:9](https://ref.ly/Isa40:9), [57:7](https://ref.ly/Isa57:7), Ez [17:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:22), [40:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek40:2); cf. phrases על כל גבעה גבהה *upon every high hill* [1 K 14:23](https://ref.ly/1Kgs14:23), [2 K 17:10](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:10), [Je 2:20](https://ref.ly/Jer2:20); על גבעות הגבהות [Je 17:2](https://ref.ly/Jer17:2); ## גבל (id: BDB01590)

גבל (acc. to Thes orig. *twist, wind*, whence not only גַּבְלֻת, מִגְלֽת, but also גְּבוּל (*cord* and then) *boundary*, as determined by measuring cord, or line, whence vb. denom. גָּבַל *bound, border*, q. v. infr.; NH גָּבַל, Aram. גְּבַל mean *mix, knead;* Ar. جَبَلَ, Syr. ܓܒܰܠ \= *create, fashion;* MV assume meaning *massive,* whence Ar. جَبَل *mountain* (cf. As. *gablu* HA48), and גְּבוּל as originally earth\-wall, etc., serving as boundary; this explains גַּבְלֻת etc. less well).

## גבן (id: BDB01599)

גבן (prob. *be curved, contracted, coagulated;* Syr. Pa. ܓܰܒܶܢ *coagulate;* Ar. جَبُنَ is *be timid,* perh. from *shrinking, cowering*).

> \[Str1384] † גִּבֵּן **adj. crook\-backed, hump\-backed** (cf. Aram. גְּבִין *id.*, NH גַּבָּן *highlander;* also NH גבין, Aram. גבינא, ܓܒܺܝܢܳܐ, all \= *brow* (*eye\-brow*, etc.); cf. Ar. جَبِينٌ *side of forehead*), [Lv 21:20](https://ref.ly/Lev21:20).

> \[Str1385] † גְּבִינָה **n.f. curd**, or cheese (NH *id.*, Ar. ግብነት፡ Eth. ጉብኖእ Aram. גּוּבְנָא, ግቤቶእ, cf.)—וְכַגְּבִינָה תַּקְפִּיאֵנִי [Jb 10:10](https://ref.ly/Job10:10) (‖חָלָב).

> \[Str1386] † \[גַּבִנֹן] **n.\[m.]** peak, rounded summit; pl. גַּבְנֻנִּים: [Psalm 68:16](https://ref.ly/Ps68:16) :הַר־בָּשָֽׁן ´הַר־אֱלֽהִים הַר־בָּשָׁן הַר־ג; ´הָרִים ג v:[17](https://ref.ly/Ps68:17) (appos., Thes Dr§ 188 al.; others adj. *many\-peaked*). Cf. WetzstBatan. Giebelgeb. 1884\.

* **Associated Passages:** Job 10:10; Psalms 68:17; Psalms 68:16; Leviticus 21:20


## גבע (id: BDB01603)

גבע (*convex, projecting, high;* cf. Aram. Pa. גַּבַּע *swell, swell up*, גְּבִיעַ *hump\-backed*, גִּבְעֲתָא *hill;* v. also I. גִּבְעָה infr.)

> \[Str1387] † גֶּ֫בַע **n.pr.loc.**—´ג abs. [Jos 21:17](https://ref.ly/Josh21:17) \+; cstr. Ju [20:10](https://ref.ly/Judg20:10) \+; ָ\+גּבַע [Jos 18:24](https://ref.ly/Josh18:24) \+;—Levitical city, in Benjamin [Jos 21:17](https://ref.ly/Josh21:17) \= [1 Ch 6:45](https://ref.ly/1Chr6:45), [Jos 18:24](https://ref.ly/Josh18:24) cf. [1 Ch 8:6](https://ref.ly/1Chr8:6), [1 K 15:22](https://ref.ly/1Kgs15:22), [Ne 11:31](https://ref.ly/Neh11:31); also [1 S 13:16](https://ref.ly/1Sam13:16) \& Ju [20:10](https://ref.ly/Judg20:10), [20:33](https://ref.ly/Judg20:33) MT, in all three rd. גִּבְעָה (גִּבְעַת), cf. context;—northernmost city in kingdom of Judah [2 K 23:8](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:8) *from Geba to Beersheba*, cf. [Zc 14:10](https://ref.ly/Zech14:10); situated S. of pass of *Michmash* [Is 10:29](https://ref.ly/Isa10:29), [1 S 14:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:5) cf. [1 S 13:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam13:3); mentioned also [2 Ch 16:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr16:6), [Ezr 2:26](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:26), [Ne 7:30](https://ref.ly/Neh7:30), [12:29](https://ref.ly/Neh12:29); in [1 S 13:18](https://ref.ly/1Sam13:18) LXX We Dr rd. הַגֶּבַע for MT גְּבוּל q.v.; ([2 S 5:25](https://ref.ly/2Sam5:25) rd. גבעון with LXX and [1 Ch 14:16](https://ref.ly/1Chr14:16)).—Mod. *Jeba‘* RobBR i. 440 f. BDPal 120\.

> \[Str1388] † גִּבְעָא **n.pr.m.** a son of Caleb [1 Ch 2:49](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:49).

> \[Str1389] † I. גִּבְעָה **n.f.** hill—abs. גִּבְעָה [2 S 2:25](https://ref.ly/2Sam2:25) \+ 13 t. \+ Ez [6:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek6:13) (del. Co q.v.) [1 S 7:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam7:1), [2 S 6:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam6:3), [6:4](https://ref.ly/2Sam6:4) (cf. Co), גִּבְעָתָהּ [Is 31:4](https://ref.ly/Isa31:4); pl. abs. גְּבָעוֺת [Dt 12:2](https://ref.ly/Deut12:2) \+ 35 t.; cstr. גִּבְעוֺת [Dt 33:15](https://ref.ly/Deut33:15), Hb [3:6](https://ref.ly/Hab3:6), גִּבְעֹת [Gn 49:26](https://ref.ly/Gen49:26); sf. גִּבְעוֺתֶיךָ Ez. ## גָּבַר (id: BDB01615)

\[Str1396] † גָּבַר **vb.** be strong, mighty (NH *id.* Aram. גְּבַר; Ar. جَبَرَ (conj. I. dial.; usually in derived conj.) *compel, force*; جَبَرِيَّةٌ *overbearing behaviour*, جَبْرٌ *constraint*; Eth. ገብረ፡: I, 1, *subigere*; II, 2, *cogere*; Syr. ܐܶܬܓܰܒܰܪ *play the man*, is denom. fr. ܓܒܰܪ man)—**Qal** *Pf.* ´ג [Psalm 103:11](https://ref.ly/Ps103:11) \+ 5 t.; גָּבְֽרוּ [Gn 7:19](https://ref.ly/Gen7:19) \+ 6 t.; גָּבֵָרוּ [2 S 1:23](https://ref.ly/2Sam1:23); *Impf.* יִגְבַּר [1 S 2:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:9); וַיִּגְבְּרוּ [Gn 7:18](https://ref.ly/Gen7:18), [7:24](https://ref.ly/Gen7:24); —**1\.** *be strong, mighty*, abs. גָּבְֽרוּ חַיִל *mighty in power* [Jb 21:7](https://ref.ly/Job21:7); with מִן *stronger than* [2 S 1:24](https://ref.ly/2Sam1:24), [Psalm 65:4](https://ref.ly/Ps65:4); with בְּ *mighty among* [1 Ch 5:2](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:2). **2\.** *prevail:*—**a** abs. e.g. enemies [Ex 17:11](https://ref.ly/Exod17:11), [17:11](https://ref.ly/Exod17:11) (E) [1 S 2:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:9), [La 1:16](https://ref.ly/Lam1:16), waters [Gn 7:18](https://ref.ly/Gen7:18), [7:19](https://ref.ly/Gen7:19), [7:20](https://ref.ly/Gen7:20), [7:24](https://ref.ly/Gen7:24) (P), power [Je 9:2](https://ref.ly/Jer9:2); **b.** with עַל *prevail over*, subj. enemies [2 S 11:23](https://ref.ly/2Sam11:23), blessings [Gn 49:26](https://ref.ly/Gen49:26)(J), mercy of God [Psalm 103:11](https://ref.ly/Ps103:11), [117:2](https://ref.ly/Ps117:2). **Pi.** *Pf.* גִּבַּרְתִּי [Zc 10:6](https://ref.ly/Zech10:6); sf. גִּבַּרְתִּים [Zc 10:12](https://ref.ly/Zech10:12); *Impf.* יְגַבֵּר [Ec 10:10](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:10) *make strong, strengthen.* **Hiph.** *Pf.* הִגְבִּיר בְּרִית לְ *confirm a covenant* [Dn 9:27](https://ref.ly/Dan9:27); *Impf.* נַגְבִּיר לִלְשֹׁנֵנוּ *we will confirm covenant with our tongue* Ew Ol Che (or, to our tongue will we give strength Hi De) [Psalm 12:5](https://ref.ly/Ps12:5). **Hithp.** *Impf.* יִתְגַּבָּר [Jb 15:25](https://ref.ly/Job15:25), [Is 42:13](https://ref.ly/Isa42:13); יִתְגַּבָּרוּ [Jb 36:9](https://ref.ly/Job36:9):—of ´י, *shew himself a mighty one* against (עַל) [Is 42:13](https://ref.ly/Isa42:13); of wicked, *behave proudly* toward (אֶל) [Jb 15:25](https://ref.ly/Job15:25); of erring righteous (aba.) [36:9](https://ref.ly/Job36:9).

> \[Str1399] גִּבַר v. following.

> \[Str1397,1399] I. גֶּ֫בֶר:66 **n.m.** man (NH *id*., MI16 גברן (pl.), Aram. גְּבַר, ܓܒܰܪ; As. *gabru, rival is* Akk. Loan\-word acc. to SchrJLZ 1874, 200 DlS 120, Sm. Chald. Gen. 286\)—[Dt 22:5](https://ref.ly/Deut22:5) \+ 39 t.; ָ\+גּבֶר [Jb 3:3](https://ref.ly/Job3:3) \+ 13 t.; cstr. גְּבַּר [Psalm 18:26](https://ref.ly/Ps18:26) (\= [2 S 22:26](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:26) גִּבּוֺר but LXX SyrVer rd. גֶּבֶר); pl. גְּבָרים [Je 41:16](https://ref.ly/Jer41:16) \+ 10 t.;—man as strong, disting. fr. women, children, and non\-combatants whom he is to defend, chiefly poetic [Ex 10:11](https://ref.ly/Exod10:11), [Nu 24:3](https://ref.ly/Num24:3), [24:15](https://ref.ly/Num24:15) (E) [Ex 12:37](https://ref.ly/Exod12:37), [Jos 7:14](https://ref.ly/Josh7:14), [7:17](https://ref.ly/Josh7:17), [7:18](https://ref.ly/Josh7:18) (J) [Dt 22:5](https://ref.ly/Deut22:5), [22:5](https://ref.ly/Deut22:5), Ju [5:30](https://ref.ly/Judg5:30), [2 S 23:1](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:1), [1 Ch 23:3](https://ref.ly/1Chr23:3), [24:4](https://ref.ly/1Chr24:4), [26:12](https://ref.ly/1Chr26:12), [Jb 3:3](https://ref.ly/Job3:3) \+ 14 t. Jb; [Psalm 18:26](https://ref.ly/Ps18:26) \+ 8 t. Psalm; [Pr 6:34](https://ref.ly/Prov6:34) \+ 7 t. Pr; [Is 22:17](https://ref.ly/Isa22:17), [Je 17:5](https://ref.ly/Jer17:5) \+ 8 t. Je; [La 3:1](https://ref.ly/Lam3:1), [3:27](https://ref.ly/Lam3:27), [3:35](https://ref.ly/Lam3:35), [3:39](https://ref.ly/Lam3:39), [Dn 8:15](https://ref.ly/Dan8:15), [Mi 2:2](https://ref.ly/Mic2:2), Hb [2:5](https://ref.ly/Hab2:5), [Zc 13:7](https://ref.ly/Zech13:7); also [1 S 10:21](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:21) LXX We Dr; \= *each* (of locusts) [Jo 2:8](https://ref.ly/Joel2:8), cf. אישׁ.

> \[Str1398] II. גֶּ֫בֶר **n.pr.m.** an official of Solomon [1 K 4:19](https://ref.ly/1Kgs4:19) (cf. בֶּן־גֶּבֶר v:[13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs4:13), p. 122 supr.)

> \[Str1402] † גִּבָּר **n.pr.** (Aram. *id.* \= *hero*) [Ezr 2:20](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:20) prob. \= גִּבְעוֺן [Ne 7:25](https://ref.ly/Neh7:25).

> \[Str1368] גִּבּוֺר:159 **adj.** strong, mighty (cf. Ar. جَبَّارٌ *one who magnifies himself, behaves proudly, a tyrant, who is bold, audacious*)—[Gn 10:9](https://ref.ly/Gen10:9) \+ 5 t.; גִּבֹּר [Gn 10:8](https://ref.ly/Gen10:8) \+ 2 t.; גִּבּוֺרָם [1 S 17:51](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:51); pl. גִּבּוֺרִים [Je 46:9](https://ref.ly/Jer46:9) \+ 27 t.; גִּבֹּרִים [Jos 10:2](https://ref.ly/Josh10:2) \+ 21 t.; cstr. גִּבּוֺרֵי [1 Ch 11:27](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:27) \+ 29 t.; גִּבֹּרֵי [1 Ch 9:26](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:26) \+ 4 t.; sf. גִּבּוֺרֶיךָ [Ho 10:13](https://ref.ly/Hos10:13) \+ (var. sfs. 11 t.);—**1\. adj.** גִּבּוֺר בַּבְּהֵמָה *mightiest among beasts* [Pr 30:30](https://ref.ly/Prov30:30); אִישׁ גִּבּוֺר [1 S 14:52](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:52); גִּבּוֺר בָּאָרֶץ [Psalm 112:2](https://ref.ly/Ps112:2); גִּבּוֺר צַיִד *mighty in hunting* [Gn 10:9](https://ref.ly/Gen10:9) (J); מלך גבור [Dn 11:3](https://ref.ly/Dan11:3); אֵל גִּבּוֺר the Messiah [Is 9:5](https://ref.ly/Isa9:5); attribute of God especially as fighting for his people [Psalm 24:8](https://ref.ly/Ps24:8), [24:8](https://ref.ly/Ps24:8), [Dt 10:17](https://ref.ly/Deut10:17), [Ne 9:32](https://ref.ly/Neh9:32), [Is 10:21](https://ref.ly/Isa10:21), [Je 32:18](https://ref.ly/Jer32:18) (cf. > \[Str1369] † גְּבוּרָה: 61 **n.f.** strength, might—[Ex 32:18](https://ref.ly/Exod32:18) \+ 16 t.; cstr. גְּבוּרַת [Psalm 147:10](https://ref.ly/Ps147:10); sf. גְּבוּרָתוֺ \+ (sfs. 32 t.); pl. גְּבוּרוֺת [Jb 41:4](https://ref.ly/Job41:4) \+ 3 t.; גְּבֻרוֺת Psalm [71:16](https://ref.ly/Ps71:16); גְּבוּרֹתֶיךָ [Dt 3:24](https://ref.ly/Deut3:24) \+ (sfs. 6 t.);—**1\.** *strength*, of horse [Jb 39:19](https://ref.ly/Job39:19), [Psalm 147:10](https://ref.ly/Ps147:10), crocodile [Jb 41:4](https://ref.ly/Job41:4), sun Ju [5:31](https://ref.ly/Judg5:31), body of man [Psalm 90:10](https://ref.ly/Ps90:10), [Ec 9:16](https://ref.ly/Eccl9:16), [10:17](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:17). **2\.** *might, valour*, of warriors Ju [8:21](https://ref.ly/Judg8:21), [Pr 8:14](https://ref.ly/Prov8:14), [Is 3:25](https://ref.ly/Isa3:25), [28:6](https://ref.ly/Isa28:6), [30:15](https://ref.ly/Isa30:15), [Je 9:22](https://ref.ly/Jer9:22), [23:10](https://ref.ly/Jer23:10), [49:35](https://ref.ly/Jer49:35), [51:30](https://ref.ly/Jer51:30), [Ex 32:29](https://ref.ly/Exod32:29), [32:30](https://ref.ly/Exod32:30), [Mi 3:8](https://ref.ly/Mic3:8), [7:16](https://ref.ly/Mic7:16); קוֺל עֲנוֺת גְּבוּרָה, *noise of shouting in warlike strength* [Ex 32:18](https://ref.ly/Exod32:18) (E); עֵצָה וּגְבוּרָה [2 K 18:20](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:20), [Is 11:2](https://ref.ly/Isa11:2), [36:5](https://ref.ly/Isa36:5); cf. phrases of compiler of Kings כֹּל גְּבוּרָתוֺ [1 K 15:23](https://ref.ly/1Kgs15:23), [2 K 10:34](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:34), [20:20](https://ref.ly/2Kgs20:20); אֲשֶׁר עָשָה וּגְבוּרָתוֺ [1 K 6:5](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:5), [6:27](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:27), [22:46](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:46), [2 K 13:8](https://ref.ly/2Kgs13:8), [13:12](https://ref.ly/2Kgs13:12), [14:15](https://ref.ly/2Kgs14:15), [14:28](https://ref.ly/2Kgs14:28); ## גבשׁ (id: BDB01626)

גבשׁ (*be firm, massive*, cf. As. *gâbašu, be thick massive*, ZimBP 76, \& deriv.; cf. also Aram. גַּבֵּשׁ; Pa. *heap up* \& גַּבְשׁוּשִׁית *height, hill*).

> \[Str1378] † גָּבִישׁ **n.m.** crystal (cf. אֶלְגָּבִישׁ *hail*—on relation of meanings cf. Gk. κρύσταλλος, \& Eth. usage of እብነ በረድ፡ DILex. Aeth.759—Ar. جِبْسٌ *gypsum*, As; *gibsûu, mass, abundance*, COT: Gloss)—רָא֯מוֺת וְגָבִישׁ [Jb 28:18](https://ref.ly/Job28:18) *coral and crystal*.

> \[Str4019] † מַגְבִּישׁ **n.pr.m.** [Ezr 2:30](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:30) ´בְּנֵי מ LXX Μαγεβως, etc., a family of returning exiles, om. ‖ [Ne 7:33](https://ref.ly/Neh7:33), but LXX A א Μαγεβως, etc., LXXL Μαγεβεις; cf. Sm: Listen 15\.

## גָּדַד (id: BDB01632)

\[Str1413] † \[גָּדַד] **vb. penetrate**, cut (NH *id., cut, cut out,* Aram. גְּדַד, ܓܰܕ, Ar. جَدّ *cut, cut off;* Eth. ገድድ፡ in deriv.)—**Qal** *Impf.* [Psalm 94:21](https://ref.ly/Ps94:21) יָג֯וֺדּוּ עַל־נֶפֶשׁ צַדִּיִק *they attack* (penetrate, make inroads upon) *the life of a righteous man* (Ol prop. יָגוּרוּ cf. [56:7](https://ref.ly/Ps56:7), [59:4](https://ref.ly/Ps59:4) where, however, Che יגודו; si vera l., perh. denom. fr. גְּדוּד cf. [Ho 6:9](https://ref.ly/Hos6:9) \& infr.; cf. also גוד \& Köi. 356\). **Hithpo.** *Impf.* יִתְגֹּדַד [Je 16:6](https://ref.ly/Jer16:6); 2 fs. תִּתְגֹּדֲדִי [Mi 4:14](https://ref.ly/Mic4:14), תִּתְגּוֺדָדִי [Je 47:5](https://ref.ly/Jer47:5); pl. וַיִּתְגֹּדְדוּ [1 K 18:28](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:28), יִתְגּוֺדָדוּ [Je 5:7](https://ref.ly/Jer5:7) \+ [Ho 7:11](https://ref.ly/Hos7:11) v. infr., תִּתְגֹּדְדוּ [Dt 14:1](https://ref.ly/Deut14:1); *Pt.* pl. מִתְגֹּדְדִים [Je 41:5](https://ref.ly/Jer41:5); —**1\.** *cut oneself*, as religious (heathen) practice [1 K 18:28](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:28); practised also by men of Schechem, etc. in worship of ´י (late) [Je 41:5](https://ref.ly/Jer41:5); for the dead, forbidden [Dt 14:1](https://ref.ly/Deut14:1) לאֹ תִתְגֹּדְדוּ וְלאֹ תָשִימוּ קָרְחָה בֵּין עֵינֵיכֶם לָמֵת, [Je 16:6](https://ref.ly/Jer16:6); cf. [47:5](https://ref.ly/Jer47:5) (subject Philistia personified); also for MT יִתְגּוֺרָר֭וּ [Ho 7:14](https://ref.ly/Hos7:14) Codd, *they cut themselves*, LXX Gr Che RVm, or perh. (cf. גּוּר) sub. **2\.** *gather in troops,* or *bands; go in troops* or *throngs, throng;* (denominative fr. גְּדוּד q.v.) cf. תִּתְגֹּדֲדִי [Mi 4:14](https://ref.ly/Mic4:14) (addressed to Jerusalem); [Je 5:7](https://ref.ly/Jer5:7) ´וּבֵית זוֺנָה יִתְגּ *and to a harlot's house they throng.*

> \[Str1416] I. גְּדוּד **n. m.**:[Gn 49:19](https://ref.ly/Gen49:19) band, troop (as *making inroads;* others, as a *division, detachment* (as severed), but this usually later in Heb.)—´נ abs. [1 S 30:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:8) \+ 19 t.; cstr. [2 Ch 25:9](https://ref.ly/2Chr25:9); pl. ם [2 S 4:2](https://ref.ly/2Sam4:2) \+ 2 t.; cstr. גְּדוּדֵי [2 K 6:23](https://ref.ly/2Kgs6:23) \+ 6 t.; sf. גְּדוּדָיו [Jb 19:12](https://ref.ly/Job19:12), [25:3](https://ref.ly/Job25:3); —**1\.** *marauding band* (making *incursions, inroads,* cf. גדד) [1 S 30:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:8), [30:15](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:15), [30:15](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:15), [30:23](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:23) cf. [1 Ch 12:22](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:22), also [2 Ch 22:1](https://ref.ly/2Chr22:1), [1 K 11:24](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:24), [2 K 5:2](https://ref.ly/2Kgs5:2), [6:23](https://ref.ly/2Kgs6:23), [13:20](https://ref.ly/2Kgs13:20), [13:21](https://ref.ly/2Kgs13:21), [24:2](https://ref.ly/2Kgs24:2), [24:2](https://ref.ly/2Kgs24:2), [24:2](https://ref.ly/2Kgs24:2), [24:2](https://ref.ly/2Kgs24:2) cf. [2 S 22:30](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:30) \= [Psalm 18:30](https://ref.ly/Ps18:30), \& [Gn 49:19](https://ref.ly/Gen49:19) (v. גַּד [30:11](https://ref.ly/Gen30:11)) [Ho 6:9](https://ref.ly/Hos6:9), [7:1](https://ref.ly/Hos7:1), [Je 18:22](https://ref.ly/Jer18:22); שָרֵי גִּדוּדִים [2 S 4:2](https://ref.ly/2Sam4:2), cf. וַיִּתְּנֵם בְּרָאשֵׁי הַגְּדוּד [1 Ch 12:19](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:19); fig. of God's attacking forces, his chastisements [Jb 19:12](https://ref.ly/Job19:12) (cf. [25:3](https://ref.ly/Job25:3) infr.) **2\.** *troop*, of divisions of army of Isr. (late) גְּדוּדֵי צְבָא מִלְחָמָה [1 Ch 7:4](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:4), cf. [2 Ch 26:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr26:11); *band of Israel,* i.e. troop of mercenaries hired from Isr. by Amaziah [2 Ch 25:9](https://ref.ly/2Chr25:9), [25:10](https://ref.ly/2Chr25:10); בְּנֵי הַגְּדוּד i.e. *soldiers of the band* [2 Ch 25:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr25:13); of army in general כְּמֶלֶח בַּגְּדוּד [Jb 29:25](https://ref.ly/Job29:25), in sim.; also [Mi 4:14](https://ref.ly/Mic4:14) בַּת־גְּדוּד of Zion. **3\.** *foray, raid* [2 S 3:22](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:22).

> \[Str1417–18] † II. \[גְּדוּד, or גְּדוּדָה] **n. \[m. or f.]** furrow, cutting—**1\.** *furrow*, pl. defect. גְּדוּדֶָהָ [Psalm 65:11](https://ref.ly/Ps65:11) (‖תְּלָמֶיהָ). **2\.** pl. גְּדֻדֹת, *cuttings* upon hands, cf. גדד Hithpo. **2\.** sign of mourning [Je 48:37](https://ref.ly/Jer48:37) (‖שק).

sf. גְּדֵלַנִי [Jb 31:18](https://ref.ly/Job31:18); *Impf.* יִגְדַּל etc. [Psalm 35:27](https://ref.ly/Ps35:27) \+ 34 t.;—**1\.** *grow up,* **a.** child [Gn 21:8](https://ref.ly/Gen21:8), [21:20](https://ref.ly/Gen21:20), [25:27](https://ref.ly/Gen25:27), [38:11](https://ref.ly/Gen38:11), [38:14](https://ref.ly/Gen38:14) [Ex 2:10](https://ref.ly/Exod2:10), [2:11](https://ref.ly/Exod2:11) (JE) Ju [11:2](https://ref.ly/Judg11:2), [13:24](https://ref.ly/Judg13:24), [Ru 1:12](https://ref.ly/Ruth1:12), [1 S 2:21](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:21), [3:19](https://ref.ly/1Sam3:19), [1 K 12:8](https://ref.ly/1Kgs12:8), [12:10](https://ref.ly/1Kgs12:10) ( \= [2 Ch 10:8](https://ref.ly/2Chr10:8), [10:10](https://ref.ly/2Chr10:10)) [2 K 4:18](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:18); גְּדֵלַנִי כְאָב *he grew up to me as to a father* [Jb 31:18](https://ref.ly/Job31:18); **b.** lamb [2 S 12:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:3). **2\.** *become great*, **a.** in extent, wealthy [Gn 26:13](https://ref.ly/Gen26:13), [26:13](https://ref.ly/Gen26:13), [41:40](https://ref.ly/Gen41:40) (JE) [Je 5:27](https://ref.ly/Jer5:27); **b.** in value. ´בְּעֵינֵי י, גָּֽדְלָה נֶפֶשׁ בְּעֵיָ\+ני *prized by* [1 S 26:24](https://ref.ly/1Sam26:24), [26:24](https://ref.ly/1Sam26:24); **c.** in intensity, grief [Jb 2:13](https://ref.ly/Job2:13), mourning [Zc 12:11](https://ref.ly/Zech12:11), punishment [La 4:6](https://ref.ly/Lam4:6), trespass Ez [9:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek9:6); **d.** in sound, loud cry [Gn 19:13](https://ref.ly/Gen19:13) (J); **e.** in importance, of a king [Ec 2:9](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:9), [1 K 10:23](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:23) ( \= [2 Ch 9:22](https://ref.ly/2Chr9:22)) [Dn 8:9](https://ref.ly/Dan8:9), [8:10](https://ref.ly/Dan8:10) (under fig. of horn), chief [Gn 24:35](https://ref.ly/Gen24:35), [48:19](https://ref.ly/Gen48:19) (J), Messiah [Mi 5:3](https://ref.ly/Mic5:3), Jerusalem Ez [16:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:7); **b.** Yahweh [Psalm 35:27](https://ref.ly/Ps35:27), [40:17](https://ref.ly/Ps40:17), [70:5](https://ref.ly/Ps70:5), [Mal 1:5](https://ref.ly/Mal1:5), his name [2 S 7:26](https://ref.ly/2Sam7:26) ( \= [1 Ch 17:24](https://ref.ly/1Chr17:24)). **Pi.** *Pf.* גִּדַּל [Jos 4:14](https://ref.ly/Josh4:14), [Est 3:1](https://ref.ly/Esth3:1); גִּדֵּ֔ל [Is 49:21](https://ref.ly/Isa49:21); גִּדֵָּלָה [Is 51:18](https://ref.ly/Isa51:18), etc.; *Impf.* יְגַטֵּל [Is 44:14](https://ref.ly/Isa44:14) \+ 8 t.; *Imv.* גַּדְּלוּ [Psalm 34:4](https://ref.ly/Ps34:4); *Inf.* גַּדֵּל [Nu 6:5](https://ref.ly/Num6:5) \+ 3 t.; *Pt.* מְגַדְּלִים [2 K 10:6](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:6) (מְגַדְּלוֺת Ct [5:15](https://ref.ly/Song5:15) LXX Targum Hi Bö De);—**1\.** *cause to grow,* e.g. hair [Nu 6:5](https://ref.ly/Num6:5) (P), plants [Jon 4:10](https://ref.ly/Jonah4:10), [Is 44:14](https://ref.ly/Isa44:14), Ez [31:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek31:4), Ct [5:13](https://ref.ly/Song5:13); *bring up children* [2 K 10:6](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:6), [Is 1:2](https://ref.ly/Isa1:2), [23:4](https://ref.ly/Isa23:4), [49:21](https://ref.ly/Isa49:21), [51:18](https://ref.ly/Isa51:18), [Dn 1:5](https://ref.ly/Dan1:5), [Ho 9:12](https://ref.ly/Hos9:12). **2\.** *make great, powerful* [Gn 12:2](https://ref.ly/Gen12:2) (J) [Jos 3:7](https://ref.ly/Josh3:7), [4:14](https://ref.ly/Josh4:14) (D) [1 K 1:37](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:37), [1:47](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:47), [1 Ch 29:12](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:12), [29:25](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:25), [2 Ch 1:1](https://ref.ly/2Chr1:1), [Est 3:1](https://ref.ly/Esth3:1), [5:11](https://ref.ly/Esth5:11), [10:2](https://ref.ly/Esth10:2). **3\.** *magnify,* **a.** man [Jb 7:17](https://ref.ly/Job7:17); *Impf.* יַגְדִּיל [Is 42:21](https://ref.ly/Isa42:21), [Dn 8:25](https://ref.ly/Dan8:25), etc.; *Inf.* הַגְדִּיל [1 Ch 22:8](https://ref.ly/1Chr22:8), [Am 8:5](https://ref.ly/Amos8:5); *Pt.* מַגְדִּיל [Psalm 18:51](https://ref.ly/Ps18:51) ( \= מִגְדֹּיל in ‖ [2 S 22:51](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:51)); pl. מַגְדִּילִים [Psalm 35:26](https://ref.ly/Ps35:26); —**1\.** *make great*, e.g. shekel [Am 8:5](https://ref.ly/Amos8:5), pile for fire Ez [24:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:9), joy [Is 9:2](https://ref.ly/Isa9:2), counsel [Is 28:29](https://ref.ly/Isa28:29), wisdom [Ec 1:16](https://ref.ly/Eccl1:16), works [Ec 2:4](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:4), house of Yahweh [1 Ch 22:5](https://ref.ly/1Chr22:5); the heel [Psalm 41:10](https://ref.ly/Ps41:10) either *lifted high* (Ges), or (cf. De Now) *gave me insidiously a great fall;* cf. פִּיךָ ´ג Ob:[12](https://ref.ly/Obad1:12) i.e. utter proud words (v. רחב Hiph.) **2\.** *magnify,* salvation [Psalm 18:51](https://ref.ly/Ps18:51), mercy [Gn 19:19](https://ref.ly/Gen19:19) (J), teaching [Is 42:21](https://ref.ly/Isa42:21), the word of Yahweh [Psalm 138:2](https://ref.ly/Ps138:2). **3\.** *do great things* הִגְדִּיל לַעֲשוֺת, **a.** in a good sense, of God [Psalm 126:2](https://ref.ly/Ps126:2), [126:3](https://ref.ly/Ps126:3), [Jo 2:21](https://ref.ly/Joel2:21), also pregn. without Inf. [La 1:9](https://ref.ly/Lam1:9), [Zp 2:8](https://ref.ly/Zeph2:8), [2:10](https://ref.ly/Zeph2:10), [Dn 8:4](https://ref.ly/Dan8:4), [8:8](https://ref.ly/Dan8:8), [8:11](https://ref.ly/Dan8:11), [8:25](https://ref.ly/Dan8:25); with עַל, of enemies [Psalm 35:26](https://ref.ly/Ps35:26), [38:17](https://ref.ly/Ps38:17), [55:13](https://ref.ly/Ps55:13), [Jb 19:5](https://ref.ly/Job19:5), [Je 48:26](https://ref.ly/Jer48:26), [48:42](https://ref.ly/Jer48:42); Ez [35:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek35:13) of speaking עָלַי בְּפִיכֶם ´ג. **c.** also, with Inf. implied, wept greatly [1 S 20:41](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:41). **Hithp.** *Pf.* וְהִתְגַּדִּלְתִּ֫י Ez [38:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek38:23) *I will magnify myself*, shew myself great and powerful (of God); *Impf.* יִתְגַּדֵּל, with עַלin a bad sense, *magnify oneself against* [Is 10:15](https://ref.ly/Isa10:15), [Dn 11:36](https://ref.ly/Dan11:36); יִתְגַּדָּל [Dn 11:37](https://ref.ly/Dan11:37).

> \[Str1435] † גִּדֵּל **n.pr.m.** (*very great*)—**1\.** head of one of the families of Nethinim [Ezr 2:47](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:47), [Ne 7:49](https://ref.ly/Neh7:49). **2\.** head of one of the families of Solomon's servants [Ezr 2:56](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:56), [Ne 7:58](https://ref.ly/Neh7:58).

> \[Str1436] † גְּדַלְיָה **n.pr.m.** (*Yah is great*)—**1\.** governor of Judea appointed by Nebuchadnezzar [Je 40:5](https://ref.ly/Jer40:5), [40:8](https://ref.ly/Jer40:8), [41:16](https://ref.ly/Jer41:16). **2\.** son of Amariah, a son of Hezekiah [Zp 1:1](https://ref.ly/Zeph1:1). **3\.** priest of the sons of Jeshua [Ezr 10:18](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:18).

> \[Str1436] גְּדַלְיָ֫הוּ **n.pr.m.** (*Yah(u) is great*)—**1\.** governor of Judea \= גדליה[2 K 25:22–25](https://ref.ly/2Kgs25:22-2Kgs25:25), [Je 39:14](https://ref.ly/Jer39:14), [40:6–16](https://ref.ly/Jer40:6-Jer40:16), [41:1–18](https://ref.ly/Jer41:1-Jer41:18), [43:6](https://ref.ly/Jer43:6) (24 t.) **2\.** son of Pashur, one of the chiefs of Jerusalem in the time of Jeremiah [Je 38:1](https://ref.ly/Jer38:1). **3\.** one of the sons of Jeduthun, in the time of David [1 Ch 25:3](https://ref.ly/1Chr25:3), [25:9](https://ref.ly/1Chr25:9).

> \[Str1437] † גִּדַּ֫לְתִּי **n.pr.m.** (*I magnify (God)*) son of Heman [1 Ch 25:4](https://ref.ly/1Chr25:4) (cf. on this remarkable list of names Ew§ 274 b We RSOTJC 422; 2nd ed. 143\) v:[29](https://ref.ly/1Chr25:29).

> \[Str3012] † יִגְדַּלְיָ֫הוּ **n.pr.m.** (*Yah(u) is great*) a prophet of the age of Josiah [Je 35:4](https://ref.ly/Jer35:4).

> \[Str4024] † מִגְדּוֺל **n.m.** tower, [2 S 22:51](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:51) Qr (Kt מגדיל \= [Psalm 18:51](https://ref.ly/Ps18:51) מַגְדִּל).

> \[Str4024] † מִגְדֹּל **n.pr.** (מִגְדּוֺל only [Je 46:14](https://ref.ly/Jer46:14)) fortified city on the NE. border of Egypt [Ex 14:2](https://ref.ly/Exod14:2), [Nu 33:7](https://ref.ly/Num33:7), [Je 44:1](https://ref.ly/Jer44:1), [46:14](https://ref.ly/Jer46:14), Ez [29:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek29:10), [30:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:6); Copt. *meschtôl* (Champoll.:L’Égypte sous les Pharaons ii. 79\), Egyptian *makæel* EbGS 522\.

## גָּדַע (id: BDB01669)

\[Str1438] † גָּדַע **vb. hew, hew down or off** (NH *id.* (rare), Aram. גְּדַע Ithp.; Ar. جَدَعَ *cut off* hand or other member, *mutilate*)—**Qal** *Pf.* גָּדַע [La 2:3](https://ref.ly/Lam2:3); וְגָדַעְתִּ֫י [1 S 2:31](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:31); *Impf.* וָאָגְדַּע [Zc 11:10](https://ref.ly/Zech11:10), [11:14](https://ref.ly/Zech11:14); *Pt.* pass. גְּדוּעָה [Is 15:2](https://ref.ly/Isa15:2) (so many edd. but) Baer גְּרוּעָה; cf. infr., גְּדֻעִים [Is 10:33](https://ref.ly/Isa10:33); —*hew, cut in two,* a staff [Zc 11:10](https://ref.ly/Zech11:10), [11:14](https://ref.ly/Zech11:14); metaph. *hew off*, an arm [1 S 2:31](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:31); horns [La 2:3](https://ref.ly/Lam2:3); *hew down,* trees [Is 10:33](https://ref.ly/Isa10:33); if in [Is 15:2](https://ref.ly/Isa15:2) then \= *shave off* (object זקן, beard), but no other indication of this meaning, and true MT גְּרוּעָה, cf. Baer's note \& [Je 48:37](https://ref.ly/Jer48:37); v. גרע. **Niph.** *Pf.* נִגְדַּע Ju [21:6](https://ref.ly/Judg21:6) נֶגְדַּע [Je 50:23](https://ref.ly/Jer50:23); 3 fs. נִגְדְּעָה [Je 48:25](https://ref.ly/Jer48:25); וְנִגְדְּעָה consec. [Is 22:25](https://ref.ly/Isa22:25); 2 ms. נִגְדַּעְתָּ [Is 14:12](https://ref.ly/Isa14:12); 3 pl. וְנִגְדְּעוּ consec. Ez [6:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek6:6), [Am 3:14](https://ref.ly/Amos3:14); —*be hewn off*, of altar\-horns [Am 3:14](https://ref.ly/Amos3:14), of idols Ez [6:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek6:6) (‖נשׁבר); of severance of a tribe from nation Ju [21:6](https://ref.ly/Judg21:6); fig. of king of Babylon [Is 14:12](https://ref.ly/Isa14:12); of Babylon as hammer [Je 50:23](https://ref.ly/Jer50:23) (‖נשׁבר); of a minister, under fig. of secure peg or pin [Is 22:25](https://ref.ly/Isa22:25); of horn of Moab [Je 48:25](https://ref.ly/Jer48:25) (‖נשׁבר). **Pi.** *Pf.* גִּדַּע [2 Ch 34:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:7), גִּדֵָּעַ [2 Ch 34:4](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:4), [Psalm 107:16](https://ref.ly/Ps107:16), וַיְגַדַּע [2 Ch 14:2](https://ref.ly/2Chr14:2), אֲגַדֵָּעַ [Psalm 7:5](https://ref.ly/Ps7:5), [Is 45:2](https://ref.ly/Isa45:2), וַיְגַדְּעוּ [2 Ch 31:1](https://ref.ly/2Chr31:1); 3 mpl. תְּגַדֵּעָוּן [Dt 7:5](https://ref.ly/Deut7:5), [12:3](https://ref.ly/Deut12:3); —*hew off, down, in two* (cf. Qal) of *Asherîm* [Dt 7:5](https://ref.ly/Deut7:5), [2 Ch 14:2](https://ref.ly/2Chr14:2), [31:1](https://ref.ly/2Chr31:1), of idols [Dt 12:3](https://ref.ly/Deut12:3) (פְּסִילִים) [2 Ch 34:4](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:4), [34:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:7) (חַמָּנִים); fig. horns of wicked [Psalm 75:11](https://ref.ly/Ps75:11); bars of iron (i.e. of Babylon's gates) [Is 45:2](https://ref.ly/Isa45:2), cf. [Psalm 107:16](https://ref.ly/Ps107:16). †**Pu.** *Pf.* גֻּדָּעוּ [Is 9:9](https://ref.ly/Isa9:9) *hew down* (of trees).

> \[Str1439] גִּדִעוֺן **n.pr.m.** judge of Israel Ju [6:11](https://ref.ly/Judg6:11), [6:13](https://ref.ly/Judg6:13), [6:19](https://ref.ly/Judg6:19) \+ 36 t. Ju 6–8; —called also יְרֻבַּעַל (q.v.) Ju [6:32](https://ref.ly/Judg6:32), [7:1](https://ref.ly/Judg7:1) etc., \& יְרֻבֶּשֶׁת (q.v.) [2 S 11:21](https://ref.ly/2Sam11:21).

> \[Str1441] † גִּדְעֹנִי **n.pr.m.** a Benjamite [Nu 1:11](https://ref.ly/Num1:11), [2:22](https://ref.ly/Num2:22), [7:60](https://ref.ly/Num7:60), [7:65](https://ref.ly/Num7:65); גִּדְעוֺנִי [10:24](https://ref.ly/Num10:24) (always ´אֲבִידָן בֶּן־גּ).

> \[Str1440] † גִּדְעֹם **n.pr.loc.** marking limit of pursuit of Benjamites by rest of Israel Ju [20:45](https://ref.ly/Judg20:45).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 14:12; 1 Samuel 2:31; Jeremiah 48:37; Isaiah 15:2; Psalms 7:5; Judges 6:32; 2 Chronicles 34:7; Judges 7:1; Numbers 7:60; Isaiah 45:2; 2 Chronicles 34:4; Deuteronomy 12:3; Isaiah 22:25; Judges 6:19; Numbers 7:65; Isaiah 10:33; Judges 6:13; Lamentations 2:3; 2 Chronicles 31:1; Numbers 2:22; Amos 3:14; Judges 20:45; Psalms 75:11; Jeremiah 50:23; Numbers 10:24; 2 Samuel 11:21; Ezekiel 6:6; Jeremiah 48:25; Zechariah 11:14; 2 Chronicles 14:2; Zechariah 11:10; Psalms 107:16; Isaiah 9:9; Deuteronomy 7:5; Numbers 1:11; Judges 6:11; Judges 21:6
* **Associated Articles:** גָּרַע (ID: `BDB01937@BDBHebrewLexicon`)


## גָּדַף (id: BDB01673)

\[Str1442] † \[גָּדַף] **vb.** only **Pi. revile, blaspheme** (NH גָּדַף *cut, wound,* then (esp. Pi) *revile;* Ar. جَدَفَ *cut, cut off,* II. *deny a favour, be ungrateful,* etc.; Aram. Pa. גַּדֵּף, ܓܰܕܶܦ, *revile*)—*Pf.* 2 ms. וְגִדַּפְתָּ [2 K 19:22](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:22) \= [Is 37:23](https://ref.ly/Isa37:23); 3 pl. גִּדְּפוּ [2 K 19:6](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:6) \= [Is 37:6](https://ref.ly/Isa37:6); *Pt.* מְגַדֵּף [Nu 15:30](https://ref.ly/Num15:30), [Psalm 44:17](https://ref.ly/Ps44:17); —**1\.** *revile,* between man and man, (abs.) קוֺל מְחָרֵף וּמְגַדֵּף [Psalm 44:17](https://ref.ly/Ps44:17) *the voice of* (him that) *reproacheth and revileth*. **2\.** *blaspheme*, sq. acc. ´י [Nu 15:30](https://ref.ly/Num15:30) (P); [2 K 19:22](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:22) \= [Is 37:23](https://ref.ly/Isa37:23) (‖חֵרֵף; obj. אֶת־מִי, ref. to ´י as above); Ez [20:27](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:27); 2 acc. הַדְּבָרִים נַעֲרֵי מֶלֶח אַשּׁוּר אֹתִי ´אֲשֶׁר ג … [2 K 19:6](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:6) \= [Is 37:6](https://ref.ly/Isa37:6) *the words* with *which the servants of the king of Assyria blaspheme me*.

> \[Str1422] † גְּדוּפָה **n.f. taunt,** only Ez [5:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek5:15) וְהָֽיְתָה חֶרְפָּה מוּסָר וּמְשַׁמָּה לַגּוֺיִם ´וּג *and she shall become a reproach and a taunt, an admonition and an astonishment, to the nations*.

> \[Str1421] † גִּדּוּפִים **n.m.pl. revilings, reviling words**—´ג [Is 43:28](https://ref.ly/Isa43:28); cstr. ´גִּדֻּפֵ [Zp 2:8](https://ref.ly/Zeph2:8); sf. גִּדֻּפֹתָם [Is 51:7](https://ref.ly/Isa51:7) between men, [Is 43:28](https://ref.ly/Isa43:28) (‖חֵרֶם); [51:7](https://ref.ly/Isa51:7), [Zp 2:8](https://ref.ly/Zeph2:8) (both ‖חֶרְפָּה).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 37:6; Isaiah 51:7; 2 Kings 19:22; Zephaniah 2:8; Ezekiel 5:15; Numbers 15:30; Isaiah 37:23; Psalms 44:17; 2 Kings 19:6; Ezekiel 20:27; Isaiah 43:28


## גָּדַר (id: BDB01676)

\[Str1443] גָּדַר **vb.** wall up or off, build a wall (denom.? NH *id.,* Ar. جَدَرَ and v. גָּדֵר infr.)—**Qal** *Pf.* גָּדַר [Jb 19:8](https://ref.ly/Job19:8) \+ 2 t., וְגָדַרְתִּ֫י [Ho 2:8](https://ref.ly/Hos2:8), [Am 9:11](https://ref.ly/Amos9:11); *Impf.* 2 mpl. וַתִּגְדְּרוּ Ez [13:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:5); *Pt.* גֹּדֵר [Is 58:12](https://ref.ly/Isa58:12), Ez [22:30](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:30); pl. גֹּדְרִים [2 K 12:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs12:13), [22:6](https://ref.ly/2Kgs22:6); —*wall up, shut off*, lit. only *Pt.,* abs. \= *masons* (wall\-builders) [2 K 12:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs12:13), [22:6](https://ref.ly/2Kgs22:6); also [Is 58:12](https://ref.ly/Isa58:12) (obj. פֶּרֶץ); fig. of Yahweh's dealings with men, obj. אֹרַה [Jb 19:8](https://ref.ly/Job19:8), דֶּרֶך [La 3:9](https://ref.ly/Lam3:9); cf. [Ho 2:8](https://ref.ly/Hos2:8) (acc. cogn. גָּדֵר), in all \= obstructing path of life, cf. also [La 3:7](https://ref.ly/Lam3:7) (c. בַּעֲדִי); of restoring fallen booth of David [Am 9:11](https://ref.ly/Amos9:11) (obj. פרצים); of repairing fortunes of Israel, a work neglected by prophets Ez [13:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:5), and by all in power [22:30](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:30) (both c. acc. cogn.)

> \[Str1444,1447] † גָּדֵר **n.m.**:Ex 42:7 **wall** (NH גָּדֵר, Aram. גָּדֵירָא, גְּדֵירָא, Ar. جَدْرٌ, جِدْرٌ, and more commonly جِدَارٌ *enclosing\-wall* cf. Heb. גְּדוֺר infr.)—abs. גָּדֵר [Nu 22:24](https://ref.ly/Num22:24), [24:24](https://ref.ly/Num24:24) \+ 5 t. \+ [Psalm 62:4](https://ref.ly/Ps62:4) (rd. גדרה cf. infr.) \+ Ez [13:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:5) (Co גדרת cf. infr.); cstr. גָּדֶר [Pr 24:31](https://ref.ly/Prov24:31), Ez [42:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek42:10) (Co del.); sf. גְּדֵרוֺ [Is 5:5](https://ref.ly/Isa5:5), גְּדֵרָהּ [Ho 2:8](https://ref.ly/Hos2:8) (cf. Baer, note); pl. sf. גְּדֵרַיִךְ [Mi 7:11](https://ref.ly/Mic7:11), גּדֵרֶיהָ [Psalm 80:13](https://ref.ly/Ps80:13); —*wall, fence,* bordering a road [Nu 2:24](https://ref.ly/Num2:24), [2:24](https://ref.ly/Num2:24) cf. [Ec 10:8](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:8); connected with Ezekiel's temple Ez [42:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek42:7); city wall [Mi 7:11](https://ref.ly/Mic7:11) cf. [Ezr 9:9](https://ref.ly/Ezra9:9); fig. of Yahweh's vineyard [Is 5:5](https://ref.ly/Isa5:5), [Psalm 80:13](https://ref.ly/Ps80:13); of fortunes of Israel Ez [22:30](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:30); so also Ez [13:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:5) (Co גְּדֶרֶת cf. גְּדֵרָה); of hindrance in path of Israel (fig. as woman) [Ho 2:8](https://ref.ly/Hos2:8); of man beset by enemies [Psalm 62:4](https://ref.ly/Ps62:4) (´ג הַדְּחוּיָה, but rd. ´גְּדֵרָה ד Ol De etc.)

> \[Str1445] † גֶּ֫דֶר **n.pr.loc.** Canaanitish city [Jos 12:13](https://ref.ly/Josh12:13). Γαδερ A \& LXXL also Euseb LagOnom. 244 2nd ed. 254 \= גָּדֵר? LagBN 76; cf. גְּדֵרִי infr.; possibly \= בֵּית גָּדֵר [1 Ch 2:51](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:51) (v. p. III).

> \[Str1448] † I. גְּדֵרָה, גְּדֶ֫רֶת **n.f. wall**—[1 Ch 4:23](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:23) \+ [Psalm 62:4](https://ref.ly/Ps62:4) (MT גָּדֵר q. v.), גְּדֶרֶת Ez [13:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:5) (Co, cf. LXX; MT גָּדֵר) [42:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek42:12) (del. ## גּוּב (id: BDB01696)

\[Str1461] † \[גּוּב] **vb.** dig (cf. Ar. جَابَ *pierce, bore, hollow out*)—**Qal** *Pt.* גָּבִים *diggers* or *plough\-men* [2 K 25:12](https://ref.ly/2Kgs25:12) Kt; but cf. Qr יֹגְּבִים as [Je 52:16](https://ref.ly/Jer52:16) (v. יגב), and v. גֵּב infr.

> \[Str1356] †II. גֵּב **n.\[m.]** pit, ditch, trench, (Ar. جَوْبَةٌ *hollow, depression;* Syr. ܓܽܘܒܳܐ *cistern*)—only pl. גֵּבִים [Je 14:3](https://ref.ly/Jer14:3); in [2 K 3:16](https://ref.ly/2Kgs3:16) גבים גבים, i.e. *full of ditches;* further, *cisterns* (acc. to Klo [2 K 25:12](https://ref.ly/2Kgs25:12)) [Je 39:10](https://ref.ly/Jer39:10) וִּיגֵבִים (rd. וְגֵבִים); cf. also ‖ [2 K 25:12](https://ref.ly/2Kgs25:12) (ולגבים Kt, וּלְיוֺגְבִים Qr) \= [Je 52:16](https://ref.ly/Jer52:16) (וּלְיֹגְבִים; both c. כֹּרְמִים); Klo prop. to emend these, so as to rd. in all וַיִּתֵּן לָהֶם כְּרָמִים וְגֵבִים *and gave to them vineyards and cisterns;* another view in Th; cf. further גוב, יגב.—I. גֵּב v. sub גבה.

> \[Str1374] † גּבִים **n.pr.loc.** (*trenches*) N. fr. Jerusalem, c. art. ´הַגּ [Is 10:31](https://ref.ly/Isa10:31); site unknown.

> \[Str1356] †III. \[גֵּב] **n.\[m.] beam, rafter**? (Thes sub גוב, but mng. \& √ dub.), גֵּבִים [1 K 6:9](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:9); but LagArmen. Stud. § 499, M i. 212; BN 155 rds. גֻּבְּדִים (Pers. گنبد, Armen. γμβεθ) *vaulted roofs*.

> \[Str1463,1996] † גּוֺג **n.pr.m.** (√ unknown)—**1\.** [1 Ch 5:4](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:4) a Reubenite. **2\.** *Gog of the land of Magog, prince of Rosh, Meshak, and Tubal,* גּוֺג אֶרֶץ מָגוֺג נְשִיא ראֹשׁ מֶשֶׁח וְתֻבָל Ez [38:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek38:2), [38:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek38:3) (om. מגוֺג ´א) [39:1](https://ref.ly/Ezek39:1) (om. id.) cf. ## גוה (id: BDB01704)

גוה (*project, be convex*?).

> \[Str1458] † \[גַּו] **n.\[m.]** back—only sf. גֶַּ\+וּךָ [1 K 14:9](https://ref.ly/1Kgs14:9), גֵַּ\+וּךְ Ez [23:35](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:35), גַּוָּם [Ne 9:26](https://ref.ly/Neh9:26); —alw. ´הִשְׁלִיךְ אַחֲרֵי ג *cast behind the back,* i.e. put out of mind, ignore, reject ´י [1 K 14:9](https://ref.ly/1Kgs14:9), Ez [23:35](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:35), and his law [Ne 9:26](https://ref.ly/Neh9:26).
> 
> 156:a. 1\. גֵּו: del. ‘abs. [Jb 39:5](https://ref.ly/Job39:5).’

> \[Str1460] †I. גֵּו **n.\[m.]** back—גֵּו abs. unused, cstr. [Pr 10:13](https://ref.ly/Prov10:13) \+ 2 t., sf. גֵּוִי [Is 50:6](https://ref.ly/Isa50:6), גֵּוְךָ [Is 38:17](https://ref.ly/Isa38:17), גֵּוֵךְ [51:23](https://ref.ly/Isa51:23); —*back,* as beaten, lashed [Pr 10:13](https://ref.ly/Prov10:13), [19:29](https://ref.ly/Prov19:29), [26:3](https://ref.ly/Prov26:3) cf. [Is 50:6](https://ref.ly/Isa50:6); in phr. ´הִשְׁלִיךְ אַחֲרֵי ג (as גַּו) [Is 38:17](https://ref.ly/Isa38:17), of Yahweh's casting sins of penitent behind his back, putting them out of mind; as trodden upon [Is 51:23](https://ref.ly/Isa51:23), fig. of extreme humiliation.

> \[Str1460] †II. גֵּו **n.\[m.]** midst, Aramaism (cf. Aram. גַּוָּא, גֵּו *middle,* **NH** גֵּו *midst, interior;* ܓܰܘ *id., belly,* Ar. جَوٌّ *midst*) [Jb 30:5](https://ref.ly/Job30:5) מִן־גֵּו יְנֹרָשׁוּ *from the midst* (of men) *they are driven;* Rosenmüller al. comp. CiceroOff. iii. 8: *e medio pelluntur*.

BaZMG 1887, 605\) [Jb 20:25](https://ref.ly/Job20:25) וַיֵּצֵיא מִגֵּוָה *it comes out from the back* (of arrow which has struck a fugitive, and is then extracted; Hoffm rds. מִגֵּוֺה).—I. גֵּוָה sub גאה.

Kt); esp. of Israel and (or) Judah as sinful, rebellious [Dt 32:28](https://ref.ly/Deut32:28), Ju [2:20](https://ref.ly/Judg2:20), [Is 1:4](https://ref.ly/Isa1:4), [10:6](https://ref.ly/Isa10:6), [Je 5:9](https://ref.ly/Jer5:9), [5:29](https://ref.ly/Jer5:29), [7:28](https://ref.ly/Jer7:28), [9:8](https://ref.ly/Jer9:8), [12:17](https://ref.ly/Jer12:17), Ez [2:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek2:3) (del. ## I גּוּז (id: BDB01711)

\[Str1468] †I. \[גּוּז] **vb. pass over, away** (Ar. جَازَ *pass by, over;* Aram. גּוּז *cross,* ܓܳܙ *pass away, fail*—**Qal** *Pf* גָּז [Psalm 90:10](https://ref.ly/Ps90:10); *Impf.* וַיָּ֫גָז [Nu 11:31](https://ref.ly/Num11:31); —*pass away*, of the life of the aged, [Psalm 90:10](https://ref.ly/Ps90:10) גָּז חִישׁ; trans. *bring over, subj.* wind, obj. quails fr. sea, [Nu 11:31](https://ref.ly/Num11:31) (rd. perh. Hiph. וַיָּגֶז cf. Köi. 442 Di).

> \[Str1470] † גּוֺזָן **n.pr.loc** (As. *Guzana* COT [2 K 17:6](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:6) DlPa 184\) city and district of Mesopotamia, on or near the middle course of the Euphrates, through which the river *Chabur* (חָבוֺר) flowed; thither some of the exiled Israelites were brought \[by Sargon B.C. 722–21]: גּוֺזָן [2 K 19:12](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:12) \= [Is 37:12](https://ref.ly/Isa37:12); חָבוֺר נְהַר גּוֺזָן [2 K 17:6](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:6), [18:11](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:11); וְחָבוֺר וְהָרָא וּנְ הַר גּוֺזָן [1 Ch 5:26](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:26) where *Chabur* is separated entirely from the *river of Gozan* (v. חבור).

> \[Str1518] גוח v. גיח

> \[Str1471] גּוֺי v. sub גוה. p. 156

> \[Str1472] גְּוִיָּה v. sub גוה. p. 156

> \[Str1471] † גּוֺיִם **n.pr.gent.** [Gn 14:1](https://ref.ly/Gen14:1), [14:9](https://ref.ly/Gen14:9), in phr. תִּדְעָל מֶלֶח גּוֺיִם *Tid‘al king of Goim;* prob. a Babylonian (Elamitic, etc.) name corrupted; H. Rawlinson prop. *Gutî* a people NE. of Babylonia COT on [Gn 14:1](https://ref.ly/Gen14:1); also KGF258 al.; cf. also DlPa 233 f.

> \[Str1523] גול v. גיל

> \[Str1474] † גּוֺלָן **n.pr.loc** (? connected with √ גול Ar. جَالَ *go about, around;* جَوْلٌ *circuit;* cf. Ph. גול n.pr. insul.; also גָּלִיל, גְּלִילָה) city of Manasseh in Bashan, named as a city of refuge [Dt 4:43](https://ref.ly/Deut4:43) cf. [Jos 20:9](https://ref.ly/Josh20:9), [21:27](https://ref.ly/Josh21:27) both Qr (Kt גלון); given to sons of Gershom son of Levi acc. to [1 Ch 6:56](https://ref.ly/1Chr6:56); Euseb. knew it as a very large village Γαυλὼν ἤ Γωλάν LagOnom. 242, 2nd ed. 253; district of same name *ib.,* JosephusAnt. viii. 2, 3 etc Gaulanitis, mod. Jaulân RobBR ii, 432 BDPal 270 SchumacherZPV 1886 \= Jaulân 1888, acc. to most, name applied first to city, thence to district, but perh. otherwise if above deriv. be correct.

* **Associated Passages:** Genesis 14:9; Joshua 20:9; 1 Chronicles 5:26; Joshua 21:27; Psalms 90:10; 1 Chronicles 6:56; Numbers 11:31; Deuteronomy 4:43; 2 Kings 19:12; Genesis 14:1; 2 Kings 17:6; 2 Kings 18:11; Isaiah 37:12

## גּון (id: BDB01719)

גּון (mng. dub., cf. perh. Aram. גַּוֵּן *tinge,* ܓܘܰܢ, ܓܘܽܢ; Ar. جَوْنٌ *reddish black,* cf. HommelSäugethiere 64\).

> \[Str1476] † גּוּנִי **n.pr.m. 1\.** a Naphtalite [Gn 46:24](https://ref.ly/Gen46:24), [Nu 26:48](https://ref.ly/Num26:48), [1 Ch 7:13](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:13). **2\.** [1 Ch 5:15](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:15) a Gadite.

> \[Str1477] † גּוּנִי **adj. gent.** of foregoing **1\.**; c. art as n. coll. הַגּוּנִי [Nu 26:48](https://ref.ly/Num26:48); also of individ. [2 S 23:32](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:32) acc. to LXXL (Ιεσσαι ὁ Γουνι) Klo Dr; rd. יָשֵׁן הַגּוּנִי; also [1 Ch 11:34](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:34) where rd. *id.* for השׁם הגזוני (LXXL Εἰρασαι ὁ Γουνι).

[Nu 17:28](https://ref.ly/Num17:28) (P); ‖אבד [Nu 17:27](https://ref.ly/Num17:27) *we expire, we perish* (P); ‖עָנִי [Psalm 88:15](https://ref.ly/Ps88:15) (progressive parall.) *distressed and about to die;* ‖יאסף אל אבותיו [Gn 49:33](https://ref.ly/Gen49:33); ‖על־עפר ישׁוב [Jb 34:15](https://ref.ly/Job34:15) cf. [Psalm 104:29](https://ref.ly/Ps104:29); ‖יִכָּֽרְתוּ [Zc 13:8](https://ref.ly/Zech13:8); abs., both prose and (esp.) poetry [Nu 20:3](https://ref.ly/Num20:3), [20:3](https://ref.ly/Num20:3), [20:29](https://ref.ly/Num20:29), [Jos 22:20](https://ref.ly/Josh22:20), [Gn 6:17](https://ref.ly/Gen6:17), [7:21](https://ref.ly/Gen7:21) (all P) [Jb 10:13](https://ref.ly/Job10:13), [13:19](https://ref.ly/Job13:19), [27:5](https://ref.ly/Job27:5), [36:12](https://ref.ly/Job36:12), [La 1:19](https://ref.ly/Lam1:19); [Jb 29:18](https://ref.ly/Job29:18) עִם־קִנִּי אגוע *with my nest shall I die*, i.e. in full possession of what is mine, cf. ## I גּוּר (id: BDB01725)

\[Str1481] †I. גּוּר **vb.** sojourn (cf. Ar. جَارَ *turn aside tarry,* v. esp. III. IV. VIII. X; Aram. ܓܳܪ Eth in der.)—**Qal** *Pf.* גָּר [Gn 35:27](https://ref.ly/Gen35:27) \+ 3 t., וְגָר consec. [Is 11:6](https://ref.ly/Isa11:6); גַּרְתָּה [Gn 21:23](https://ref.ly/Gen21:23); גַּרְתִּי [Gn 32:5](https://ref.ly/Gen32:5), [Psalm 120:5](https://ref.ly/Ps120:5); גָּרוּ [Ex 6:4](https://ref.ly/Exod6:4); *Impf.* יָגוּר [Ex 12:48](https://ref.ly/Exod12:48) \+ 12 t., וַיָּ֫גָו [Gn 20:1](https://ref.ly/Gen20:1) \+ 2 t.; sf. יְגֻרְךָ [Psalm 5:5](https://ref.ly/Ps5:5); 3 fs. וַתָּ֫גָר [2 K 8:2](https://ref.ly/2Kgs8:2); 2 fs. תָּגוּרִי [2 K 8:1](https://ref.ly/2Kgs8:1); cohort. אָג\+וּרָה [Psalm 61:5](https://ref.ly/Ps61:5) יָגוּרוּ [Is 16:4](https://ref.ly/Isa16:4); *Imv.* גּוּר [Gn 26:3](https://ref.ly/Gen26:3), גּוּ֫רִי [2 K 8:1](https://ref.ly/2Kgs8:1); *Inf.* cstr. גּוּר [Gn 12:10](https://ref.ly/Gen12:10) \+ 17 t.; *Pt* גָּר [Dt 18:6](https://ref.ly/Deut18:6), Ju [17:7](https://ref.ly/Judg17:7) \+ 17 t.; f. cstr. גָּרַת [Ex 3:22](https://ref.ly/Exod3:22); pl. גָּרִים [Lv 25:6](https://ref.ly/Lev25:6) \+ 8 t.; cstr. גָּרֵי [Jb 19:15](https://ref.ly/Job19:15); —**1\.** *sojourn,* dwell for a (defintie or indef.) time, dwell as a new\-comer (cf. גֵּר) without original rights, v. esp. [Gn 19:9](https://ref.ly/Gen19:9) (J) [Dt 18:6](https://ref.ly/Deut18:6) \& cf. Ju [17:7](https://ref.ly/Judg17:7), [17:8](https://ref.ly/Judg17:8), [17:9](https://ref.ly/Judg17:9), [19:1](https://ref.ly/Judg19:1); also [Gn 12:10](https://ref.ly/Gen12:10), [20:1](https://ref.ly/Gen20:1), [21:23](https://ref.ly/Gen21:23), [21:34](https://ref.ly/Gen21:34), [32:5](https://ref.ly/Gen32:5), [47:4](https://ref.ly/Gen47:4) (all JE); of partriarchs in Canaan [Gn 26:3](https://ref.ly/Gen26:3) (J) [35:27](https://ref.ly/Gen35:27), [Ex 6:4](https://ref.ly/Exod6:4) (both P) cf. [Psalm 105:12](https://ref.ly/Ps105:12) \= [1 Ch 16:19](https://ref.ly/1Chr16:19), Ju [19:16](https://ref.ly/Judg19:16), [2 S 4:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam4:3), [2 K 8:1](https://ref.ly/2Kgs8:1), [8:1](https://ref.ly/2Kgs8:1), [8:2](https://ref.ly/2Kgs8:2) (seven years) [Is 16:4](https://ref.ly/Isa16:4), [Ru 1:1](https://ref.ly/Ruth1:1), [Ezr 1:4](https://ref.ly/Ezra1:4) (in exile); cf. further [Ex 3:22](https://ref.ly/Exod3:22) (E) גָּרַת בַּיִת of a woman in another's house as lodger or guest (v. Di), גָּרֵי בַיִת [Jb 19:15](https://ref.ly/Job19:15); so (poet.) of worshipper in Yahweh's house [Psalm 15:1](https://ref.ly/Ps15:1), [61:5](https://ref.ly/Ps61:5) cf. [Is 33:14](https://ref.ly/Isa33:14), [33:14](https://ref.ly/Isa33:14); fig. of evil לאֹ יְגֻֽרְךָ רָע [Psalm 5:5](https://ref.ly/Ps5:5) *evil cannot be a guest of thine* (Che; sq. acc., as in Ar. جاوره III); particulary of the גֵּר (q.v.) in Israel [Ex 12:48](https://ref.ly/Exod12:48), [12:49](https://ref.ly/Exod12:49), [Lv 16:29](https://ref.ly/Lev16:29) (all P) [17:8](https://ref.ly/Lev17:8), [17:10](https://ref.ly/Lev17:10), [17:12](https://ref.ly/Lev17:12), [17:13](https://ref.ly/Lev17:13), [18:26](https://ref.ly/Lev18:26), [19:33](https://ref.ly/Lev19:33), [19:34](https://ref.ly/Lev19:34), [20:2](https://ref.ly/Lev20:2), [25:6](https://ref.ly/Lev25:6), [25:45](https://ref.ly/Lev25:45) (all H) [Nu 9:14](https://ref.ly/Num9:14), [15:14](https://ref.ly/Num15:14), [15:15](https://ref.ly/Num15:15), [15:16](https://ref.ly/Num15:16), [15:26](https://ref.ly/Num15:26), [15:29](https://ref.ly/Num15:29), [19:10](https://ref.ly/Num19:10), [Jos 20:9](https://ref.ly/Josh20:9) (all P) Ez [14:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek14:7), [47:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:22), [47:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:23); of Ephraim and Manasseh sojourning with Judah and Benjamin [2 Ch 15:9](https://ref.ly/2Chr15:9); of Rechabites sojourning in Judah [Je 35:7](https://ref.ly/Jer35:7); of Israelites sojourning in Egypt [Dt 26:5](https://ref.ly/Deut26:5), [Is 52:4](https://ref.ly/Isa52:4), [Psalm 105:23](https://ref.ly/Ps105:23); so of Judah, to escape from under Babylonian power [Je 42:15](https://ref.ly/Jer42:15), [42:17](https://ref.ly/Jer42:17), [42:22](https://ref.ly/Jer42:22), [43:2](https://ref.ly/Jer43:2), [44:8](https://ref.ly/Jer44:8), [44:12](https://ref.ly/Jer44:12), [44:14](https://ref.ly/Jer44:14), [44:28](https://ref.ly/Jer44:28). **2\.** *abide*, nearly or quite \= *dwell* [Je 43:5](https://ref.ly/Jer43:5) cf. [La 4:15](https://ref.ly/Lam4:15), [Je 49:18](https://ref.ly/Jer49:18), [49:33](https://ref.ly/Jer49:33), [50:40](https://ref.ly/Jer50:40), also [Jb 28:4](https://ref.ly/Job28:4); of wolf dwelling with lamb [Is 11:6](https://ref.ly/Isa11:6); *stay* (inactive) Ju [5:17](https://ref.ly/Judg5:17) ;—in [Is 5:17](https://ref.ly/Isa5:17) rd. perh., for גָּרִים, גְּדָיִם or כָּרִים cf. LXX Lo Ew Di \& Che crit. n. (\> Stu גֵּרִים) **Hithpol.** *Pt.* מִתְגּוֺרֵר [1 K 17:20](https://ref.ly/1Kgs17:20) *seek hospitality with,* sq. עִם (cf.Ar. x); יִתְגּוֺרָר֭וּ [Ho 7:14](https://ref.ly/Hos7:14) is dub. (cf. II. גור); AE Ki Thes Wü AV RV *they assemble themselves*, but txt. prob. err., v. גדד.

> \[Str1483] †I. גּוּר **n.pr.loc.** (*sojourning, dwelling*) [2 K 9:27](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:27) אשׁר את־יִבְלְעָם ´מַעֲלֵה־ג, otherwise unknown.

> \[Str1485] † גּוּר־בָּ֫עַל **n.pr.loc.** (*dwelling* or *Gûr of Baal*) [2 Ch 26:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr26:7) הָעֲרָבִי֯ים הַיּשְׁבִים בְּגוּר־בָּעַל.

> \[Str1616] גֵּר **n.m.**:[Ex 12:48](https://ref.ly/Exod12:48) sojourner (Ar. جَارٌ, Eth. ግዩር, ጎር፡ Aram. ܓܝܻܘܽܪ, גִּיּוֺר, *prosetlyte*, גַּיֵּר *proselytize*, Ph. גר in n.pr., \& pl. גרם)—גֵּר [Gn 15:16](https://ref.ly/Gen15:16) \+ 74 t.; sf. גֵּרְךָ [Ex 20:10](https://ref.ly/Exod20:10) \+ 4 t., גֵּרוֺ [Dt 1:16](https://ref.ly/Deut1:16); pl. גֵּרִים [Ex 22:20](https://ref.ly/Exod22:20) \+ 9 t., גֵּירִים [2 Ch 2:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr2:16); —**1\.** *sojourner,* temporary dweller, new\-comer (no inherited rights), cf. [Ex 12:19](https://ref.ly/Exod12:19), [Lv 24:16](https://ref.ly/Lev24:16), [Nu 15:30](https://ref.ly/Num15:30), [Jos 8:33](https://ref.ly/Josh8:33) (opp. *homeborn*); of Abraham at Hebron [Gn 23:4](https://ref.ly/Gen23:4) (P; ‖תּוֺשָׁב); Moses in desert [Ex 2:22](https://ref.ly/Exod2:22) (J) [18:3](https://ref.ly/Exod18:3) (E; here explan. of name Gershom, Moses' son); as claiming hospitality [Jb 31:32](https://ref.ly/Job31:32); perh. in above cases, and certainly in general, with technical sense; fig. of Yahweh [Je 14:8](https://ref.ly/Jer14:8); of Israel in Egypt [Gn 15:13](https://ref.ly/Gen15:13), [Ex 22:20](https://ref.ly/Exod22:20), [23:9](https://ref.ly/Exod23:9) (all JE) [Lv 19:34](https://ref.ly/Lev19:34) (H) [Dt 10:19](https://ref.ly/Deut10:19), [23:8](https://ref.ly/Deut23:8); גֵּרִים with Yahweh [Lv 25:23](https://ref.ly/Lev25:23) (H) [1 Ch 29:15](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:15), [Psalm 39:13](https://ref.ly/Ps39:13) (in all ‖תּוֺשָׁב) cf. [119:19](https://ref.ly/Ps119:19). **2\.** usually of גֵּרים in Israel [2 S 1:13](https://ref.ly/2Sam1:13) (Amalekite) cf. [Jos 8:33](https://ref.ly/Josh8:33), [8:35](https://ref.ly/Josh8:35) (E) [20:9](https://ref.ly/Josh20:9) (P) [Is 14:1](https://ref.ly/Isa14:1); dwellers in Israel with certain conceded, not inherited right (cf. RSOTJC 434; 2nd ed. 342\. n.; K 42; Sem 75 f. StaGesch. i.400\). The גֵּר is to share in Sabbath rest [Ex 20:10](https://ref.ly/Exod20:10), [23:12](https://ref.ly/Exod23:12) (both JE) [Dt 5:14](https://ref.ly/Deut5:14); otherwise he is have like obligations with Israel [Ex 12:19](https://ref.ly/Exod12:19), [12:48](https://ref.ly/Exod12:48), [12:49](https://ref.ly/Exod12:49), [Lv 16:29](https://ref.ly/Lev16:29) (all P) [Lv 17:8](https://ref.ly/Lev17:8), [17:10](https://ref.ly/Lev17:10), [17:12](https://ref.ly/Lev17:12), [17:13](https://ref.ly/Lev17:13), [17:15](https://ref.ly/Lev17:15), [18:26](https://ref.ly/Lev18:26), [20:2](https://ref.ly/Lev20:2), [22:18](https://ref.ly/Lev22:18), [24:16](https://ref.ly/Lev24:16), [24:22](https://ref.ly/Lev24:22) (all H) [Nu 9:14](https://ref.ly/Num9:14), [9:14](https://ref.ly/Num9:14), [15:14](https://ref.ly/Num15:14), [15:15](https://ref.ly/Num15:15), [15:15](https://ref.ly/Num15:15), [15:16](https://ref.ly/Num15:16), [15:26](https://ref.ly/Num15:26), [15:29](https://ref.ly/Num15:29), [15:30](https://ref.ly/Num15:30), [19:10](https://ref.ly/Num19:10), [35:15](https://ref.ly/Num35:15) (all P) Ez [14:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek14:7); similar rights [Dt 1:16](https://ref.ly/Deut1:16), Ez [47:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:22), [47:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:23); and like privileges [Dt 16:11](https://ref.ly/Deut16:11), [16:14](https://ref.ly/Deut16:14), [26:11](https://ref.ly/Deut26:11), [29:10](https://ref.ly/Deut29:10), [31:12](https://ref.ly/Deut31:12) cf. [2 Ch 30:25](https://ref.ly/2Chr30:25); very rarely any distinction made, in obligation [Lv 25:47](https://ref.ly/Lev25:47), [25:47](https://ref.ly/Lev25:47), [25:47](https://ref.ly/Lev25:47) (H), in permissible food [Dt 14:21](https://ref.ly/Deut14:21); in future success [Dt 28:43](https://ref.ly/Deut28:43); kindness to גֵּר frequently enjoined: [Lv 19:10](https://ref.ly/Lev19:10) (‖עָנִי), [23:22](https://ref.ly/Lev23:22) (‖ *id.*) [19:34](https://ref.ly/Lev19:34) (all H); [Dt 10:18](https://ref.ly/Deut10:18), [10:19](https://ref.ly/Deut10:19), [14:29](https://ref.ly/Deut14:29), [24:19](https://ref.ly/Deut24:19), [24:20](https://ref.ly/Deut24:20), [24:21](https://ref.ly/Deut24:21), [26:12](https://ref.ly/Deut26:12), [26:13](https://ref.ly/Deut26:13) (all ‖יָתוֺם וְאַלְמָנָה); oppression prohibited [Lv 19:33](https://ref.ly/Lev19:33) (H) [Dt 24:14](https://ref.ly/Deut24:14); [Ex 22:20](https://ref.ly/Exod22:20), [23:9](https://ref.ly/Exod23:9), [23:9](https://ref.ly/Exod23:9) (JE) [Dt 24:17](https://ref.ly/Deut24:17), [27:19](https://ref.ly/Deut27:19), [Je 7:6](https://ref.ly/Jer7:6), [22:3](https://ref.ly/Jer22:3), [Zc 7:10](https://ref.ly/Zech7:10) (these eight ‖יָתוֺם וְאַלְמָנָה); obj. of care to ´י [Psalm 146:9](https://ref.ly/Ps146:9) (‖ *id.*); charge that גֵּר has been oppressed Ez [22:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:7), [Mal 3:5](https://ref.ly/Mal3:5) (both ‖ *id.*); also Ez [22:29](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:29) (‖עָנִי וְאֶבְווֺן), [Psalm 94:6](https://ref.ly/Ps94:6); cf. also command that a poor brother be treated like גֵּר i.e. kindly, [Lv 25:35](https://ref.ly/Lev25:35) ( H). Latest conception somewhat different: גֵּר [1 Ch 22:2](https://ref.ly/1Chr22:2), [2 Ch 2:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr2:16) (הַגֵּירִים) gathered for hard service; yet cf. [2 Ch 30:25](https://ref.ly/2Chr30:25). (Oft. c. verb. cogn. [Ex 12:48](https://ref.ly/Exod12:48), [12:49](https://ref.ly/Exod12:49), [Lv 16:29](https://ref.ly/Lev16:29), [17:8](https://ref.ly/Lev17:8), [17:10](https://ref.ly/Lev17:10), [17:12](https://ref.ly/Lev17:12), [17:13](https://ref.ly/Lev17:13), [18:26](https://ref.ly/Lev18:26), [19:33](https://ref.ly/Lev19:33), [20:2](https://ref.ly/Lev20:2), [Nu 9:14](https://ref.ly/Num9:14), [15:14](https://ref.ly/Num15:14), [15:15](https://ref.ly/Num15:15), [15:16](https://ref.ly/Num15:16), [15:26](https://ref.ly/Num15:26), [15:29](https://ref.ly/Num15:29), [19:10](https://ref.ly/Num19:10), [Jos 20:9](https://ref.ly/Josh20:9), Ez [47:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:22), [47:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:23); oft ‖תּוֺשָׁב [Gn 23:4](https://ref.ly/Gen23:4), [Lv 25:23](https://ref.ly/Lev25:23), [25:35](https://ref.ly/Lev25:35), [25:47](https://ref.ly/Lev25:47), [1 Ch 29:15](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:15), [Psalm 39:13](https://ref.ly/Ps39:13)).

> \[Str1628,3643] † גּרוּת **n.f. lodging** (\- place)—וישׁבו בְּגֵרוּת כִּמְוהָם אשׁר אצל בית לחם [Je 41:17](https://ref.ly/Jer41:17), *inn, khan*? so Ew Gf; Hi al. גִּדְרוֺת *folds, after* Joseph. Aq.

> \[Str3017] † יָגוּר **n.pr.loc.** a southern city of Judah, towards Edom [Jos 15:21](https://ref.ly/Josh15:21).

> \[Str4033] † \[מָגוֺר] **n.\[m.]** sojourning\-place, dwelling\-place, sojourning—sf. ם [Psalm 55:16](https://ref.ly/Ps55:16); pl. cstr. מְגוּרֵי [Gn 37:1](https://ref.ly/Gen37:1); sf. מְגוּרַי [Gn 47:9](https://ref.ly/Gen47:9), מְגוּרָ\+י [Psalm 119:54](https://ref.ly/Ps119:54), מְגֻרֶיךָ [Gn 17:8](https://ref.ly/Gen17:8), [28:4](https://ref.ly/Gen28:4), מְגוּרָיו [Jb 18:19](https://ref.ly/Job18:19), מְגוּרֵיהֶם [Gn 36:7](https://ref.ly/Gen36:7) \+ 2 t. מְגֻרֵיהֶם [Ex 6:4](https://ref.ly/Exod6:4); —*dwelling\-place* [Psalm 55:16](https://ref.ly/Ps55:16), [Jb 18:19](https://ref.ly/Job18:19); —*sojourning( \-place)*, always pl. ´אֶרֶץ מ [Gn 17:8](https://ref.ly/Gen17:8), [28:4](https://ref.ly/Gen28:4), [36:7](https://ref.ly/Gen36:7), [37:1](https://ref.ly/Gen37:1), [Ex 6:4](https://ref.ly/Exod6:4) (Hex always P) Ez [20:38](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:38); *sojourning* (pl.) \= life time, ´שְׁנֵי מ [Gn 47:9](https://ref.ly/Gen47:9) ´יְמֵי מ [Gn 47:9](https://ref.ly/Gen47:9); cf. ´בֵּית מ [Psalm 119:54](https://ref.ly/Ps119:54).

> \[Str4035] † מְגוּרָה **n.f.** store\-house, granary [Hg 2:19](https://ref.ly/Hag2:19).

> \[Str4460] † מַמְּגֻרוֺת **n.f.pl.** *id.,* [Jo 1:17](https://ref.ly/Joel1:17) (‖אֹצָרוֺת; but LXX ληνοί, Me גתות)
