## II גּוּר (id: BDB01734)

\[Str1481] †II. \[גּוּר] **vb. stir up strift, quarrel** (cf. גרה)—so Ew Che Di RVm ([Is 54:15](https://ref.ly/Isa54:15)); gather together Ges De Br cf. Targum Vulgate, so AV RV;—**Qal.** *Pf.* גָּר [Is 54:15](https://ref.ly/Isa54:15); *Impf.* יָגוּר v:[15](https://ref.ly/Isa54:15), יָגוּרוּ [Psalm 56:7](https://ref.ly/Ps56:7), [59:4](https://ref.ly/Ps59:4), [140:3](https://ref.ly/Ps140:3) (on all cf. infr.); *Inf.* abs. גּוֺר Is [54:15](https://ref.ly/Isa54:15); —**1\.** *stir up strife*, abs. גּוֺר יָגוּר [Is 54:14](https://ref.ly/Isa54:14); cf. [Psalm 56:7](https://ref.ly/Ps56:7)(but AE Che rd. יָגוֺדּוּ *attack*, v. גדד); sq. עַ־ל [Psalm 59:4](https://ref.ly/Ps59:4) (but Che *id.*; cf. [Psalm 94:21](https://ref.ly/Ps94:21), wh. however Ol would emend by [56:7](https://ref.ly/Ps56:7), [59:4](https://ref.ly/Ps59:4)); sq. acc. מִלְחָמוֺת [Psalm 140:3](https://ref.ly/Ps140:3) (Hup Che rd. יְגָרוּ Pi. Impf. of גרה) **2\.** *quarrel,* sq. אֶ־ת *with* [Is 54:15](https://ref.ly/Isa54:15). Here also **Hithpol.** *Impf.* [Ho 7:14](https://ref.ly/Hos7:14) יִתְגּוֺרָ֭רוּ (cf. I. גור), acc. to Ew, *they excite themselves,* but unlikely; v. גדד.

> \[Str1484] † \[גּוֺר] **n.\[m.] whelp** (as *quarrelsome?* or onomatop.? perhaps \= As. *giru* ZehnpfundBAS i. 504\) of lion ;—אֲרָיוֺת [Je 51:38](https://ref.ly/Jer51:38) (‖כְּפִרִים) sim. of Babylonians; cf. גֹּרֹתָיו [Na 2:13](https://ref.ly/Nah2:13) (‖ *id.* v:[14](https://ref.ly/Nah2:13)) fig. of Assyrians.

> \[Str1482] †II. גּוּר **n.m.**:Ez [19:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek19:3), [19:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek19:5) **whelp, young**—cstr. (אַרְיֵה)גּוּר [Gn 49:9](https://ref.ly/Gen49:9) \+ 2 t.; sf. גּוּרֶיהָ Ez [19:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek19:2), גֻּרֶיהָ Ez [19:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek19:3), [19:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek19:5), גּוּרֵיהֶן [La 4:3](https://ref.ly/Lam4:3); —**1\.** lion's *whelps,* fig. of Judah [Gn 49:9](https://ref.ly/Gen49:9); of Dan [Dt 33:22](https://ref.ly/Deut33:22); of Assyrian (prince ?) [Na 2:12](https://ref.ly/Nah2:12); fig. of Israelites Ez [19:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek19:2), [19:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek19:3), [19:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek19:5) (‖כְּפִיר). **2\.** young of jackals (תַּנִּין) [La 4:3](https://ref.ly/Lam4:3).

## III גּוּר (id: BDB01737)

\[Str1481] †III. \[גּוּר] **vb. dread** (cf. יָגֹר)—**Qal** *Impf.* וַיָּגָר [Nu 22:3](https://ref.ly/Num22:3), [1 S 18:15](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:15); 2 ms. תָּגוּר [Dt 18:22](https://ref.ly/Deut18:22); אָגוּר [Dt 32:27](https://ref.ly/Deut32:27); pl. יָגוּרוּ [Psalm 33:8](https://ref.ly/Ps33:8), [Jb 41:17](https://ref.ly/Job41:17) \+ [Ho 10:5](https://ref.ly/Hos10:5), תָּגוּרוּ [Dt 1:17](https://ref.ly/Deut1:17); *Imv.* גּוּרוּ [Psalm 22:24](https://ref.ly/Ps22:24), [Jb 19:29](https://ref.ly/Job19:29); —**1\.** *be afraid of,* sq. מִפְּנֵי [Nu 22:3](https://ref.ly/Num22:3), [Dt 1:17](https://ref.ly/Deut1:17), [1 S 18:15](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:15), גּוּרוּ לָכֶם מִפְּנֵי [Jb 19:29](https://ref.ly/Job19:29); c. מִן [41:17](https://ref.ly/Job41:17), [Dt 18:22](https://ref.ly/Deut18:22); c. acc. [Dt 32:27](https://ref.ly/Deut32:27) (poet.) [Ho 10:5](https://ref.ly/Hos10:5) rd. prob. ינודו *lament* (‖אבל, used c. לְ *e.g.* [Na 3:7](https://ref.ly/Nah3:7), [Je 15:5](https://ref.ly/Jer15:5) al.; cf. Che). **2\.** *stand in awe* of, sq. מִן [Psalm 22:24](https://ref.ly/Ps22:24), [33:8](https://ref.ly/Ps33:8) (יָרֵא).

> \[Str4032,4036] † מָגוֺר **n.m.** fear, terror—abs. ´מ [Psalm 31:14](https://ref.ly/Ps31:14) \+ 6 t. \+ [Je 20:3](https://ref.ly/Jer20:3) cf. infr.; pl. sf. מְגוּרַי [La 2:22](https://ref.ly/Lam2:22); —*fear, terror* [Is 31:9](https://ref.ly/Isa31:9); \= cause of terror [Je 20:4](https://ref.ly/Jer20:4); elsewhere in phrase, coined by Jerem., מִסָּבִיב ´מ *terror on every side* [Je 6:25](https://ref.ly/Jer6:25), [20:3](https://ref.ly/Jer20:3) (where as n.pr.), v:[10](https://ref.ly/Jer20:10), [46:5](https://ref.ly/Jer46:5), [49:29](https://ref.ly/Jer49:29), [Psalm 31:14](https://ref.ly/Ps31:14), [La 2:22](https://ref.ly/Lam2:22) (´מְגוּרַי מִסּ).

> \[Str4034] † \[מְגוֺרָה] **n.f.** fear, terror—cstr. מְגוֺרַת [Pr 10:24](https://ref.ly/Prov10:24); pl. sf. מְגוּרוֺתַי [Psalm 34:5](https://ref.ly/Ps34:5), מְגוּרֹתָם [Is 66:4](https://ref.ly/Isa66:4) (on these forms with וּ cf. Ges§ 27\. 3\. R.1, \& 85\. 48\);—*terror* (\= thing dreaded) [Psalm 34:5](https://ref.ly/Ps34:5), [Pr 10:24](https://ref.ly/Prov10:24), [Is 66:4](https://ref.ly/Isa66:4).

> \[Str1627] \[גורן] [Je 2:25](https://ref.ly/Jer2:25) גורנך Kt v. גָּרוֺן. p.173

## גָּזָה (id: BDB01745)

\[Str1491] † \[גָּזָה] **vb.** cut, (cut off, sever), √ of גָּוִית (cf. Talm. גְּזָא *cut off*); hence perh. **Qal** *Pt. act.*sf. גּוֺוִי [Psalm 71:6](https://ref.ly/Ps71:6) thou art *he that severed me* from my mother's womb (De Che; LXX σκεπαστής, De prop. Εκσπαστής, v. LXX [Psalm 22:10](https://ref.ly/Ps22:10); Vulgate *protector* cf. LXX, Thes Ew Hup Pe *benefactor*, cf. Ar. گنجور جزا *requite*, Jerus. Syr. جَزَا, Talm. גְּזָא *id.*);—txt. and mng. dub.; ‖ [Psalm 22:10](https://ref.ly/Ps22:10) has גֹּחִי; v. Checrit. n. and OP 476; Du עֻזִּי.

> \[Str1496] † גָּזִית **n.f. a cutting, hewing**—גָּזִית [Ex 20:25](https://ref.ly/Exod20:25) \+ 10 t., always abs.;—*hewing*, ´אַבְנֵי ג \= *hewn stones* (building\-stones) [1 K 5:31](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:31), [1 Ch 22:2](https://ref.ly/1Chr22:2), Ez [40:42](https://ref.ly/Ezek40:42) (for tables), cf. ´אֲבָנִים יְקָרוֺת כְּמִדּוֺת ג [1 K 7:9](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:9), [7:11](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:11) (but Klo del. as gloss, cf. LXX); also without אבני \= *hewn stones* [Ex 20:25](https://ref.ly/Exod20:25) (altar) [Am 5:11](https://ref.ly/Amos5:11) (´בָּֽתֵּי ג) [Is 9:9](https://ref.ly/Isa9:9) (opp. לְבֵנִים); ´טוּרֵי ג [1 K 6:36](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:36), ´טוּרִים ג [7:12](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:12); ܓܙܐ; also [La 3:9](https://ref.ly/Lam3:9) ´גָּדַר דַּרְכִּי בְּג.

> \[Str1492] גִּזָּה v. sub גזז. below

> \[Str1493] †גִּזוֺנִי **adj. gent.** [1 Ch 11:34](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:34) הָשֵׁם הַגִּֽווֺנִי, but rd. יָשֵׁן הַגּוּנִי (LXXL Εἰρασαι ὁ Γουνι) cf. גּוּנִי.

\[Str1494] †\[גָּזַז] **vb.** shear (NH *id.,* Aram. גֲּזָו ܓܰܙ Ar. جَزَّ As. deriv.)—**Qal** *Impf.* וַיָּ֫גָו [Jb 1:20](https://ref.ly/Job1:20); 2 ms. תָּגֹו [Dt 15:19](https://ref.ly/Deut15:19); *Imv.* 2 fs. גָּוִּ֫י [Je 7:29](https://ref.ly/Jer7:29), גֹּ֫וִּי [Mi 1:16](https://ref.ly/Mic1:16); *Inf.* cstr. גְּזֹו [Gn 31:19](https://ref.ly/Gen31:19) (E) [1 S 25:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:2), גֹּו [Gn 38:13](https://ref.ly/Gen38:13) (J); *Pt.* גֹּזֵז [1 S 25:4](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:4); pl. גֹּזֲזִים [1 S 25:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:7) \+ 2 t.; cstr. גֹּזַוֵי [Gn 38:13](https://ref.ly/Gen38:13); sf. גֹּזְַזָ\+י [1 S 25:11](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:11), גֹּזְַוֶיהָ [Is 53:7](https://ref.ly/Isa53:7); —*shear* sheep (obj. צאֹן) [Gn 31:19](https://ref.ly/Gen31:19), [38:13](https://ref.ly/Gen38:13), [Dt 15:19](https://ref.ly/Deut15:19), [1 S 25:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:2), [25:4](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:4); cf. *Pt. shearer* (c. חאו) [Gn 38:12](https://ref.ly/Gen38:12), also גֹּזְַוֶיהָ (i.e. רָחֵל) [Is 53:7](https://ref.ly/Isa53:7); abs. \= *sheep\-shearer,* [1 S 25:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:7), [25:11](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:11), [2 S 13:23](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:23), [13:24](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:24); obj. man's head (ראשׁוֺ) [Jb 1:20](https://ref.ly/Job1:20); of a woman's (fig. of Israel) [Mi 1:16](https://ref.ly/Mic1:16) (no obj.; ‖קרחי), (fig. of Jerusalem) [Je 7:29](https://ref.ly/Jer7:29) (obj. נֵזֶר q.v.) **Niph.** נָגוֹ֫זּוּ *be cut off* (\= destroyed) of Assyrians [Na 1:12](https://ref.ly/Nah1:12).

[Dt 18:4](https://ref.ly/Deut18:4), [Jb 31:20](https://ref.ly/Job31:20); pl. cstr. גִּזֵּי [Am 7:1](https://ref.ly/Amos7:1); —*shearing* \= thing sheared off, *wool, fleece* צאֹן ´ג [Dt 18:4](https://ref.ly/Deut18:4), כְּבָשַי ´ג [Jb 31:20](https://ref.ly/Job31:20); *mowing* [Am 7:1](https://ref.ly/Amos7:1) (הַמֶּלֶךְ ´ג cf. ## I גָּזַל (id: BDB01754)

\[Str1497] †I. גָּזַל **vb. tear away, seize**, rob (NH *id.,* Ph. גזל Niph., Aram. גְּוַל, ܓܠܰܙ \& (Nas.) ܓܙܰܠ; Ar. جَزَلَ *cut off*)—**Qal** *Pf.* גָּזַל [Jb 20:19](https://ref.ly/Job20:19), Ez [18:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek18:18), גָּ\+זל [Lv 5:23](https://ref.ly/Lev5:23) \+2 t., גָּוַלְתִּי [Psalm 69:5](https://ref.ly/Ps69:5) etc.; *Impf.* יִגְזֹל Ez [18:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek18:7), וַיִּגְזֹל [2 S 23:21](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:21), [1 Ch 11:23](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:23) etc.; *Inf.* גְּזֹל [Is 10:2](https://ref.ly/Isa10:2); *Pt.* act. גּוֺזֵל [Pr 28:24](https://ref.ly/Prov28:24); sf. גֹּזְלוֺ [Psalm 35:10](https://ref.ly/Ps35:10); pl. cstr. גֹּזְלֵי [Mi 3:2](https://ref.ly/Mic3:2); pass. גָּזוּל [Dt 28:29](https://ref.ly/Deut28:29) \+ 4 t.;—*tear away, rob,* c. obj. rei (movable), flock (עֵדֶר) [Jb 24:2](https://ref.ly/Job24:2), ass [Dt 28:31](https://ref.ly/Deut28:31), women Ju [21:23](https://ref.ly/Judg21:23) cf. [Gn 31:31](https://ref.ly/Gen31:31) (E), tear away orphan child from breast [Jb 24:9](https://ref.ly/Job24:9) יתום מִשֹּׁד, snatch a spear from enemy's hand [2 S 23:21](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:21) \= [1 Ch 11:23](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:23); fig. of drying up snow\-water [Jb 24:19](https://ref.ly/Job24:19); (obj. stationary) *take violent possession of*, well of water [Gn 21:25](https://ref.ly/Gen21:25) (E), house [Jb 20:19](https://ref.ly/Job20:19), fields [Mi 2:2](https://ref.ly/Mic2:2); (hyperb.) the skin [Mi 3:2](https://ref.ly/Mic3:2); c. acc. cogn. \= *seize, plunder,* obj. גְּזֵלָה [Lv 5:23](https://ref.ly/Lev5:23) (P) Ez [18:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek18:7), [18:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek18:12), [18:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek18:16), גָּזֵל Ez [18:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek18:18), [22:29](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:29); indef. [Psalm 69:5](https://ref.ly/Ps69:5); fig., obj. מִשְׁפָּט [Is 10:2](https://ref.ly/Isa10:2); c. obj. Pers. robbed [Psalm 35:10](https://ref.ly/Ps35:10), [Pr 22:22](https://ref.ly/Prov22:22), [28:24](https://ref.ly/Prov28:24), prob. also Ju [9:25](https://ref.ly/Judg9:25) cf. [Lv 19:13](https://ref.ly/Lev19:13) (H; abs.); cf. also pass. [Dt 28:29](https://ref.ly/Deut28:29) (‖עָשׁוּק); הציל גָּזוּל מיד עושׁק [Je 21:12](https://ref.ly/Jer21:12) cf. [22:3](https://ref.ly/Jer22:3); [Mal 1:13](https://ref.ly/Mal1:13) גָּזוּל (‖פִּסֵּחַ *lame* and חוֺלֶה *sick*), prob. that rescued after seizure by wild beasts, therefore mutilated. †**Niph.** *Pf.* 3 fs. וְנִגְוְלָה, subj. *sleep* [Pr 4:16](https://ref.ly/Prov4:16) לא (‖יִשְֽׁנוּ).

> \[Str1498,1499] † גָּזֵל **n.\[m.]** robbery—abs. גָּזֵל [Lv 5:21](https://ref.ly/Lev5:21) \+3 t. \+ Ez [18:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek18:18) cf. infr.; cstr. גֵּוֶל [Ec 5:7](https://ref.ly/Eccl5:7), Ez [18:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek18:18) (but cf. infr.);—*robbery* [Lv 5:21](https://ref.ly/Lev5:21) (P) [Psalm 62:11](https://ref.ly/Ps62:11) (‖עשֶׁק) [Is 61:8](https://ref.ly/Isa61:8); \= *thing plundered*, taken as plunder, as plunder, acc. cogn. [Psalm 136:13](https://ref.ly/Ps136:13) sq. לִגְזָרִים. **3\.** *cut down* הָעֵצִים [2 K 6:4](https://ref.ly/2Kgs6:4). **4\.** *cut off* (piece of meat to eat, but obj. not expr. ‖אכל) [Is 9:19](https://ref.ly/Isa9:19). **5\.** *cut off,* i.e. *destroy, exterminate* Hb [3:17](https://ref.ly/Hab3:17) (c. acc.; indef. subj.), sq. מִן loc. **6\.** *decree* (Aramaism, cf. B Aram.) [Jb 22:28](https://ref.ly/Job22:28) c. acc. **Niph.** *Pf.* נִגְזַר [2 Ch. 26:21](https://ref.ly/2Chr26:21), [Est 2:1](https://ref.ly/Esth2:1), נִגְָ\+זרְתִּי [La 3:24](https://ref.ly/Lam3:24), נִגְָ\+זרוּ [Psalm 88:6](https://ref.ly/Ps88:6), נִגְזַרְנוּ Ez [37:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek37:11), [Is 53:8](https://ref.ly/Isa53:8); —**1\.** *be cut off*, separate, excluded from (מִן) temple [2 Ch 26:21](https://ref.ly/2Chr26:21), from (מִן) Yahweh's hand [Psalm 88:5](https://ref.ly/Ps88:5) (of the slain), from (מִן) the land of the living [Is 53:8](https://ref.ly/Isa53:8) (of the suffering servant of ´י). **2\.** *be cut off \= destroyed* [La 3:54](https://ref.ly/Lam3:54), Ez [37:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek37:11). **3\.** *be decreed,* [Est 2:1](https://ref.ly/Esth2:1) sq. עַל *against* (cf. **Qal 6**).

[1 Ch 18:12](https://ref.ly/1Chr18:12), [2 Ch 25:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr25:11) \= ־מֶלַךְ´ג [2 S 8:13](https://ref.ly/2Sam8:13), [Psalm 60:2](https://ref.ly/Ps60:2) (title); S. of Dead Sea, in or bordering on Edom; *el\-Ghôr* RobBR ii. 109\. **d.** הֶחָרָשִׁים ´ג [1 Ch 4:14](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:14), [Ne 11:35](https://ref.ly/Neh11:35), appar. near Lod \& Ono, NW. of Jerus., not far from the sea. **e.** צְפַ֭תַָה לְמָרֵשָׁה ´ג [2 Ch 14:9](https://ref.ly/2Chr14:9) (? rd. צָפֹנַה, LXX κατὰ βορρᾶν, *to the north of M.*) prob. near the great plain of Judah, W. of Jerus., toward the sea; הָעֹבְרִים ´ג Ez [39:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek39:11), E. of Dead Sea (Hi Sm), Co rds. הָעֲבָרִים ´ג **f.** name to be changed to הֲמוֺן־גּוֺג ´ג Ez [39:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek39:11), [39:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek39:15). Most frequently **g.** גֵּי הִנֹּם [Jos 15:8](https://ref.ly/Josh15:8), [18:16](https://ref.ly/Josh18:16), [Ne 11:30](https://ref.ly/Neh11:30), גֵּי בֶן־הִנֹּם [Jos 15:8](https://ref.ly/Josh15:8), [18:16](https://ref.ly/Josh18:16), [2 K 23:10](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:10) Qr (Kt ´גי בני ה) [2 Ch 28:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr28:3), [33:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr33:6), [Je 7:31](https://ref.ly/Jer7:31), [7:32](https://ref.ly/Jer7:32), [19:2](https://ref.ly/Jer19:2), [19:6](https://ref.ly/Jer19:6), [32:35](https://ref.ly/Jer32:35); valley SW. \& S. of Jerus. (RobBR ii. 273 f.), where incense was burned, and children were offered in sacrifice to ‘Molech.’ Others identify with the Tyropæon valley, cf. RS Sem. i. 353; vid. further הִנֹּם, תֹּפֶת.

> \[Str1522] † גֵּיחֲזִי, גֵּחֲזִי **n.pr.m.** (*valley of vision?*) servant of Elisha, גֵּיחֲזִי [2 K 4:12](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:12), [4:14](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:14), [4:25](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:25), [4:27](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:27), [4:29](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:29), [4:36](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:36), [5:20](https://ref.ly/2Kgs5:20), [5:21](https://ref.ly/2Kgs5:21); גֵּחֲזִי [2 K 4:31](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:31), [5:25](https://ref.ly/2Kgs5:25), [8:4](https://ref.ly/2Kgs8:4), [8:5](https://ref.ly/2Kgs8:5).

## גיד (id: BDB01783)

גיד (√ of foll.; mng. dub.; ? cf. As *gâdu, bind, fetter,* JägerBAS i. 589f.)

> \[Str1517] † גִּיד **n.m.**:[Gn 32:33](https://ref.ly/Gen32:33) sinew (NH *id.,* Aram. גִּידָא, ܕܐ; cf. Ar. جِيدٌ *neck*)—cstr. גִּיד [Gn 32:33](https://ref.ly/Gen32:33), [32:33](https://ref.ly/Gen32:33), [Is 48:4](https://ref.ly/Isa48:4); pl. גִּידִים [Jb 10:11](https://ref.ly/Job10:11), Ez [37:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek37:6), גִּדִים Ez [37:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek37:8); cstr. גִּידֵי [Jb 40:17](https://ref.ly/Job40:17); —*sinew,* in general of human body, ‖בשר, עור, עצמות, [Jb 10:11](https://ref.ly/Job10:11) cf. Ez [37:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek37:6), [37:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek37:8) of thigh [Gn 32:33](https://ref.ly/Gen32:32) (of beast) v:[33](https://ref.ly/Gen32:32); iron sinew, fig. of obstinacy, [Is 48:4](https://ref.ly/Isa48:4) בַּרְזֶל עָרְפֶָּךָ וּמִצְחֲךָ נְחוּשָׁה ´וג; of hippopot., פחדו ´ג, i.e. of his loins, [Jb 40:17](https://ref.ly/Job40:17).

## גִּיחַ (id: BDB01785)

\[Str1518] † \[גִּיחַ, גּוּחַ] **vb.** burst forth (cf. NöZMG 1883, 538; NH *id.,* Aram. *id.,* and ܓܳܚ; Eth. ጎሐ፡ *break forth,* of light, etc.)—**Qal** *Impf.* יָגִיחַ [Jb 40:23](https://ref.ly/Job40:23), ms. וַתָּ֫גַח Ez [32:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:2); *Imv.* fs. גֹּ֫חִי [Mi 4:10](https://ref.ly/Mic4:10); *Inf.* sf. גִיחו(בְּ) [Jb 38:8](https://ref.ly/Job38:8); *Pt.* sf. גֹּחִ֫י [Psalm 22:10](https://ref.ly/Ps22:10) (but cf. infr.);—**1\.** intrans. *burst forth,* of dashing river (a very Jordan) [Jb 40:23](https://ref.ly/Job40:23); of sea fig. as babe from womb [Jb 38:8](https://ref.ly/Job38:8), cf. **2\.** trans. **a.** *draw forth* from womb (subj. ´י) [Psalm 22:10](https://ref.ly/Ps22:10) (where rd. גָּחִ֫י pt. fr. גּוּחַ, or regard גֹּחִי as metapl., as if fr. גחךְ…? v. De \& Checrit. note) cf. [Psalm 71:6](https://ref.ly/Ps71:6); also **b.** *thrust forth, bring forth,* fig. of travail, applied to Jerusalem in distress [Mi 4:10](https://ref.ly/Mic4:10) (cf. Sta§ 599 b Köi. 505\); burst forth with rivers (fig. of Pharaoh under image of תַּנִּים) Ez [32:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:2), but for בנהרותיך (*rivers*) rd. prob. בִּנְחִרוֺתֶיךָ [Jb 41:12](https://ref.ly/Job41:12) (*nostrils*), i.e. snort with thy nostrils, so Ew Co; on fig. then cf. [Jb 41:11](https://ref.ly/Job41:11)–[13](https://ref.ly/Job41:13).

161:b. \[גִּיחַ] **2 b**: rather ‘*break forth* (with that which is to be born),’ but text dub. At end, also, add Ju [20:33](https://ref.ly/Judg20:33).

> \[Str1520] † גִּיחַ **n.pr.loc.** (fr. *a spring?*) near Gibeon in Benjamin [2 S 2:24](https://ref.ly/2Sam2:24).

> \[Str1521] † גִּיחוֺן **n.pr.fl.** (*a bursting forth*)—**1\.** one of the rivers of Eden [Gn 2:13](https://ref.ly/Gen2:13) (on theories of identity v. Comm.; also SmithDict. Bible Schaff\-HerzogArt. *Eden* SpurrellText of Gen. *ad loc.* RiHWB). **2\.** spring of water near Jerusalem: גִּחוֺן [1 K 1:33](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:33), [1:38](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:38), [1:45](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:45), מֵימֵי גִיחוֺן הָעֶלְיוֺן [2 Ch 32:30](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:30), לְגִיחוֺן בַּנַּחַל [2 Ch 33:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr33:14); —there are two main theories as to locality: **a.** W. of Jerusalem, connected with *Birket Mamilla,* and aqueduct into city RobBR i. 239, 345 ff. SurveyJerusalem al.; **b.** E. of Jerusalem \= Fountain of the Virgin, FurrerSchenkel BL ii. 483 BDPal 101, or Siloah water\-system GutheZPV, v., 1882, 359 ff.

> \[Str1522] גֵּיחֲזִי v. sub גַּיְא. p. 161

* **Associated Passages:** 2 Chronicles 32:30; 1 Kings 1:33; 2 Chronicles 33:14; Job 40:23; Micah 4:10; Ezekiel 32:2; 1 Kings 1:38; Psalms 71:6; Genesis 2:13; 1 Kings 1:45; Job 41:11; Psalms 22:10; Job 38:8; 2 Samuel 2:24; Job 41:12; Job 41:13

## גָּלָה (id: BDB01808)

\[Str1540] גָּלָה:189 **vb. uncover, remove** (NH *id.,* cf. Ar. جَلَا *be* or *become clear, uncovered; display, reveal, declare; go forth, emigrate;* cf. Eth ገለወ፡ *obducere, velare,* \& II. ገለየ፡ in deriv. (rare) DI1141; Aram. גְּלָא, ܓܠܴܐ *reveal*)—**Qal**:51 *Pf.* ´ג etc. [1 S 4:21](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:21) \+ (18 t. in all); *Impf.* יִגְלֶה [1 S 20:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:2) \+ 5 t., וַיִּ֫גֶֽל [2 K 17:23](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:23) \+ 3 t., also juss. יִ֫גֶל [Jb 20:28](https://ref.ly/Job20:28), [36:15](https://ref.ly/Job36:15); 3 mpl. יִגְלוּ [Am 6:7](https://ref.ly/Amos6:7); *Imv.* ms. גְּלֵה Ez [12:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek12:3); *Inf. abs.* גָּלהֹ [Am 5:5](https://ref.ly/Amos5:5) \+ 2 t., *cstr.* גְּלוֺת Ju [18:30](https://ref.ly/Judg18:30), [Je 1:3](https://ref.ly/Jer1:3); *Pt.* גּוֺלֶה [2 K 24:14](https://ref.ly/2Kgs24:14), גֹּלֶה [1 S 22:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:8) \+ 3 t., f. גּוֺלָה [Is 49:21](https://ref.ly/Isa49:21); *pass.* גָּלוּי [Est 3:14](https://ref.ly/Esth3:14) \+ 2 t., cstr. גְּלוּי [Nu 24:4](https://ref.ly/Num24:4), [24:16](https://ref.ly/Num24:16); —**1\.** גָּלָה אֹזֶן *uncover the ear* of one, i.e. *reveal* to him [1 S 9:15](https://ref.ly/1Sam9:15), [20:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:2), [20:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:12), [20:13](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:13), [22:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:8), [22:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:8), [22:17](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:17), [2 S 7:27](https://ref.ly/2Sam7:27), [1 Ch 17:25](https://ref.ly/1Chr17:25), [Ru 4:4](https://ref.ly/Ruth4:4), [Jb 33:16](https://ref.ly/Job33:16), [36:10](https://ref.ly/Job36:10), [36:15](https://ref.ly/Job36:15); גְּלוּי עינים *uncovered of eyes,* having the eyes open [Nu 24:4](https://ref.ly/Num24:4), [24:16](https://ref.ly/Num24:16); גָּלָה סוֺד *reveal a secret* [Am 3:7](https://ref.ly/Amos3:7), [Pr 20:19](https://ref.ly/Prov20:19); הגלוי *the revealed* opp. הֶחָתוּם [Je 32:11](https://ref.ly/Jer32:11); גָּלוּי לְ *disclosed, published* [Est 3:14](https://ref.ly/Esth3:14), [8:13](https://ref.ly/Esth8:13). **2\.** intr. *remove, depart*, גלה משוש הארץ *the mirth of the land is departed* [Is 24:11](https://ref.ly/Isa24:11); יגל יבול ביתו *the increase of his house shall depart* [Jb 20:28](https://ref.ly/Job20:28); גלה חציר [Pr 27:25](https://ref.ly/Prov27:25). **3\.** *go into exile* Ju [18:30](https://ref.ly/Judg18:30), [2 K 17:23](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:23), [25:21](https://ref.ly/2Kgs25:21), [Is 5:13](https://ref.ly/Isa5:13), [Je 1:3](https://ref.ly/Jer1:3), [52:27](https://ref.ly/Jer52:27), Ez [12:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek12:3), [39:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek39:23), [Am 1:5](https://ref.ly/Amos1:5), [5:5](https://ref.ly/Amos5:5), [6:7](https://ref.ly/Amos6:7), [7:11](https://ref.ly/Amos7:11), [7:17](https://ref.ly/Amos7:17), [Mi 1:16](https://ref.ly/Mic1:16), [La 1:3](https://ref.ly/Lam1:3); גלה כבוד [1 S 4:21](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:21), [4:22](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:22), [Ho 10:5](https://ref.ly/Hos10:5); pt. גֹּלָה *an exile* [2 S 15:19](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:19), [2 K 24:14](https://ref.ly/2Kgs24:14), [Is 49:21](https://ref.ly/Isa49:21), [Am 6:7](https://ref.ly/Amos6:7). **Niph.** *Pf.* נִגְלָה [1 S 3:21](https://ref.ly/1Sam3:21) \+ 9 t., נִגְלָתָה [Is 53:1](https://ref.ly/Isa53:1), נִגְלֵתִי [1 S 2:27](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:27), נִגְלוּ[Gn 35:7](https://ref.ly/Gen35:7) \+ 2 t., נִגְלִינוּ [1 S 14:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:8); *Impf.* יִגָּלֶה, תִּגָּלֶה [1 S 3:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam3:7) \+ 4 t., תִּגָּל [Is 47:3](https://ref.ly/Isa47:3), יִגָּלוּ [2 S 22:16](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:16) \+ 2 t.; *Imv.* הִגָּלוּ [Is 49:9](https://ref.ly/Isa49:9); *Inf.* abs. נִגְלֽה[1 S 2:27](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:27); cstr. נִגְלוֺת [2 S 6:20](https://ref.ly/2Sam6:20), הִגָּלוֺת [2 S 6:20](https://ref.ly/2Sam6:20) \+ 2 t.; *Pt.* pl. נִגְלֽת [Dt 29:28](https://ref.ly/Deut29:28); —**1\.** refl. **a.** *uncover oneself* (one's nakedness) [2 S 6:20](https://ref.ly/2Sam6:20). **b.** *discover* or *shew oneself* [Is 49:9](https://ref.ly/Isa49:9), אֶל [1 S 14:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:8), [14:11](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:11). **c.** *reveal himself* (of God), אֶל [Gn 35:7](https://ref.ly/Gen35:7) (E) [1 S 2:27](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:27) [3:21](https://ref.ly/1Sam3:21), באזני [Is 22:14](https://ref.ly/Isa22:14). **2\.** pass. **a.** *be uncovered* (one's nakedness), ערוה [Ex 20:26](https://ref.ly/Exod20:26) (E) [Is 47:3](https://ref.ly/Isa47:3), Ez [16:36](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:36), [23:29](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:29), שׁולים [Je 13:22](https://ref.ly/Jer13:22). **b.** *be disclosed, discovered,* foundations [2 S 22:16](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:16) (\= [Psalm 18:16](https://ref.ly/Ps18:16)) Ez [13:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:14); gates of death [Jb 38:17](https://ref.ly/Job38:17), עון[Ho 7:1](https://ref.ly/Hos7:1), פשׁע Ez [21:29](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:29), רעה [Pr 26:26](https://ref.ly/Prov26:26), Ez [16:57](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:57). **c.** *be revealed* ´כבוד י [Is 40:5](https://ref.ly/Isa40:5), ´זרוע י [Is 53:1](https://ref.ly/Isa53:1), ´דבר י [1 S 3:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam3:7), צדקה [Is 56:1](https://ref.ly/Isa56:1); with לְ [Is 23:1](https://ref.ly/Isa23:1), [Dn 10:1](https://ref.ly/Dan10:1), הַנִּגְלֽת *the things revealed* [Dt 29:28](https://ref.ly/Deut29:28). **3\.** *be removed*, דּוֺרִי נִסַּע וְנִגְלָה מִנִּי *my habitation is plucked up and removed from me* [Is 38:12](https://ref.ly/Isa38:12). **Pi.**:56 *Pf.* גִּלָּה[Lv 20:11](https://ref.ly/Lev20:11), גִּלִּית [Is 57:8](https://ref.ly/Isa57:8), גִּלֵּיתִי[Je 33:6](https://ref.ly/Jer33:6); *Impf.* יְגַלָּה [Dt 23:1](https://ref.ly/Deut23:1), תְּגַל [Pr 25:9](https://ref.ly/Prov25:9); *Imv.* גַּל [Psalm 119:18](https://ref.ly/Ps119:18), גַּלִּי [Is 47:2](https://ref.ly/Isa47:2); *Inf.* גַּלּוֺת [Lv 18:6](https://ref.ly/Lev18:6); *Pt.* מְגַלֶּה [Jb 12:22](https://ref.ly/Job12:22); —**1\.** *uncover*, **a.** *nakedness* (oft \= *contract marriage,* RSLagGN 1882, 408; JPh ix. 94\) [Lv 18:6](https://ref.ly/Lev18:6)–[19](https://ref.ly/Lev18:19) [20:11](https://ref.ly/Lev20:11)–[21](https://ref.ly/Lev20:21) (H 23 t.) [22:8](https://ref.ly/Isa22:8), פני לבושׁו [Jb 41:5](https://ref.ly/Job41:5), eyes [Nu 22:31](https://ref.ly/Num22:31) (E) [Psalm 119:18](https://ref.ly/Ps119:18) (open them so as to see). **2\.** *disclose, discover, lay bare,* secret places [Je 49:10](https://ref.ly/Jer49:10), deep places [Jb 12:22](https://ref.ly/Job12:22), foundations [Mi 1:6](https://ref.ly/Mic1:6), blood [Is 26:21](https://ref.ly/Isa26:21), iniquity [Jb 20:27](https://ref.ly/Job20:27), secret [Pr 11:13](https://ref.ly/Prov11:13), [25:9](https://ref.ly/Prov25:9), a wanderer (betray) [Is 16:3](https://ref.ly/Isa16:3). **3\.** *make known, shew, reveal,* רִיב אֶל *make known a cause unto* [Je 11:20](https://ref.ly/Jer11:20), [20:12](https://ref.ly/Jer20:12); with לְ [Jb 33:6](https://ref.ly/Job33:6); גִּלָּה צִדְקָתוֺ לעיני *shew his righteousness in the eyes of* [Psalm 98:2](https://ref.ly/Ps98:2); with עָל:על עון, על חטאת *make known concerning,* iniquity, sin [La 2:14](https://ref.ly/Lam2:14), [4:22](https://ref.ly/Lam4:22). **Pu.** *Pf.* גֻּלְּתָה *be uncovered* [Na 2:8](https://ref.ly/Nah2:8); *Pt.* תּוֺכַחַת מְגֻלָּה *open rebuke* [Pr 27:5](https://ref.ly/Prov27:5). **Hiph.**:39 *Pf.* הֶגְלָה [2 K 17:11](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:11) \+, הִגְלָה [2 K 24:14](https://ref.ly/2Kgs24:14), הֶגְלָם [1 Ch 8:7](https://ref.ly/1Chr8:7), הִגְלָם [Je 20:4](https://ref.ly/Jer20:4); ## גָּלַח (id: BDB01816)

\[Str1548] † \[גָּלַח] **vb.** be bald (Ar. جَلِحَ); **Pi.** shave, shave off (so NH, Aram.)—*Pf.* וְגִלַּח consec. [Lv 14:8](https://ref.ly/Lev14:8) \+ 2 t.; sf. וְגִלְּחוֺ consec. [2 S 14:26](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:26); 3 fs. וְגִלְּחָה consec. [Dt 21:12](https://ref.ly/Deut21:12); *Impf.* יְגַלַּח [Lv 14:9](https://ref.ly/Lev14:9), [Is 7:20](https://ref.ly/Isa7:20), יְגַָלֵּחַ [Lv 13:33](https://ref.ly/Lev13:33) \+ 2 t., וַיְגַלַּח [Gn 41:14](https://ref.ly/Gen41:14), [2 S 10:4](https://ref.ly/2Sam10:4); sf. (3 ms.) יְגַלְּחֶגּוּ, וַיְגַלְּחֵם[1 Ch 19:4](https://ref.ly/1Chr19:4); 3 fs. וַתְּנַלַּךְ Ju [16:19](https://ref.ly/Judg16:19); 3 mpl. יְגַלֵָּחוּ [Lv 21:5](https://ref.ly/Lev21:5), Ez [44:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek44:20); *Inf.* sf. of subj. גַּלְּחוֺ [2 S 14:26](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:26); —**1\.** *shave,* obj. the head, ראֹשׁ [Nu 6:9](https://ref.ly/Num6:9), [6:9](https://ref.ly/Num6:9), [6:18](https://ref.ly/Num6:18) (P) [Dt 21:12](https://ref.ly/Deut21:12) (hair as containing impurity, cf. RS Sem i. 407\), [2 S 14:26](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:26), [14:26](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:26) cf.; [14:26](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:26), Ez [44:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek44:20); [Lv 13:33](https://ref.ly/Lev13:33) (P), obj. הַנֶתֶק; obj. persons [1 Ch 19:4](https://ref.ly/1Chr19:4). **2\.** *shave off*, the hair שֵעָר [Lv 14:8](https://ref.ly/Lev14:8), [14:9](https://ref.ly/Lev14:9), [14:9](https://ref.ly/Lev14:9) (P) cf. Ju [16:19](https://ref.ly/Judg16:19); the beard זָקָן [Lv 21:5](https://ref.ly/Lev21:5) (H) [2 S 10:4](https://ref.ly/2Sam10:4). **3\.** fig. of devastation by Assyrians [Is 7:20](https://ref.ly/Isa7:20) (obj. הָראֹשׁ, שַעַר הָרַגְלָיִם and הַזָּקָן). **4\.** intrans. *he shaved* (himself) [Gn 41:14](https://ref.ly/Gen41:14) (E). **Pu.** *Pf.* גֻּלָּח Ju [16:22](https://ref.ly/Judg16:22), גֻּלַּחְתִּי Ju [16:17](https://ref.ly/Judg16:17); *Pt.* cstr. מְגֻלְּחֵי [Je 41:5](https://ref.ly/Jer41:5) ;—*be shaven,* subj. Pers. (of Samson) Ju [16:17](https://ref.ly/Judg16:17), [16:22](https://ref.ly/Judg16:22); מְגֻלְּחֵי זָקָן [Je 41:5](https://ref.ly/Jer41:5). **Hithp.** *Pf.* וְהִתְגַּלָּח consec. [Lv 13:33](https://ref.ly/Lev13:33); *Inf.* sf. הִתְגַּלְּחוֺ [Nu 6:19](https://ref.ly/Num6:19); —*shave oneself* [Lv 13:33](https://ref.ly/Lev13:33); c. acc. אֶת־נִזְרוֺ [Nu 6:19](https://ref.ly/Num6:19) (both P).

## I גלל (id: BDB01817)

I. גלל (جَلَّ *be great in rank* or *dignity* (often of God)).

> \[Str1558] † I. \[גָּלָל] **n.\[m.]** account, only in cstr. st. with בְּ, בִּגְלַל *on account of, for the sake of* (جَلَلٌ *a great and momentous matter;* مِنْ جَلَلِكَ on thy *account;* cf. מִן בִּגְלַל דְּ *because that* in Palestinian Targum, as [Psalm 4:9](https://ref.ly/Ps4:9), and ܓܠܰܠ in Jerus. Syriac PS731) [Gn 39:5](https://ref.ly/Gen39:5) ´י blessed the house of the Egyptian בִּגְלַל יוֺסֵף *on account of* Joseph, [Dt 15:10](https://ref.ly/Deut15:10), [18:12](https://ref.ly/Deut18:12), [1 K 14:16](https://ref.ly/1Kgs14:16), [Je 11:17](https://ref.ly/Jer11:17), [15:4](https://ref.ly/Jer15:4); with sf. בִּגְלָלֶָךָ [Gn 30:27](https://ref.ly/Gen30:27); בִּגְלָלֵח [12:13](https://ref.ly/Gen12:13); בִּגְלַלְכֶם [Dt 1:37](https://ref.ly/Deut1:37), [Mi 3:12](https://ref.ly/Mic3:12).

* **Associated Passages:** Genesis 39:5; Jeremiah 11:17; Deuteronomy 18:12; Deuteronomy 15:10; Deuteronomy 1:37; Psalms 4:9; Genesis 12:13; Jeremiah 15:4; 1 Kings 14:16; Genesis 30:27; Micah 3:12


## II גָּלַל (id: BDB01819)

\[Str1556] † II. \[גָּלַל] **vb.** roll, roll away (NH *id.,* \& deriv.; Aram. Pa. גַּלֵּל, Palp. גַּלְגֵּיל *roll*, Ithpalp. reflex., \& deriv.; cf. ܓܰܠ Ethp., Ethpalp. (v. PS), ܓܺܓܰܠ *rota,* ܓܰܠܠܴܐ *fluctus,* etc.)—**Qal** *Pf.* 1 s. גַּלּוֺתִי [Jos 5:9](https://ref.ly/Josh5:9), וְגָֽלֲלוּ consec. [Gn 29:3](https://ref.ly/Gen29:3), [29:8](https://ref.ly/Gen29:8); *Imv.* גֹּל [Psalm 22:9](https://ref.ly/Ps22:9) (MT De; but LXX BI Che rd. גַּל 3 ms. pf., so AV RVm) [Pr 16:3](https://ref.ly/Prov16:3), גּוֺל [Psalm 37:5](https://ref.ly/Ps37:5), גַּל [119:22](https://ref.ly/Ps119:22) (but rd. גֹּל Ew Hi Ri Gr Che), גֹּלּוּ [Jos 10:18](https://ref.ly/Josh10:18), [1 S 14:33](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:33); *Pt.* גֹּלֵל [Pr 26:27](https://ref.ly/Prov26:27); —*roll* a stone [Pr 26:27](https://ref.ly/Prov26:27); *roll away* stone from upon (מֵעַל) the mouth of a well [Gn 29:3](https://ref.ly/Gen29:3), [29:8](https://ref.ly/Gen29:8); *roll* stones unto (אֶל) the mouth of a cave [Jos 10:18](https://ref.ly/Josh10:18); stone unto (אֶל) Saul, at which to slay beasts [1 S 14:33](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:33); fig. reproach from upon (מֵעַל) the people [Jos 5:9](https://ref.ly/Josh5:9) (subj. ´י), cf. [Psalm 119:22](https://ref.ly/Ps119:22) (sq. מֵעַל); but also דַּרְכֶָּךָ ´גּוֺל עַל־י [Psalm 37:5](https://ref.ly/Ps37:5), cf. [22:9](https://ref.ly/Ps22:9), [Pr 16:3](https://ref.ly/Prov16:3) (both sq. אֶ־ל). **Niph.** *Pf.* וְנָגֹלּוּ consec. [Is 34:4](https://ref.ly/Isa34:4); *Impf.* וְיִגַּל (juss.) [Am 5:24](https://ref.ly/Amos5:24); —*roll, roll up* or *along* (intrans.), וְנָגֹלּוּ כַסֵּפֶר הַשָּׁמַיִם [Is 34:4](https://ref.ly/Isa34:4) *and the heavens shall roll up like a book;* יִגַּל כַּמַּיִם מִשְׁפָּט [Am 5:24](https://ref.ly/Amos5:24) *let judgement roll along* (flow down) *as the waters.* **Pilp.** *Pf.* וְגִלְגַּלְתִּיךָ consec. [Je 51:25](https://ref.ly/Jer51:25) *and I will roll thee down from* (מִן) the rocks (subj. ´י; obj. Babylon under fig. of mt.) **Pō‘al** *Pt.* שִמְלָה מְגוֺלָלָה בְדָמִים [Is 9:4](https://ref.ly/Isa9:4) *garment rolled* (dabbled, rolled over and over) *in blood.* **Hithpo.** *Inf.* לְהִתְגֹּלֵל [Gn 43:8](https://ref.ly/Gen43:8); *Pt.* מִתְגֹּלֵל [2 S 20:12](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:12); *rol oneself over and over,* בַּדָּם ´וַעֲמָשָא מִתְג [2 S 20:12](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:12) *now Amasa was wallowing in his blood;* fig. [Gn 43:18](https://ref.ly/Gen43:18) עָלֵינוּ ´לְהִתְג *to roll himself upon us,* i.e. assail us with overwhelming force (‖וּלְהִתְנַפֵּל עָלֵינוּ). **Hithpalp.** *Pf.* הִתְגַּלְָ\+גּלוּ [Jb 30:14](https://ref.ly/Job30:14) (abs.; ‖כְּפֶרֶץ רָחָב יֶאֱתָיוּ) cf. Hithpo. [Gn 43:8](https://ref.ly/Gen43:8). **Hiph.** *Impf.* וַיָּ֫גֶל [Gn 29:10](https://ref.ly/Gen29:10); —*roll away* stone from upon (מֵעַל) mouth of well; as **Qal** (which it really is, Impf. in *i*, acc. to BaZMG 1888, 178\).

> \[Str1530] † גַּל **n.m.**:[Gn 31:48](https://ref.ly/Gen31:48) heap, wave, billow (as *rolled together, rolling, rolling up*), also **spring**, MT Ct [4:12](https://ref.ly/Song4:12) cf. **3** infr.—abs. גַּל [Jb 8:17](https://ref.ly/Job8:17) \+ 5 t. (incl. הַגַּל [Gn 31:48](https://ref.ly/Gen31:48) \+); ָ\+גּל [Gn 31:46](https://ref.ly/Gen31:46); הַָ\+גּל [Gn 31:46](https://ref.ly/Gen31:46); לַָ\+גּל [Is. 25:2](https://ref.ly/Isa25:2); cstr. גַּ־ל [Jos 7:26](https://ref.ly/Josh7:26) \+ 2t. \+ [Gn 31:47](https://ref.ly/Gen31:47), [31:48](https://ref.ly/Gen31:48) q.v. infr.; pl. גַּלִּים [2 K 19:25](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:25) \+ 6 t.; cstr. גַּלֵּ־י [Is 48:18](https://ref.ly/Isa48:18); sf. גַּלָּיו [Psalm 89:10](https://ref.ly/Ps89:10) \+ 6 t.; גַּלֵּיהֶם [Psalm 65:8](https://ref.ly/Ps65:8) \+ 2 t.;—**1\.** *heap* of stones, **a.** אבנים ´ג raised (הקים) over dead body [Jos 7:26](https://ref.ly/Josh7:26), [8:29](https://ref.ly/Josh8:29); cf. [2 S 18:17](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:17) (c. הִצִּיב). **b.** גַּל alone [Jb 8:17](https://ref.ly/Job8:17) (roots wrapped about it). **c.** *heap* or *pile* made (עשה) for use in ratifying compact of Jacob with Laban [Gn 31:46](https://ref.ly/Gen31:46), [31:46](https://ref.ly/Gen31:46), [31:48](https://ref.ly/Gen31:48), [31:51](https://ref.ly/Gen31:51), [38:52](https://ref.ly/Gen38:52), [31:52](https://ref.ly/Gen31:52), [31:52](https://ref.ly/Gen31:52) cf. also גַּלְעֵד **n.pr. d.** *heap* of ruins [Is 25:2](https://ref.ly/Isa25:2); elsewhere pl. [Ho 12:12](https://ref.ly/Hos12:12), [2 K 19:25](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:25) \= [Is 37:26](https://ref.ly/Isa37:26), [Jb 15:28](https://ref.ly/Job15:28), [Jer. 9:10](https://ref.ly/Jer9:10), [51:37](https://ref.ly/Jer51:37). **2\.** *waves (rollers)* poet., only pl., waves of sea [Je 5:22](https://ref.ly/Jer5:22), [31:35](https://ref.ly/Jer31:35), [Is 51:15](https://ref.ly/Isa51:15), [Jb 38:11](https://ref.ly/Job38:11), [Psalm 65:8](https://ref.ly/Ps65:8), [89:10](https://ref.ly/Ps89:10), [107:25](https://ref.ly/Ps107:25), [107:29](https://ref.ly/Ps107:29); cf. also in simile Ez [26:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek26:3), [Is 48:18](https://ref.ly/Isa48:18) (גַּלֵּי־הַיָּם); fig. of chastisements from ´י [Psalm 42:8](https://ref.ly/Ps42:8) (‖מִשְׁבָּרִים) [Jon 2:3](https://ref.ly/Jonah2:3) (‖ id.); of army of Babylon's conqueror [Je 51:42](https://ref.ly/Jer51:42), [51:55](https://ref.ly/Jer51:55); cf. [Zc 10:11](https://ref.ly/Zech10:11) (VB; but BevJPh xviii. 88 prop. גִּבְלִים cf. Ez [27:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:9)). **3\.** *spring,* Ct [4:12](https://ref.ly/Song4:12) גַּן נָעוּל אֲחוֺתִי כַּלָּה גַּל נָעוּל מַעְיָן חָתוּם *a garden barred (is) my sister, bride, a spring barred, a fountain sealed;* but גַּן for גַּל LXX SyrVer Vulgate.

> \[Str1554] † גַּלִּים **n.pr.loc.** (*heaps*);—place N. of Jerus. [1 S 25:44](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:44); personified as ´בַּת־גִ [Is 10:30](https://ref.ly/Isa10:30).

> \[Str1567] † גַּלְעֵד **n.pr.loc.** (*witness\-pile*) name of pile of stones erected by Jacob and his company (KitGesch. 129; by Laban We Di) [Gn 31:47](https://ref.ly/Gen31:47), [31:48](https://ref.ly/Gen31:48) (v. גַּל **1 c**) appar. intended to explain גִּלְעָד q.v.; Di conj. also (as name of altar) [Jos 22:34](https://ref.ly/Josh22:34) (cf. > \[Str1561] † \[גֵּל] **n.\[m.]** dung (*ball* of dung, Ar. جِلَّةٌ etc., dung of camels etc., cf. Aram. גַּלָּא; on form v. Ew§ 255 b Ol:§ 150\. 163\)—sf. גֶּלֲלוֺ [Jb 20:7](https://ref.ly/Job20:7), pl. cstr. הָאָדָם (צֵאַת) גֶּלֲלֵי Ez [4:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek4:12), [4:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek4:15); —human dung used as fuel Ez [4:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek4:12), [4:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek4:15) הָאָדָם (צֵאַת) ´ג; perh. also [Jb 20:7](https://ref.ly/Job20:7), but cf. Di, who thinks no ref. to fuel, \& De who thinks of cattle\-dung; (v. on Ar. word Wetzst in De).—See also II. גָּלָל.

## גָּלַם (id: BDB01839)

\[Str1563] † \[גָּלַם] **vb.** wrap up, fold, fold together (NH *id.,* Aram. in deriv.)—**Qal** *Impf.* וַיִּגְלֽם [2 K 2:8](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:8) וַיִּקַּח אֵלִיָּהוּ אֶת־אַדַּרְתּוֺ וַיִּגְלֽם.

> \[Str1545] † \[גְּלוֺם] **n. \[m.]** wrapping, garment (Aram. גְּלִימָא, ܓܠܝܻܡܳܐ \= Pers. گليم acc. to Fl Ch WBi. 143 SFräLOPh. i. (1883–4\) 410\), בִּגְלוֺמֵי תְּכֵלֶת Ez [27:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:24).

> \[Str1564] † \[גֹּ֫לֶם] **n.\[m.]** embryo (NH גּוֺלֶךָ, Aram. גּוֺלְמָא, *unfinished vessel*), גָּלְמִי רָאוּ עֵינֶיךָ [Psalm 139:16](https://ref.ly/Ps139:16) *mine imperfect substance* (RV) *did thine eyes see.*

> \[Str1565] † גַּלְמוּד **adj.** hard, barren (NH *id. lonely* NHWB; Ar. جَلْمَدٌ *rock,* جَلْمَدَةٌ *stony* (of land); Aram. גַּלְמוּדָה cf. BaNB 208\)—´ג [Jb 3:7](https://ref.ly/Job3:7) \+ 2 t. Jb; f. גַּלְמוּדָה [Is 49:21](https://ref.ly/Isa49:21); —*hard, barren, unproductive,* mostly fig.: [Jb 15:34](https://ref.ly/Job15:34) of company of wicked men, where prob. a subst. v. De Di; [Jb 30:3](https://ref.ly/Job30:3) *through want and famine* (they are) *stiff (lifeless;* RV *gaunt);* of exiled Zion as bereaved and barren woman [Is 49:21](https://ref.ly/Isa49:21); so of night of Job's birth [Jb 3:7](https://ref.ly/Job3:7).

* **Associated Passages:** Job 30:3; 2 Kings 2:8; Job 15:34; Job 3:7; Ezekiel 27:24; Psalms 139:16; Isaiah 49:21


## גָּלַע (id: BDB01843)

\[Str1566] † \[גָּלַע] **vb.** expose, lay bare (NH Pi. *disclose, make known;* cf. Ar. جَلَعَ (Frey) **a.** *exuit* (vestem), *removit* (praeputium); جُلُوعٌ *nuda fuit* (femina); **b.** جَلِعَ *patuit* (os) *labiis non tegentibus dentes; impudica fuit* (mulier); comp. also Syr. ܓܠܰܥ *circumcise* (in Lexx.); Eth. ገልዕ፡ *cortex, crusta, testa;* GrMonatsschr. 1884, 24 f.; Schult Thes De [Pr 17:14](https://ref.ly/Prov17:14) Fl in De, Now Str \& most der. Heb. mng. from Ar. **b;** *shew the teeth,* then *snarl, quarrel* (cf. Ar. conj. III), but v. Grl. c.)—**Hithp.** *Pf.* הִתְגַּלַּע [Pr 17:14](https://ref.ly/Prov17:14); *Impf.* יִתְגַּלָּע [Pr 18:1](https://ref.ly/Prov18:1), [20:3](https://ref.ly/Prov20:3); —disclose oneself, break out, [Pr 17:14](https://ref.ly/Prov17:14) subj. רִיב; *break* or *burst out* in contention, strife [Pr. 20:3](https://ref.ly/Prov20:3) subj. כָּל־אֱוִיל; similarly [18:1](https://ref.ly/Prov18:1) (sq. בְּ *against;* Grl. c. prop. יִלְעַג or יַלְעִיג).

> \[Str1567] גַּלְעֵד v. sub II. גלל. p. 165

> \[Str1568] † גִּלְעָד **n.pr.loc.** of mountain\-range or hill\-country, land \& city, E. of Jordan (cf. Ar. جَلْعَدٌ *durus, fortis* Frey, so Thes; HiGesch. Isr. 26 comp. same √ used of *camel* (جَلْعَادٌ *camelus robustus ac firmus,* cf. HomNS 144\), whence *camel hump,* orig. name of mt.)—´ג [Gn 37:25](https://ref.ly/Gen37:25) \+ 79 t. (mostly as **n.pr.m.** and in combin. with other **n.pr.loc.** cf. infr.); ´הַגּ [Gn 31:21](https://ref.ly/Gen31:21) \+ 50 t. (so usually as name of mt. and land); גִּלְעָ֫דָה [Nu 32:39](https://ref.ly/Num32:39), [1 Ch 27:21](https://ref.ly/1Chr27:21); הִגְּלְעָ֫דָה [2 S 24:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:6); —*Gilead,* used of territory between the Arnon and the Jabboḳ, mod. *Belḳa;* also of that between the Jabboḳ and the Jarmuk, mod. *Jebel Ajlûn;* also of the entire E. Jordan land occupied by Israel, including both the parts just named (cf. Di [Gn 31:54](https://ref.ly/Gen31:54) Stu Ju [10:4](https://ref.ly/Judg10:4); BDPal 178; also L. OliphantLand of Gilead, 1880\);—**1\.** territory S. of Jabboḳ, **a.** ´אֶרֶץ הַגּ [Nu 32:29](https://ref.ly/Num32:29) (P), ´אֶרֶץ ג v:[1](https://ref.ly/Num32:1) (JE), both connected with Reuben and Gad; so ´חֲצִי הַר הַגּ [Dt 3:12](https://ref.ly/Deut3:12); ´אֶרֶץ ג also [1 Ch 5:9](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:9) (of Reuben); [1 K 4:19](https://ref.ly/1Kgs4:19) (of Sihon and Og); cf. ´אֶרֶץ גַּד וְג [1 S 13:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam13:7); further, **b.** ´הַגּ alone, Ju [10:17](https://ref.ly/Judg10:17) (or possibly here name of city ?), [11:29](https://ref.ly/Judg11:29) (prob.); also [1 Ch 5:10](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:10) (of Reuben) v:[16](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:16) (of Gad); cf. חֲצִי וְעַד יַבֹּק ´הַגּ [Jos 12:2](https://ref.ly/Josh12:2) (of Sihon), so v:[5](https://ref.ly/Josh12:5) (both D); **c.** similarly ´ג alone (without art.) in poet. [Psalm 60:9](https://ref.ly/Ps60:9) (‖ Manasseh) \= [108:9](https://ref.ly/Ps108:9); but also in prose in phrases ´שָרֵי ג Ju [10:18](https://ref.ly/Judg10:18), ´ישְׁבֵי ג v:[18](https://ref.ly/Judg10:18), [11:8](https://ref.ly/Judg11:8), ´זִקְנֵי ג Ju [11:5](https://ref.ly/Judg11:5), [11:7](https://ref.ly/Judg11:7), [11:8](https://ref.ly/Judg11:8), [11:9](https://ref.ly/Judg11:9), [11:10](https://ref.ly/Judg11:10), [11:11](https://ref.ly/Judg11:11), ´אַנְשֵׁי ג Ju [12:4](https://ref.ly/Judg12:4), [12:4](https://ref.ly/Judg12:4), [12:5](https://ref.ly/Judg12:5); of Gad); besides these, or particular cities, esp. ´רָאמֹת בַּגּ [Dt 4:43](https://ref.ly/Deut4:43) (of Gad); cf. ´רָאמוֺת בַּגּ [Jos 20:8](https://ref.ly/Josh20:8) (P), [1 Ch 6:65](https://ref.ly/1Chr6:65) (of Gad), and ´רָמֹת בַּגּ [Jos 21:38](https://ref.ly/Josh21:38) (P); Gilead,* appar. belongs here, but מֵהַר הַגִּלְבֹּעַ is read by Cler Hi Gr Ke Be; against this cf. Stu BuRS 112\. **3\.** more generally, of all Gilead, **a.** ´אֶרֶץ הַגּ [Jos 22:9](https://ref.ly/Josh22:9), [22:13](https://ref.ly/Josh22:13), [22:15](https://ref.ly/Josh22:15), [22:32](https://ref.ly/Josh22:32) (P; of Reuben, Gad and Manasseh; opp. land of Canaan); cf. [2 S 17:26](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:26); so ´כָּל־אֶרֶץ הַגּ [2 K 10:38](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:38). **b.** ´אֶרֶץ ג (no art.) [Zc 10:10](https://ref.ly/Zech10:10); cf. ´הַר אֶפְרַיִם וְג [Je 50:19](https://ref.ly/Jer50:19) (‖ Carmel and Bashan); ´ג alone, also [Gn 37:25](https://ref.ly/Gen37:25); likewise [Mi 7:14](https://ref.ly/Mic7:14) (‖ Carmel \& Bashan, reference to fertility). **c.** ´הַגּ alone, of Isr. territ. in gen. E. of Jordan, Ju [10:8](https://ref.ly/Judg10:8); cf. [2 S 2:9](https://ref.ly/2Sam2:9), [2 K 10:33](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:33) (of Reuben, Gad and Manasseh); ´כָּל־הַגּ [Dt 3:10](https://ref.ly/Deut3:10); אֶת־כָּל־הָאָרֶץ ´אֶת־הַגּ [Dt 34:1](https://ref.ly/Deut34:1); ´הַגּ also in general sense Ez [47:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:18), [2 K 15:29](https://ref.ly/2Kgs15:29) (?) Ob:[19](https://ref.ly/Obad1:19). **4\.** ´ג, name of a city [Ho 6:8](https://ref.ly/Hos6:8) (\& Ju [10:17](https://ref.ly/Judg10:17) ? cf. **1\. a.** supr.);—on the ruined cities *Jil‘âd, Jil‘aud* on the *Jebel Jil‘âd,* N. of Es\-Salṭ, and S. of Jabboḳ v. Di [Gn 31:54](https://ref.ly/Gen31:54) \& reff. **5\.** ´אֶרֶץ הַגּ of Israelites living E. of Jordan (as a whole) Ju [20:1](https://ref.ly/Judg20:1); ´הַגּ of people of Gilead [Am 1:3](https://ref.ly/Amos1:3), [1:13](https://ref.ly/Amos1:13); so ´ג Ju [5:17](https://ref.ly/Judg5:17) (\= גַּד), [12:4](https://ref.ly/Judg12:4), [12:5](https://ref.ly/Judg12:5) (‖´אַנְשֵׁי ג v:[4](https://ref.ly/Judg12:4), v:[4](https://ref.ly/Judg12:4), v:[5](https://ref.ly/Judg12:5)) [Ho 12:12](https://ref.ly/Hos12:12). Cf. also גִּלְעָדִי infr. **6\.** treated as **n.pr.m. a.** ´ג, son of Machir \& grandson of Manasseh [Nu 26:29](https://ref.ly/Num26:29), [26:29](https://ref.ly/Num26:29), [26:30](https://ref.ly/Num26:30), [27:1](https://ref.ly/Num27:1), [36:1](https://ref.ly/Num36:1), [Jos 17:3](https://ref.ly/Josh17:3) (all P) [1 Ch 2:21](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:21), [2:23](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:23), [7:14](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:14), [7:17](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:17); once ´הַגּ [Jos 17:1](https://ref.ly/Josh17:1) (JE) cf. Di;—ref. in all these to Northern Gilead. **b.** father of Jephthah Ju [11:1](https://ref.ly/Judg11:1), [11:2](https://ref.ly/Judg11:2); —ref. to Gad (cf. supr. esp. sub **5**). **c.** [1 Ch 5:14](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:14) (a Gadite).

## גָּלַשׁ (id: BDB01847)

\[Str1570] † \[גָּלַשׁ] **vb.** sit, sit up, possibly also recline (cf. Ar. جَلَسَ *sit up;* so Thes De al. (not Ew))—only **Qal** *Pf.* גָּֽלִשׁוּ(שֶׁ) Ct [4:1](https://ref.ly/Song4:1), [6:5](https://ref.ly/Song6:5) שֶׁגָּלְֽשׁוּ גִּלְעָד (הר)מ of flocks of goats, in sim. of a woman's hair; construction \& sense rather awkward.

> \[Str1571] גַּם v. sub גמם. p. 168

* **Associated Passages:** Song of Songs 4:1; Song of Songs 6:5


## גָּמָא (id: BDB01849)

\[Str1572] † \[גָּמָא] **vb.** swallow (liquids), (NH *id.;* cf. Aram. גְּמָא, גְּמַע, ܓܡܰܥ; perh. cf. also Eth. ግምዔ፡ *jar, vessel* v. Di)—**Pi.** *Impf.* יְגַמֶּא־אֶרֶץ [Jb 39:24](https://ref.ly/Job39:24) fig. of horse in swift gallop; (this, however, possibly denom. fr. גּוּמָּא NH, Aram. גּוּמְּתָא, ܓܽܘܡܬܳܐ *pit, hollow,* i.e. *he makes \[paws* or *stamps] hollows in the earth*). **Hiph.** *Imv. fs. sf.* הַגְמִיאִינִי נָא מְעַט־מַיִם [Gn 24:17](https://ref.ly/Gen24:17) *let me drink a little water, pray.*

> \[Str1573] † גֹּ֫מֶא **n.m.**:[Jb 8:11](https://ref.ly/Job8:11) rush, reed, papyrus (cf. Löwp. 55\) (NH גּוֺמֶא, Eth. ጎሜዕ፡ loan\-word, acc. to Di;—name from *swallowing*, sucking up, water? so Thes, cf. Che [Is 18:2](https://ref.ly/Isa18:2) al.; \= Copt. *gome*, v. Di [Ex 2:3](https://ref.ly/Exod2:3) whence others der. as loan\-word)—גֹּ֫מֶא [Ex 2:3](https://ref.ly/Exod2:3) \+ 3 t.;—*rush, paper\-reed,* (Egypt.) [Jb 8:11](https://ref.ly/Job8:11) (‖אָחוּ); coll. [Is 35:7](https://ref.ly/Isa35:7) (‖קָנֶה); as material, ´תֵּבַת ג [Ex 2:3](https://ref.ly/Exod2:3) *chest of paper\-reed;* ´כְּלֵי־ג [Is 18:2](https://ref.ly/Isa18:2) *vessels of paper\-reed.*

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 18:2; Job 8:11; Isaiah 35:7; Job 39:24; Exodus 2:3; Genesis 24:17


## גמד (id: BDB01851)

גמד (√ of foll.; cf. Ar. جَمَدَ *congeal, become solid; be hard, stern;* also *cut, cut off;* Aram. גְּמַד *contract;* Aph. *be bold, daring*).

> \[Str1574] † גֹּ֫מֶד **n.m.** cubit (fr. elbow to knuckles of clenched (contracted) hand; Aq on Ez [27:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:11), Ra al.; Gk. πυγμή; NH גּוֺמֶד *cubit;* so Aram. גַּרְמִידָא Ch WB1. 155, ܓܰܪܡܺܝܕܳܐ (lexx.))—Ju [3:16](https://ref.ly/Judg3:16) of Ehud's sword אָרְכָּהּ ´ג (v. GFM).

> \[Str1575] † גַּמָּדִים **n.pr.gent.** Ez [27:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:11); *valorous men* ThesAdd. 79 Ew RV m; but n.pr. apparently needed; גּמדים not elsewhere; LagOnom. Sacr. 2, 95, 2nd ed. 367 prop. גמרים v. גֹּמֶר; \< Co צמרים cf. [Gn 10:18](https://ref.ly/Gen10:18).

> \[Str1579] † גִּמְזוֺ **n.pr.loc.** (cf. Syr. ܓܽܘܡܺܝܙ (Lexx.) *sycamore;* Ar. جُمَّيزٌ is loan\-wd. Frä140; cf. As. n.pr.loc. *Gamuzanu* PinchesHbr. July, 1886, 222\), city of Judah toward Philistines [2 Ch 28:18](https://ref.ly/2Chr28:18); mod. *Jimzu,* E. from Lydda RobBR ii. 249 BDPal 21\.

* **Associated Passages:** 2 Chronicles 28:18; Ezekiel 27:11; Judges 3:16; Genesis 10:18
* **Associated Articles:** גָּמַר (ID: `BDB01868@BDBHebrewLexicon`)


## גָּמַל (id: BDB01855)

\[Str1580] † גָּמַל **vb. 1\.** deal fully or adequately with, deal out to. **2\.** wean. **3\.** ripen (As. *gamâlu, deal with, benefit,* e.g. VR 35:19; NH גָּמַל, Aram. גְּמַל; v. Palm. n.pr. אגמלא VogNo. 124, Nab. n. pr. f. גמלת Vog.:Nab. sub No. 7; perh. orig. *complete, accomplish,* cogn. גמר;—Ar. جَمَلَ is *collect,* جَمِلَ *be beautiful, goodly*)—**Qal** *Pf.* [Psalm 13:6](https://ref.ly/Ps13:6) \+; *Impf.* יִגְמֹל [Nu 17:23](https://ref.ly/Num17:23) \+; *Imv.* גְּמֹל [Psalm 119:17](https://ref.ly/Ps119:17); *Inf.* גָּמְלֵח, גָּמְלָהּ [1 S 1:23](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:23); *Pt.* גֹּמֵל [Pr 11:17](https://ref.ly/Prov11:17) \+; pass. גָּמוּל [Is 11:8](https://ref.ly/Isa11:8); גָּמֻל [Psalm 131:2](https://ref.ly/Ps131:2), [131:2](https://ref.ly/Ps131:2); —**1\. a.** *deal out to, do to,* c. 2 acc. גְּמָלַתְהוּ טוֺב וְלאֹ רָע *she doeth him good and not evil* [Pr 31:12](https://ref.ly/Prov31:12); כִּי אַתָּה גְּמַלְתַּנִי הַטּוֺבָה וַאֲנִי גְּמַלְתִּיךָ הָֽרָעָה *for thou hast done unto me the good but I have done unto thee the evil* [1 S 24:18](https://ref.ly/1Sam24:18); *do* evil רע(ה) *unto* [Gn 50:15](https://ref.ly/Gen50:15), [50:17](https://ref.ly/Gen50:17), [Pr 3:30](https://ref.ly/Prov3:30); c. לְ Pers. [Is 3:9](https://ref.ly/Isa3:9); *do* good *unto,* 2 acc. [Is 63:7](https://ref.ly/Isa63:7), [63:7](https://ref.ly/Isa63:7); cf. also גֹּמֵל נַפְשׁוֺ אִישׁ חֶסֶד [Pr 11:17](https://ref.ly/Prov11:17). **b.** with עַל Pers. *deal bountifully with* [Psalm 13:6](https://ref.ly/Ps13:6), [116:7](https://ref.ly/Ps116:7), [119:17](https://ref.ly/Ps119:17), [142:8](https://ref.ly/Ps142:7); c. acc. Pers. *reward* [2 S 22:21](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:21) (\= [Psalm 18:21](https://ref.ly/Ps18:21)), perh. also לָמָּה יִגְמְלֵנִי הַגְּמוּלָה הַזּאֹת *why should he reward me with this reward?* [2 S 19:37](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:37). **c.** *recompense, repay, requite,* in a bad sense, with 2 acc. [Psalm 7:5](https://ref.ly/Ps7:5); with לְ Pers. [Dt 32:6](https://ref.ly/Deut32:6), [Psalm 137:8](https://ref.ly/Ps137:8); with עַל Pers. [2 Ch. 20:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr20:11), [Psalm 103:10](https://ref.ly/Ps103:10), [Jo 4:4](https://ref.ly/Joel4:4). **2\.** *wean a child* (complete his nursing) [1 S 1:23](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:23), [1:23](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:23), [1:24](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:24), [1 K 11:20](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:20), [Ho 1:8](https://ref.ly/Hos1:8); גָּמוּל *weaned child* [Psalm 131:2](https://ref.ly/Ps131:2), [131:2](https://ref.ly/Ps131:2), [Is 11:8](https://ref.ly/Isa11:8); גְּמוּלֵי מֵחָלָב *weaned from milk* [Is 28:9](https://ref.ly/Isa28:9). **3\.** trans. *ripen, bear ripe* (almonds) [Nu 17:23](https://ref.ly/Num17:23); intrans. *become ripe* (grapes) [Is 18:5](https://ref.ly/Isa18:5). **Niph.** *Impf.* יִגָּמֵל [1 S 1:22](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:22); וַיִּגָּמַָל [Gn 21:8](https://ref.ly/Gen21:8); *Inf.* הִגָּמֵל [Gn 21:8](https://ref.ly/Gen21:8); —*be weaned.*

> \[Str1576] † גְּמוּל **n.m.** dealing, recompence, benefit—´ג [Psalm 94:2](https://ref.ly/Ps94:2) \+ 11 t.; sf. גְּמֻלְךָ Ob:[15](https://ref.ly/Obad1:15), etc.; pl. sf. גְּמוּלָיו [Psalm 103:2](https://ref.ly/Ps103:2); —**1\.** גְּמוּל יָדַיִם *dealing of the hands* Ju [9:16](https://ref.ly/Judg9:16), [Pr 12:14](https://ref.ly/Prov12:14) (יָשׁוּב לְ) [Is 3:11](https://ref.ly/Isa3:11) (יֵעָשֶה לְ). **2\.** *dealing*, hence (from context) *equivalent of dealing, recompence:* c. suff. Ob:[15](https://ref.ly/Obad1:15) גמלך ישׁוב בראשׁך, [Jo 4:4](https://ref.ly/Joel3:4), [4:7](https://ref.ly/Joel3:7) (הֵשִׁיב), so with הֵשִׁיב לְ [Psalm 28:4](https://ref.ly/Ps28:4); שִׁלֵּם לְ [137:8](https://ref.ly/Ps137:8), [Pr 19:17](https://ref.ly/Prov19:17); absolutely גְּמוּל לְ (הֵשִׁיב) שִׁלֵם [Is 59:18](https://ref.ly/Isa59:18), [59:18](https://ref.ly/Isa59:18), [66:6](https://ref.ly/Isa66:6), [Je 51:6](https://ref.ly/Jer51:6), [La 3:64](https://ref.ly/Lam3:64); עַל [Jo 4:4](https://ref.ly/Joel4:4), [Psalm 94:2](https://ref.ly/Ps94:2); [Is 35:4](https://ref.ly/Isa35:4) ´ג אלהים *the recompence of God.* **3\.** *benefit:* אַל תשׁכחי כל גמוליו [Psalm 103:2](https://ref.ly/Ps103:2); כגמול עליו *according to the benefit* (done) *unto him* [2 Ch 32:25](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:25)

> \[Str1577] † גָּמוּל **n.pr.m.** *(weaned)* a chief of the Levites [1 Ch 24:17](https://ref.ly/1Chr24:17). Vid. also בֵּית גָּמוּל ([Je 48:28](https://ref.ly/Jer48:28)).

> \[Str1578] † גְּמוּלָה **n.f.**:[2 S 19:37](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:37) dealing, recompence—´ג [2 S 19:37](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:37) cf. גָּמַל; pl. גְּמוּלוֺת *dealings* [Is 59:18](https://ref.ly/Isa59:18); [Je 51:56](https://ref.ly/Jer51:56) אֵל גְּמֻלוֺת *God of recompence.*

> \[Str1583] † \[תַּגְמוּל] **n.m.** benefit—pl. c. Aram. sf. כָּל־תַּגְמוּלוֺהִי עָלָי *all his benefits unto me* [Psalm 116:12](https://ref.ly/Ps116:12).

## גמם (id: BDB01863)

גמם (Ar. جَمَّ *become much* or *abundant,* جَمٌّ *abundance, much,* جَمَّةٌ *collection* (of water), *company* (of people)).

> \[Str1571] גַּם:768 **adv.** denoting *addition,* also, moreover, yea (Moab. *id.:*prob. akin to √ جَمَّ; cf. جَمًّا (accus.) *in a mass, altogether*)—**1\.** *also, moreover,* emphasizing sts. the thought of an entire sentence, but more usually the word immediately following, as [Gn 3:6](https://ref.ly/Gen3:6) וַתִּתֵּן גַּם לְאִישָׁהּ and gave *also* to her husband, v27 and take *also* of the tree of life, [7:3](https://ref.ly/Gen7:3), [19:21](https://ref.ly/Gen19:21), [24:19](https://ref.ly/Gen24:19), [26:21](https://ref.ly/Gen26:21), [29:27](https://ref.ly/Gen29:27), [30:15](https://ref.ly/Gen30:15), [32:21](https://ref.ly/Gen32:21) (…גַּם הִגֵּה : so [Est 7:9](https://ref.ly/Esth7:9)) [48:11](https://ref.ly/Gen48:11), [Ex 8:28](https://ref.ly/Exod8:28), [12:32](https://ref.ly/Exod12:32) b, [Dt 1:37](https://ref.ly/Deut1:37), [1 S 28:20](https://ref.ly/1Sam28:20), [2 S 11:12](https://ref.ly/2Sam11:12), [2 K 9:27](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:27), [Is 7:13](https://ref.ly/Isa7:13) \+. Often before pronouns, [Gn 4:4](https://ref.ly/Gen4:4) וְהֶבֶל הֵבִיא גַם הוּא and Abel, *he* also brought, [20:5](https://ref.ly/Gen20:5) וְהִיא־גַם־הִוא (so only here) and she herself *also,* v:[6](https://ref.ly/Gen20:6), [27:31](https://ref.ly/Gen27:31) וַיַּעַש גַּם הוּא, [30:8](https://ref.ly/Gen30:8), [Dt 3:20](https://ref.ly/Deut3:20), Ju [3:31](https://ref.ly/Judg3:31), [6:35](https://ref.ly/Judg6:35), [9:19](https://ref.ly/Judg9:19), [1 S 19:20](https://ref.ly/1Sam19:20)–24 [Je 12:6](https://ref.ly/Jer12:6), [48:26](https://ref.ly/Jer48:26) etc. (cf. MI6 ויאמר גם הא): esp. in genealogies of J (BuUrg. 220\) [Gn 4:22](https://ref.ly/Gen4:22), [4:26](https://ref.ly/Gen4:26), [10:21](https://ref.ly/Gen10:21), [19:38](https://ref.ly/Gen19:38), [22:20](https://ref.ly/Gen22:20), [22:24](https://ref.ly/Gen22:24) cf. Ju [8:31](https://ref.ly/Judg8:31). גַּם sq. pron. also begins a sentence with emph. in an elevated style, *thou (they) also,* [Is 14:10](https://ref.ly/Isa14:10), [Je 12:6](https://ref.ly/Jer12:6), [12:6](https://ref.ly/Jer12:6), [48:7](https://ref.ly/Jer48:7), Ez [16:52](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:52), [Na 3:11](https://ref.ly/Nah3:11), [3:11](https://ref.ly/Nah3:11). After a pron. in an oblique case (Ges§ 135, 2\) [Gn 27:34](https://ref.ly/Gen27:34), [1 S 19:23](https://ref.ly/1Sam19:23), [2 S 17:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:5), [Je 25:14](https://ref.ly/Jer25:14), [27:7](https://ref.ly/Jer27:7) \+. וְגַם *and also* (more often than גַּם alone attaching a sentence) [Gn 6:4](https://ref.ly/Gen6:4), [14:16](https://ref.ly/Gen14:16), [15:14](https://ref.ly/Gen15:14), [17:16](https://ref.ly/Gen17:16), [20:12](https://ref.ly/Gen20:12), [24:14](https://ref.ly/Gen24:14), [24:46](https://ref.ly/Gen24:46), [30:6](https://ref.ly/Gen30:6), [37:7](https://ref.ly/Gen37:7), [38:24](https://ref.ly/Gen38:24), [42:28](https://ref.ly/Gen42:28), [Ex 2:19](https://ref.ly/Exod2:19), [3:9](https://ref.ly/Exod3:9), [4:14](https://ref.ly/Exod4:14), [Jos 7:11](https://ref.ly/Josh7:11) (5 t.) [1 S 4:17](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:17), [1 K 21:19](https://ref.ly/1Kgs21:19) \+ oft.; with a negative \= *neither* [Ex 5:2](https://ref.ly/Exod5:2), [34:3](https://ref.ly/Exod34:3) al.—גַּם…גַּם (like *et … et*) *both … and* [Gn 44:16](https://ref.ly/Gen44:16), [47:3](https://ref.ly/Gen47:3), [47:19](https://ref.ly/Gen47:19), [Nu 18:3](https://ref.ly/Num18:3), [Je 51:12](https://ref.ly/Jer51:12), [Psalm 49:3](https://ref.ly/Ps49:3) \+: גַּם…גַּם גַּם… [Gn 24:25](https://ref.ly/Gen24:25), Ju [8:22](https://ref.ly/Judg8:22), [Ec 9:6](https://ref.ly/Eccl9:6): with a negative *neither … nor* [Nu 23:25](https://ref.ly/Num23:25), [1 S 20:27](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:27), [1 K 3:26](https://ref.ly/1Kgs3:26), and (3 t.) [Gn 43:8](https://ref.ly/Gen43:8), [Ex 4:10](https://ref.ly/Exod4:10), [1 S 28:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam28:6). So (but seldom) וְגַם…גַּם [Gn 24:44](https://ref.ly/Gen24:44), [Ex 10:25](https://ref.ly/Exod10:25) f. [1 S 2:26](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:26), [12:14](https://ref.ly/1Sam12:14), [26:25](https://ref.ly/1Sam26:25): with neg. [1 S 21:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam21:9).—N. B. In poetry independence and emphasis is sts. given by גַּם to a new idea, where in English we should be satisfied with *and:* [Psalm 107:5](https://ref.ly/Ps107:5), [137:1](https://ref.ly/Ps137:1), [Jb 24:19](https://ref.ly/Job24:19), Ct [7:14](https://ref.ly/Song7:13), [La 4:15](https://ref.ly/Lam4:15). **2\.** with stress on a particular word, *even,* [Ex 4:9](https://ref.ly/Exod4:9), [Nu 22:33](https://ref.ly/Num22:33) גַּם אוֺתְךָ *even* thee I had slain, and kept her alive, [2 S 17:10](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:10), [Je 2:33](https://ref.ly/Jer2:33), [Psalm 132:12](https://ref.ly/Ps132:12), [Pr 14:13](https://ref.ly/Prov14:13) *even* in laughter the heart is sorrowful, v:[20](https://ref.ly/Prov14:20), [17:28](https://ref.ly/Prov17:28), [20:11](https://ref.ly/Prov20:11), [Ru 2:15](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:15), [Ne 3:35](https://ref.ly/Neh3:35); and so often after כִּי, [Dt 12:31](https://ref.ly/Deut12:31) for *even* their sons they burn in the fire to their gods, [1 S 22:17](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:17), [Is 26:12](https://ref.ly/Isa26:12), [Je 6:11](https://ref.ly/Jer6:11), [12:6](https://ref.ly/Jer12:6), [14:5](https://ref.ly/Jer14:5), [Ho 9:12](https://ref.ly/Hos9:12) כִּי־גַם־אוֺי לָהֶם for *even* woe is it to them when I depart from them; הֲגַם †[Gn 16:13](https://ref.ly/Gen16:13), [1 S 10:11](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:11), [10:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:12) \= [19:24](https://ref.ly/1Sam19:24), [1 K 17:20](https://ref.ly/1Kgs17:20), [Psalm 78:20](https://ref.ly/Ps78:20), [Jb 41:1](https://ref.ly/Job41:1), [Est 7:8](https://ref.ly/Esth7:8). an inf. or cogn. accus. attached to a verb, † [Gn 31:15](https://ref.ly/Gen31:15), [46:4](https://ref.ly/Gen46:4), [Nu 11:15](https://ref.ly/Num11:15), [16:13](https://ref.ly/Num16:13), [1 S 1:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:6). גַּם שְׁנֵיהֶם lit. *even both* i.e. the one as well as the other (Germ. *alle beide*) is said idiomatically, † [Gn 27:45](https://ref.ly/Gen27:45) (שְׁנֵיכֶם), [Dt 22:22](https://ref.ly/Deut22:22), [23:19](https://ref.ly/Deut23:19), [1 S 25:43](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:43) (שְׁתֵּיהֶן), [Pr 17:15](https://ref.ly/Prov17:15), [20:10](https://ref.ly/Prov20:10), [20:12](https://ref.ly/Prov20:12), [Ru 1:5](https://ref.ly/Ruth1:5). **3\.** introducing a climax, *yea,* esp. in a rhetorical style, [Gn 27:33](https://ref.ly/Gen27:33), [Dt 23:3](https://ref.ly/Deut23:3), [23:4](https://ref.ly/Deut23:4), Ju [5:4](https://ref.ly/Judg5:4), [5:4](https://ref.ly/Judg5:4), [Is 13:3](https://ref.ly/Isa13:3), [14:8](https://ref.ly/Isa14:8), [43:13](https://ref.ly/Isa43:13) *yea*, from to\-day I am the same, [44:12](https://ref.ly/Isa44:12) גַּם רָעֵב *yea*, he is hungry, and has no strength, [47:3](https://ref.ly/Isa47:3), [48:8](https://ref.ly/Isa48:8), [48:8](https://ref.ly/Isa48:8), [48:8](https://ref.ly/Isa48:8), [57:7](https://ref.ly/Isa57:7), [Je 46:16](https://ref.ly/Jer46:16), [48:2](https://ref.ly/Jer48:2), [51:44](https://ref.ly/Jer51:44), [Ho 7:9](https://ref.ly/Hos7:9), [Psalm 41:10](https://ref.ly/Ps41:10), [84:7](https://ref.ly/Ps84:7), [139:12](https://ref.ly/Ps139:12); [Is 66:8](https://ref.ly/Isa66:8) כִּי־חָ֫לָה גַּם־יָֽלְדָה, צִיּוֺן אֶת־בָּנֶיהָ, [Je 5:28](https://ref.ly/Jer5:28), [12:2](https://ref.ly/Jer12:2), Ez [24:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:5), [Jb 21:7](https://ref.ly/Job21:7), Ct [8:1](https://ref.ly/Song8:1): emphasizing an extreme, or aggravated, case, *yea, even,* [Is 49:15](https://ref.ly/Isa49:15) *yea,* these may forgot, [Je 8:17](https://ref.ly/Jer8:17) *yea,* the stork knoweth, etc., [Is 23:12](https://ref.ly/Isa23:12) גַּם שָׁם *even* there no rest shall be for thee (so [Psalm 139:10](https://ref.ly/Ps139:10)), [49:25](https://ref.ly/Isa49:25), [57:6](https://ref.ly/Isa57:6), [Je 23:11](https://ref.ly/Jer23:11) b, [Mal 3:15](https://ref.ly/Mal3:15), [Psalm 84:4](https://ref.ly/Ps84:4). **4\.** expressing *correspondence,* esp. in the matter of *retribution* (the גַּם *correlativum*), so frequently גַּם אֲנִי, גַּם אָנֹכִי I *also* (on my part): \&md. [Gn 20:6](https://ref.ly/Gen20:6) I *also* \[as well as thyself] know that thou hast done this innocently, [Jos 24:18](https://ref.ly/Josh24:18), Ju [2:21](https://ref.ly/Judg2:21), [1 S 1:28](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:28) (cf. Dr) [28:22](https://ref.ly/1Sam28:22) גַּם אַתָּה thou *also* (as I have done v:[21](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:21)), [2 S 12:13](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:13) ´י *also* \[responding to thy confession] hath removed thy sin, [2 K 2:3](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:3), [2:5](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:5), [Is 31:2](https://ref.ly/Isa31:2), [66:3](https://ref.ly/Isa66:3)–4 (גם…גם, emphasizing the action of both parties), [Je 2:36](https://ref.ly/Jer2:36), [4:12](https://ref.ly/Jer4:12) now will I *also* \[in correspondence with their deeds] speak judgments with them, [7:11](https://ref.ly/Jer7:11), [51:49](https://ref.ly/Jer51:49) (גם…גם), Ez [5:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek5:8), [5:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek5:11), [16:43](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:43) (וְגַם \= therefore *also*), [23:35](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:35), [Ho 4:6](https://ref.ly/Hos4:6) because thou hast forgotten the direction of thy God, I *also* (on my part) will forget thy children, [Psalm 52:7](https://ref.ly/Ps52:7) (thou lovest evil, etc.) גַּם אֵל יִתָּצְךָ God *also* (on his part) will pluck thee up, [71:22](https://ref.ly/Ps71:22), [133:1](https://ref.ly/Ps133:1), [Pr 1:26](https://ref.ly/Prov1:26), [Jb 7:11](https://ref.ly/Job7:11), [12:3](https://ref.ly/Job12:3), [16:4](https://ref.ly/Job16:4); so וְגַם Ju [2:3](https://ref.ly/Judg2:3), [Am 4:6](https://ref.ly/Amos4:6), [4:7](https://ref.ly/Amos4:7), [Mi 6:13](https://ref.ly/Mic6:13), [Mal 2:9](https://ref.ly/Mal2:9). In the apodosis (uncommon): [Gn 13:16](https://ref.ly/Gen13:16) if a man could number the dust of the earth, גַּם זַרְעֲךָ יִמָּנֶה thy seed *also* should be numbered, [Je 31:36](https://ref.ly/Jer31:36), [31:37](https://ref.ly/Jer31:37), [33:21](https://ref.ly/Jer33:21), [33:26](https://ref.ly/Jer33:26), [Zc 8:6](https://ref.ly/Zech8:6). The correspondence is sometimes of the nature of a climax: [Gn 27:33](https://ref.ly/Gen27:33), [30:8](https://ref.ly/Gen30:8) I have wrestled, גַּם יָכֹלְתִּי (cf. [1 K 22:22](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:22) וְגַם תּוּכָ֔ל, [Je 50:24](https://ref.ly/Jer50:24)), [Is 66:8](https://ref.ly/Isa66:8). **5\.** connecting two ideas which express (or imply) a contradiction, גַּם acquires sts. an adversative force (cf. אַף **1** end), *yet, but, though:* [Psalm 95:9](https://ref.ly/Ps95:9) they tried me, *but also* (Che) saw my work (viz. of judgment), [129:2](https://ref.ly/Ps129:2), [Je 6:15](https://ref.ly/Jer6:15) \= [8:12](https://ref.ly/Jer8:12), Ez [20:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:23), [Ec 4:8](https://ref.ly/Eccl4:8), [4:16](https://ref.ly/Eccl4:16), [5:18](https://ref.ly/Eccl5:18), [Ne 6:1](https://ref.ly/Neh6:1). So וְגַם Ez [16:28](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:28), [20:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:15), [Ec 3:13](https://ref.ly/Eccl3:13), [6:7](https://ref.ly/Eccl6:7), [Ne 5:8](https://ref.ly/Neh5:8). **6\.** גַּם כִּי (with impf.) *yea, when* [La 3:8](https://ref.ly/Lam3:8), *even when* [Pr 22:6](https://ref.ly/Prov22:6); *yea though* (stating an imagined case) [Is 1:15](https://ref.ly/Isa1:15), [Ho 8:10](https://ref.ly/Hos8:10), [9:16](https://ref.ly/Hos9:16), [Psalm 23:4](https://ref.ly/Ps23:4) (so גַּם אִם [Ec 8:17](https://ref.ly/Eccl8:17)); *also* (is it) *that* [Ru 2:21](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:21) (v. כִּי **1 d**). כִּי גַם \= *for even* (v. supr. **2**); \= *though even, although* (conceding a fact) [Ec 4:14](https://ref.ly/Eccl4:14) (v. De) [8:12](https://ref.ly/Eccl8:12).

## גמץ (id: BDB01866)

גמץ (√ of foll., Aram. גְּמַץ *dig* (perh. denom.)).

> \[Str1475] † גּוּמָּץ **n.m.** pit (Aram. loan\-wd., cf. גּוּמְצָא, ܓܽܘܡܳܨܳܐ; NH גּוּמָּצָא cf. BaNB 66, Anm.I), [Ec 10:8](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:8) חֹפֵר בּוֺ יִפָּוֺל ´ג *he that diggeth a pit shall fall in to it;* so also [Pr 26:27](https://ref.ly/Prov26:27) Targum SyrVer where HCT כֹּרֶה שַׁחַת .

* **Associated Passages:** Ecclesiastes 10:8; Proverbs 26:27


## גָּמַר (id: BDB01868)

\[Str1584] † גָּמַר **vb. end, come to an end, complete** (only Psalm ) (NH *id. complete;* As *gaâmru* LotzTP.Register \& deriv. COTGloss al.; Aram. גְּמַר, ܓܡܰܪ; Eth. አግመረ፡ (II); cf. also Ar. جَمَّرَ *collect, assemble*)—**Qal** *Pf.* ´גּ [Psalm 12:2](https://ref.ly/Ps12:2) [77:9](https://ref.ly/Ps77:9); *Impf* יִגְמֹר [Psalm 138:8](https://ref.ly/Ps138:8); יִגְמָ־ר [Psalm 7:10](https://ref.ly/Ps7:10); *Pt.* גֹּמֵר [Psalm 57:3](https://ref.ly/Ps57:3); —**1\.** *come to an end, be no more* [Psalm 7:10](https://ref.ly/Ps7:10), [12:2](https://ref.ly/Ps12:2) (‖ \[פָּסַס]); [77:9](https://ref.ly/Ps77:9) (‖אָפַס). **2\.** trans.*bring to an end, complete,* אֵל גֹּמֵר עָלָי [Psalm 57:3](https://ref.ly/Ps57:3) God that *completeth, accomplisheth, for me* (LXX Gr BI גמל, \& so Che doubtfully), יִגְמֹר בַּעֲדִי ´י [138:8](https://ref.ly/Ps138:8).

> \[Str1586] † גֹּ֫מָר **n.pr. 1\.m. a.** son of Japhet [Gn 10:2](https://ref.ly/Gen10:2), [10:3](https://ref.ly/Gen10:3) \= [1 Ch 1:5](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:5), [1:6](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:6), Ez [38:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek38:6); Lag :Onom. Sacr. 2, 95, 2nd ed. 367, v. BN 77 prop. גמרים for גמדים Ez [27:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:11) q.v.; he identif. גֹּמֶר with Cappadocia (cf. Targum Ez [27:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:11), KiepLb. d. alt. Geogr. 91\) \= Armen. *Gamiṛ* \-χ (with pl. ending) v. also LagArm. Stud. 32 § 448; cf. further As.*Gimirrai*, SchrKG 157 ff. DlPa 245 f. also LenOr.ii. 1, 332 ff. **2\. f.** wife of Hosea, [Ho 1:3](https://ref.ly/Hos1:3).

> \[Str1587] † גְּמַרְיָ֫הוּ, גְּמַרְיָה **n.pr.m.** (´י *hath accomplished*)—**1\.** גמריהו son of Shaphan [Je 36:10](https://ref.ly/Jer36:10), [36:11](https://ref.ly/Jer36:11), [36:12](https://ref.ly/Jer36:12), [36:25](https://ref.ly/Jer36:25). **2\.** גמריה son of Hilkiah [Je 29:3](https://ref.ly/Jer29:3).

> \[Str1588] גַּן, גַּגָּה v. sub גנן. p. 171

## גָּנַב (id: BDB01872)

\[Str1589] † \[גָּנַב] **vb.** steal (NH *id.,* Aram. גְּנַב, ܓܢܰܒ; Ar. جَنَب is *hurt the side,* or *put aside,* den. fr. جَنْبٌ *side*)—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 fs. sf. גְּנָבַתּוּ [Jb 21:18](https://ref.ly/Job21:18), [27:20](https://ref.ly/Job27:20), גְּנָבָ֫תַם [Gn 31:22](https://ref.ly/Gen31:22); 2 ms. גָּנַבְתּ [Gn 31:30](https://ref.ly/Gen31:30); 1 s. וְגַָ\+נבְתִּי consec. [Pr 30:9](https://ref.ly/Prov30:9) ; גָּנְֽבוּ [Jos 7:11](https://ref.ly/Josh7:11), [2 S 21:12](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:12) ; sf. גְּנָבוּךָ [2 S 19:42](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:42); *Impf.* יִגְנֹֽ־ב [Ex 21:37](https://ref.ly/Exod21:37), יִגְנוֺב [Pr 6:30](https://ref.ly/Prov6:30), וַיִּגְנֹב [Gn 31:20](https://ref.ly/Gen31:20); 3 fs. וַתִּגְנֹב [Gn 31:19](https://ref.ly/Gen31:19) \+ 2 t.; 2 ms. תִּגְנֹב [Ex 20:15](https://ref.ly/Exod20:15) [Dt 5:17](https://ref.ly/Deut5:17), וַתִּגְנֹב [Gn 31:26](https://ref.ly/Gen31:26), [31:27](https://ref.ly/Gen31:27), יִגְנְבוּ Ob:[5](https://ref.ly/Obad1:5) , תִּגְ\+נֹבוּ [Lv 19:11](https://ref.ly/Lev19:11), נִגְנֹב [Gn 44:8](https://ref.ly/Gen44:8); *Inf. abs.* גָּנֹב [Ex 22:11](https://ref.ly/Exod22:11) \+ 2 t.; *Pt.* גֹּנֵב [Ex 21:16](https://ref.ly/Exod21:16) \+ 2 t., גָּנוּב [Gn 30:33](https://ref.ly/Gen30:33); f. cstr. גְּנֻבְֽתִי [Gn 31:39](https://ref.ly/Gen31:39), [31:39](https://ref.ly/Gen31:39); pl. גְּנוּבִים [Pr 9:17](https://ref.ly/Prov9:17); in Hexateuch always JED, exc. [Lv 19:11](https://ref.ly/Lev19:11) (H);—*steal* c. acc. rei [Gn 31:19](https://ref.ly/Gen31:19), [31:30](https://ref.ly/Gen31:30), [31:32](https://ref.ly/Gen31:32), [44:8](https://ref.ly/Gen44:8), [Ex 21:37](https://ref.ly/Exod21:37), [2 S 21:12](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:12) cf. pass. [Gn 30:33](https://ref.ly/Gen30:33), [31:39](https://ref.ly/Gen31:39), [Pr 9:17](https://ref.ly/Prov9:17); c. obj. Pers. [Ex 21:16](https://ref.ly/Exod21:16), [Dt 24:7](https://ref.ly/Deut24:7), [2 S 19:42](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:42); \= *take by stealth* (for good purpose) [2 K 11:2](https://ref.ly/2Kgs11:2) \= [2 Ch 22:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr22:11); abs. [Ex 20:15](https://ref.ly/Exod20:15) \= [Dt 5:17](https://ref.ly/Deut5:17), [Lv 19:11](https://ref.ly/Lev19:11) [Jos 7:11](https://ref.ly/Josh7:11), [Pr 6:30](https://ref.ly/Prov6:30), [30:9](https://ref.ly/Prov30:9), Ob:[5](https://ref.ly/Obad1:5), [Zc 5:3](https://ref.ly/Zech5:3), cf.*Inf. abs.* [Ho 4:2](https://ref.ly/Hos4:2), [Je 7:9](https://ref.ly/Jer7:9) \& (c. Niph.) [Ex 22:11](https://ref.ly/Exod22:11); גנב לֵב \= *deceive* [Gn 31:20](https://ref.ly/Gen31:20), [31:26](https://ref.ly/Gen31:26) cf Di; אֹתִי ´ג [Gn 31:27](https://ref.ly/Gen31:27) *id.;* of sudden sweeping off by storm, in simile [Jb 21:18](https://ref.ly/Job21:18) fig. of destruction of wicked [27:20](https://ref.ly/Job27:20). **Niph.** *be stolen,* subj. rei גָּנֹב יִגָּנֵב [Ex 22:11](https://ref.ly/Exod22:11). **Pi.** *steal away* (trans.) וַיְגַגֵּב אבשׁלום את־לֵב [2 S 15:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:6); מְגַנְּבֵי דְּבָרַי [Je 23:30](https://ref.ly/Jer23:30). **Pu.** *be stolen away* subj. Pers. גֻּנֹּב גֻּנַּבְתִּי [Gn 40:15](https://ref.ly/Gen40:15); subj. rei וְגֻנַּב consec. [Ex 22:6](https://ref.ly/Exod22:6); *be brought by stealth* [Jb 4:12](https://ref.ly/Job4:12). יְגָֻ\+נּב. **Hithp.** *go by stealth, steal away* יִתְגַּנֵּב [2 S 19:4](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:4), וַיִּתְגַּנֵּב [2 S 19:4](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:4). (Syr. ܓܢܒ ܢܦܫܗ *steal* oneself *away;* so ܐܬܓܢܒ [Gn 31:27](https://ref.ly/Gen31:27).)

[Jb 24:14](https://ref.ly/Job24:14) (in simile), Ob:[5](https://ref.ly/Obad1:5) (‖שׁוֺדֲדֵי לַיְלָה); [Jb 30:5](https://ref.ly/Job30:5); stealer of men (slave\-dealer) [Dt 24:7](https://ref.ly/Deut24:7).

## גנז (id: BDB01876)

גנז (*cover up, hide*, cf. NH *hide,* Ar. جَنَزَ *cover up,* Aram. גְּנַז Ithpa, ܓܢܰܙ Ethp. *be hidden,* Eth. ገነዘ፡ *enwrap*).

> \[Str1595] † \[גְּנָזִים] **n.\[m.] pl. 1\.** chests (?); **2\.** treasury—only pl. cstr. גִּנְזֵי;—**1\.** perh. *chests* of variegated cloth בְּרֹמִים ´ג Ez [27:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:24) so Thes AV RV; Sm Co al. *cloths, carpets*, Ew *Taschen*. **2\.** *treasury* (NH גֶּנֶז, Aram. גִּנְזָא, גְּנִיז, BAram. cstr. pl. גִּנְזֵי; Pers. loan\-word from گَنْجَه *treasure*? cf. Vullersii.1032 LagGes.Abh.27\); גִּנְזֵי הַמֶּלֶח [Est 3:9](https://ref.ly/Esth3:9), [4:7](https://ref.ly/Esth4:7).

> \[Str1597] † גַּנְזַךְ **n.\[m.]** treasury (NH גנזכה *treasure;* loan\-wd. fr. or through Pers. cf. supr. \& LagGes. Abh. 28\); וְגַנְזַכָּיו [1 Ch 28:11](https://ref.ly/1Chr28:11). LXX τῶν ζακχὼ αὐτοῦ; but LXXL τῶν ἀποθηκῶν αὐτοῦ.

* **Associated Passages:** Ezekiel 27:24; 1 Chronicles 28:11; Esther 4:7; Esther 3:9

## גָּעָה (id: BDB01888)

\[Str1600] † גָּעָה **vb.** low (of cattle) (NH *id.*, Aram. גְּעָא, ܓܥܳܐ)—יִגְעֶה־שׁוֺר [Jb 6:5](https://ref.ly/Job6:5); הָלֽח וְגָעוֺ [1 S 6:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam6:12).

> \[Str1601] † גֹּעָה **n.pr.loc.** close to Jerusalem גֹּעָתָה [Je 31:39](https://ref.ly/Jer31:39); —site unknown.

> \[Str1602] † גָּעַל **vb.** abhor, loathe (NH געל Nithpa.*be fouled*, **Hiph.** *rinse out* with hot water; גְּעִילָה *rejection;* Aram. געל Ithpe. *be fouled, soiled*)—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 fs. נָּֽעֲלָה [Lv 26:43](https://ref.ly/Lev26:43), [Je 14:19](https://ref.ly/Jer14:19), וְגָֽעֲלָה cons. [Lv 26:30](https://ref.ly/Lev26:30); גְּעַלְתִּים [Lv 26:44](https://ref.ly/Lev26:44); גָּֽעֲלוּ Ez [16:45](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:45); *Impf.* 3 fs. תִּגְעַל [Lv 26:11](https://ref.ly/Lev26:11), [26:15](https://ref.ly/Lev26:15); *Pt.* f. גֹּעֶ֫לֶת Ez [16:45](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:45), [16:45](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:45); —*abhor, loathe*, (usually c. acc.), Yahweh, his people [Lv 26:11](https://ref.ly/Lev26:11), [26:30](https://ref.ly/Lev26:30), [26:44](https://ref.ly/Lev26:44) (H) [Je 14:19](https://ref.ly/Jer14:19) (c. בְּ); the people, Yahweh's statutes [Lv 26:15](https://ref.ly/Lev26:15), [26:43](https://ref.ly/Lev26:43) (H); Ez [16:45](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:45), [16:45](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:45) women their husbands (fig., in proph. ag. Jerusalem). **Niph.** נִגְעַל מָגֵן גִּבּוֺרִים [2 S 1:21](https://ref.ly/2Sam1:21)  *the shield of heroes was defiled* RVm VB Kirkp. but \< *is rejected, cast away (with loathing)* Dr, cf. Klo; LXX προσωχθίσθη. **Hiph.** *Impf.* שׁוֺרוֺ עִבַּר וְלאֹ יַֹגְעִָל [Jb 21:10](https://ref.ly/Job21:10); *and doth not cause*, or *allow*, (the cow) *to reject as loathesome* Ra De, cf. DI1, DI2, however, of bull, *cast away* (semen), or *shew aversion*.

> \[Str1603] † גַּ֫עַל **n.pr.m.** appar. a Canaanite, son of עֶבֶד (but cf. VB) Ju [9:26](https://ref.ly/Judg9:26), [9:28](https://ref.ly/Judg9:28), [9:30](https://ref.ly/Judg9:30), [9:31](https://ref.ly/Judg9:31), [9:35](https://ref.ly/Judg9:35), [9:36](https://ref.ly/Judg9:36), [9:37](https://ref.ly/Judg9:37), [9:39](https://ref.ly/Judg9:39), [9:41](https://ref.ly/Judg9:41).

## גָּעַר (id: BDB01893)

\[Str1605] † גָּעַר **vb.** rebuke (Aram. גְּעַר ܓܥܰܪ id., Eth. ገዐረ፡ *cry out*, Sab. יגער n.pr., Sab. Denkm. p. 29\.30\)—[Is 17:13](https://ref.ly/Isa17:13); גָּעַרְתָּ [Psalm 9:6](https://ref.ly/Ps9:6) \+ 2 t., etc.; *Impf.* יִגְעַר [Zc 3:2](https://ref.ly/Zech3:2) \+ 3 t., תִּגְעֲרוּ [Ru 2:16](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:16); *Imv.* גְּעַר [Psalm 68:31](https://ref.ly/Ps68:31); *Inf.* מִגְּעָ־ר [Is 54:9](https://ref.ly/Isa54:9); *Pt.* גּוֺעֵר [Na 1:4](https://ref.ly/Nah1:4), גֹּעֵר [Mal 2:3](https://ref.ly/Mal2:3); —c. בְּ except [Psalm 9:6](https://ref.ly/Ps9:6), [68:31](https://ref.ly/Ps68:31), [119:21](https://ref.ly/Ps119:21), [Mal 2:3](https://ref.ly/Mal2:3) where acc.;—**1\.** *rebuke*, father his son [Gn 37:10](https://ref.ly/Gen37:10) (E), reapers Ruth [Ru 2:16](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:16), priest Jeremiah [Je 29:27](https://ref.ly/Jer29:27). **2\.** of God rebuking nations [Psalm 9:6](https://ref.ly/Ps9:6), [Is 17:13](https://ref.ly/Isa17:13), proud [Psalm 119:21](https://ref.ly/Ps119:21), Satan [Zc 3:2](https://ref.ly/Zech3:2), [3:2](https://ref.ly/Zech3:2), חית קנה [Psalm 68:31](https://ref.ly/Ps68:31), בָּאֹכֵל (the devouring swarm of locusts, restraining them) [Mal 3:11](https://ref.ly/Mal3:11), the sea drying it up [Psalm 106:9](https://ref.ly/Ps106:9), [Na 1:4](https://ref.ly/Nah1:4), Zion [Is 54:9](https://ref.ly/Isa54:9); הַזֶּרַע the *seed* (prevent the usual harvest) [Mal 2:3](https://ref.ly/Mal2:3) (LXX Aq Vulgate הַזְּרֹעַ, *the arm*, viz., of priests, that they may not extend it to bless).

## גָּעַשׁ (id: BDB01896)

\[Str1607] † \[גָּעַשׁ] **vb.** shake, quake (NH *id.*)—**Qal** *Impf.* וַתִּגְעַשׁ [Psalm 18:8](https://ref.ly/Ps18:8) a \= [2 S 22:8](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:8) a Kt (Qr Hithp.);—*quake* of earth at theoph., [Psalm 18:8](https://ref.ly/Ps18:8) \= [2 S 22:8](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:8) (rd. Kt; ‖רעשׁ). **Pu.** *Impf. be shaken up, convulsed* יְגֹעֲשׁוּ־עַם [Jb 34:20](https://ref.ly/Job34:20) *a people are convulsed*. **Hithp.** *shake back and forth, toss* or *reel to and fro* וַיִּתְגָּעַשׁ Qr [2 S 22:8](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:8) a (\< Kt **Qal**); יִתְגָּֽעֲשׁוּ [Je 46:7](https://ref.ly/Jer46:7), וַיִּתְגָּֽעֲשׁוּ [Psalm 18:8](https://ref.ly/Ps18:8) b \+ 2 t.;—of mountains [Psalm 18:8](https://ref.ly/Ps18:8) b \= [2 S 22:8](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:8) b; of waves of sea, [Je 5:22](https://ref.ly/Jer5:22); of waters of Nile [Je 46:7](https://ref.ly/Jer46:7) (cf. v:[8](https://ref.ly/Jer46:8)). **Hithpo.** *Pf.* וְהִתְגֹּעֲשׁוּ consec. [Je 25:16](https://ref.ly/Jer25:16); *Impf.* יִתְגֹּעֲשׁוּ [Je 46:8](https://ref.ly/Jer46:8); *id.* of waters of Nile [Je 46:8](https://ref.ly/Jer46:8); of drunken men, *reel to and fro*, [Je 25:16](https://ref.ly/Jer25:16) (fig. of nations confounded by ´י).

> \[Str1608] † \[גַּ֫עַשׁ] **n.pr.loc.** הַ־רָ\+גּעַשׁ [Jos 24:30](https://ref.ly/Josh24:30), Ju [2:9](https://ref.ly/Judg2:9) mt. in Ephraim, S. of (חֶרֶס)תִּמְנַת־סֶרַח q.v.; נַחֲלֵי ָ\+גּעַשׁ [2 S 23:30](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:30) \= [1 Ch 11:32](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:32) of ravines thereon, or near by.

> \[Str1609] † גַּעְתָּם **n.pr.m.** a chief of Edom, son of Eliphaz [Gn 36:11](https://ref.ly/Gen36:11), [36:16](https://ref.ly/Gen36:16), [1 Ch 1:36](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:36).

## גפף (id: BDB01902)

גפף (√ of foll.; cf. Syr. ܓܦܦ in deriv., ܓܦܺܝܦܳܐ *curved, convex;* also Ar. جَفْجَفٌ both *hilly country* and *depression*, Frey.)

> \[Str1610] † \[גַּף] **n.m.** body, self, height, elevation (cf. NH גּוּף)—sg. sf. גַּפּוֺ [Ex 21:3](https://ref.ly/Exod21:3), [21:3](https://ref.ly/Exod21:3), [21:4](https://ref.ly/Exod21:4); pl. cstr. גַּפֵּי [Pr 9:3](https://ref.ly/Prov9:3); —**1\.** *body, self*, only in phr. יָבאֹ בְּגַפּוֺ (יֵצֵא)) *he shall come in (go out) by himself* (with his body) [Ex 21:3](https://ref.ly/Exod21:3), [21:3](https://ref.ly/Exod21:3), [21:4](https://ref.ly/Exod21:4); in law of slave (cf. Di). **2\.** *height, elevation* עַל־גַּפֵּי מְרֹמֵי קָרֶת [Pr 9:3](https://ref.ly/Prov9:3).

> \[Str1613] † גֹּ֫פֶר **n.\[m.]** gopher, only in עֲצֵי־גֹּפֶר [Gn 6:14](https://ref.ly/Gen6:14) (P), wood of which the ark was made (word dub.; Thes comp. כֹּפֶר \& so Rob Ges (hence ‘ *pitch\-wood*, resinous wood ’), cf. Di; LagSemitica i. 64; Symmict. ii. 93, BN 217 ff. thinks word not original, but inferred from גפרית, and substituted here for גפרית by copyist, or editor. Cf. following).

> \[Str1614] † גָּפְרִית **n.f.** brimstone (Aram. גּוּפְרִיתָא, also כבריתא, כובריתא, ܟܶܒܪܝܺܬܳܐ, ܟܽܒܪܝܺܬܳܐ; Ar. كِبْرِيت (certainly foreign wd.); der. from (כֹּפֶר), גֹּפֶר by Thes RobGes, cf. Di [Gn 6:14](https://ref.ly/Gen6:14); i.e. *pitch*, and then other combustibles, esp. *sulphur;* Lag1. c. thinks fr. Bactr. *vohûkereti*)—גָּפְרִית [Gn 19:24](https://ref.ly/Gen19:24) \+ 6 t.—*brimstone*, after הִמְטִיר [Gn 19:24](https://ref.ly/Gen19:24) (J), fig. of judgment [Psalm 11:6](https://ref.ly/Ps11:6), Ez [38:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek38:22) (in all ‖אֵשׁ etc.), cf. also [Jb 18:15](https://ref.ly/Job18:15), [Is 34:9](https://ref.ly/Isa34:9), \& [Dt 29:22](https://ref.ly/Deut29:22); ´נַחַל ג simile for the destroying breath of ´י [Is 30:33](https://ref.ly/Isa30:33).

> \[Str1616] גֵּר v. sub I. גור. p. 158

> \[Str1615] גִּר v. sub גיר. p. 162

> \[Str1617] † גֵּרָא **n.pr.m.** (Ph. גרא) son of Benjamin [Gn 46:21](https://ref.ly/Gen46:21) (lacking [Nu 26:38](https://ref.ly/Num26:38)–[40](https://ref.ly/Num26:40)); son of Belaï, \& grandson of Benj., acc. to [1 Ch 8:3](https://ref.ly/1Chr8:3), [8:5](https://ref.ly/1Chr8:5) cf. v:[7](https://ref.ly/1Chr8:7); designation of a Benjamite family or clan; hence Ehud called בֶּן־גֵּרָא Ju [3:15](https://ref.ly/Judg3:15), \& so Shimei, [2 S 16:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam16:5), [19:17](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:17), [19:19](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:19), [1 K 2:8](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:8).

## גרב (id: BDB01909)

גרב (√ of foll.; Ar. جَرِبَ, Aram. ܓܪܶܒ (*have the scab* etc.), are appar. denom.)

> \[Str1618] † גָּרָב **n.\[m.]** itch, scab (Ar. جَرَبٌ, Aram. גַּרְבָּא, ܓܰܪܒܳܐ, As. *garabu* Dl:§ 162\)—גָּרָב [Lv 21:20](https://ref.ly/Lev21:20), [22:22](https://ref.ly/Lev22:22), [Dt 28:27](https://ref.ly/Deut28:27).

> \[Str1619] † גָּרֵב **1\. n.pr.m.** (cf. Palm. n.pr.m. גריבא Vog141) one of David's heroes [2 S 23:38](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:38) \= [1 Ch 11:40](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:40). **2\. n.pr.loc.** (cf. Sab. n.pr.loc. גרבם HalNo. 379, 382\) only in גִּבְעַת גָּרֵב [Je 31:39](https://ref.ly/Jer31:39) *hill of Gareb*, appar. close to Jerusalem on the SW. cf. Gf; v. also Hi Che.

> \[Str1622] † גִּרְגָּשִׁי **adj.gent.** only ´הַגּ as n.pr.coll.; ‘ begotten ’ by Canaan [Gn 10:16](https://ref.ly/Gen10:16) \= [1 Ch 1:14](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:14); in list of Canaanitish peoples displaced by Isr.; territ. unknown; [Gn 15:21](https://ref.ly/Gen15:21), [Dt 7:1](https://ref.ly/Deut7:1), [Jos 3:10](https://ref.ly/Josh3:10), [24:11](https://ref.ly/Josh24:11), [Ne 9:8](https://ref.ly/Neh9:8); cf. [Gn 10:16](https://ref.ly/Gen10:16), [1 Ch 1:14](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:14) supr. (Hex only JED).

## גָּרַד (id: BDB01914)

\[Str1623] † \[גָּרַד] **vb.** scrape, scratch (NH *id.*, Aram. גְּרַד, ܓܪܰܕ \& Ph. מגרדם *flesh\-scrapers* CIS338. 4; cf. also Ar. جَرَدَ *peel* or *strip off* bark, *shave* hair fr. a hide etc.)—**Hithp.** *Inf.* לְהִתְגָּרֵד *scrape one's self* [Jb 2:8](https://ref.ly/Job2:8).
