## גָּרָה (id: BDB01915)

\[Str1624] † \[גָּרָה] **vb.** only **Pi.** stir up strife, **Hithp.** engage in strife (cf. NH Pi. *stir up, excite*, against (בְּ) Hithp.; Aram. Pa. גָּרֵא ܓܰܪܺܝ; esp. As. *garû*, Qal *attack, be at war with, garû, giṙû, enemy*, etc. AsrbAnnals iv. 49, 50 (KBii. 190\) also SASmithAsrb. i. ii. Gloss.; Ar. جَرَى is *run*, esp. of water, *flow*, etc.)—**Pi.** *Impf.* 3 ms. יְגָרֶה [Pr 15:18](https://ref.ly/Prov15:18), [28:25](https://ref.ly/Prov28:25), [29:22](https://ref.ly/Prov29:22), all c. obj. מָדוֺן *excite, stir up, strife*.—On גֵּרָה [Dt 14:8](https://ref.ly/Deut14:8) v. I. גֵּרָה sub גרר. **Hithp.** *Pf.* 2 fs. הִתְגָּרִיתְ [Je 50:24](https://ref.ly/Jer50:24); *Impf.* 3 ms. יִתְגָּרֶה [Dn 11:25](https://ref.ly/Dan11:25), וְיִתְגָּרֶ֯ו v:[10](https://ref.ly/Dan11:10); 2 ms. תִּתְגָּרֶה [2 K 14:19](https://ref.ly/2Kgs14:19), [2 Ch 25:19](https://ref.ly/2Chr25:19); juss. apoc. תִּתְגָּר [Dt 2:9](https://ref.ly/Deut2:9), [2:19](https://ref.ly/Deut2:19); 3 mpl. יִתְגָּרוּ [Pr 28:4](https://ref.ly/Prov28:4), [Dn 11:10](https://ref.ly/Dan11:10), cf. [Dn 11:10](https://ref.ly/Dan11:10) Kt., v. supr.; 2 mpl. תִּתְגָּרוּ [Dt 2:5](https://ref.ly/Deut2:5) (juss.); *Imv.* ms. הִתְגָּר [Dt 2:24](https://ref.ly/Deut2:24); —**1\.** *excite oneself* against (בְּ Pers.), *engage in strife* with, lit. [Dt 2:5](https://ref.ly/Deut2:5), [2:19](https://ref.ly/Deut2:19), [2 K 14:19](https://ref.ly/2Kgs14:19) \= [2 Ch 25:19](https://ref.ly/2Chr25:19) (sq. בְּרָעָה;—רעה personif. as challenged opponent); also \+ acc. of manner, מִלְחָמָה [Dt 2:9](https://ref.ly/Deut2:9), [2:24](https://ref.ly/Deut2:24); fig. וְשֹׁמְרֵי תוֺרָה יִתְגָּרוּ בָם [Pr 28:4](https://ref.ly/Prov28:4) *while they that keep the law are at strife with them*, i.e. with those that forsake it. **2\.** abs., *excite oneself* (against foe), *wage war*, only Dn 11: יִתְגָּרוּ וְאָֽסְפוּ הֲמוֺן חֲיָלִים רַבִּים v:[10](https://ref.ly/Dan11:10) *they shall wage war*, etc.; וְיָשֹׁב וְיִתְגָּרֶ֯ו עַד־מָעֻזֹּה v:[10](https://ref.ly/Dan11:10) *and he shall return and war, even to his stronghold;* v:[25](https://ref.ly/Dan11:25) sq. לַמִּלְחָמָה \+ בְּחַיִל גָּדוֺל (instr.)

> \[Str1627] † גָּרוֺן **n.m.**:[Psalm 69:4](https://ref.ly/Ps69:4) neck, throat (connexion with above √ dub.; Thes RobGes (cf. MV) from גרה in (assumed) sense of גרר of a *dragging, scraping* sound; cf. מְגֵרָה; yet √ perh. גרן q.v.; Ar. جِرَانٌ (sub جَرَنَ Frey Lane) *front part of neck* of camel, etc.)—´ג [Is 3:16](https://ref.ly/Isa3:16), [58:1](https://ref.ly/Isa58:1); sf. גְּרוֺנִי [Psalm 69:4](https://ref.ly/Ps69:4); גְּרוֺנֵח Ez [16:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:11) \+ [Je 2:25](https://ref.ly/Jer2:25) Qr (Kt גורנך); גְּרוֺנָם [Psalm 115:7](https://ref.ly/Ps115:7) [149:6](https://ref.ly/Ps149:6), גְּרֹנָם [Psalm 5:10](https://ref.ly/Ps5:10); —*throat;* **1\.** of the visible exterior (front) of throat, *neck* ´נְטֻו֯וֺת ג [Is 3:16](https://ref.ly/Isa3:16) *outstretched of neck* (\= with outstretched neck); וְרָבִיד עַל־גְּרוֺנֵח Ez [41:19](https://ref.ly/Ezek41:19) *and a chain upon thy neck*. **2\.** *throat*, as capable of thirst [Je 2:25](https://ref.ly/Jer2:25); as organ of speech קְרָא בְגָרוֺן [Is 58:1](https://ref.ly/Isa58:1) (‖הָרֵם קוֺלֶָךָ), ´לאֹ יֶהְגּוּ בִּג [Psalm 115:7](https://ref.ly/Ps115:7); also רוֺמֲמוֺת אֵל בִּגְרוֺנָם [Psalm 149:6](https://ref.ly/Ps149:6) *high songs to God (be) in their throat;* cf. נִחַר גְּרוֺנִי [Psalm 69:4](https://ref.ly/Ps69:4) *my throat is parched* (‖יָגַעְתִּי בְקָרְאִי); so also קֶבֶר־פָּתוּחַ גְּרֹנָם [Psalm 5:10](https://ref.ly/Ps5:10) *their throat is an opened sepulchre*.

> \[Str8409] † \[תִּגְרָה] **n.f.** contention, strife, hostility (cf. Aram. תִּגְרָא, oft. Targum; v. Thes; תִּגְרָה NHWB \& Fl in Ch WBii. 581\)—only cstr. sg. מִתִּגְרַת יָֽדְךָ [Psalm 39:11](https://ref.ly/Ps39:11) *from* (i.e. *through*) *the hostility of thy hand I am consumed*.

> \[Str1625–26] I. גֵּרָה II. גֵּרָה v. sub גרר. p. 176

> \[Str1628] גֵּרוּת v. sub I. גור. p. 158

## גרטל (id: BDB01924)

גרטל (possibly quadrilit. √, whence foll. c. א prosthet. Cf. however infr.)

> \[Str105] † \[אֲגַרְטָל] **n.m.** a kind of vessel, basin or basket (?; others *basket\-like, basket\-shaped* vessel); only pl. cstr. אֲגַרְטְלֵי זָהָב, \& כֶסֶף ´א, both [Ezr 1:9](https://ref.ly/Ezra1:9), denoting vessels from temple at Jerus. restored by Cyrus. (Etym. \& mng. dub. cf. supr.; A LXXL Codd. LXX of ψυκτῆρες, *wine\-coolers;* B om.; now commonly compared with Aram. קַרְטָלִיתָא Levy NHWBiv. 376, ܩܰܪܛܳܠܴܐ, Ar. قِرْطَلَّةٌ, قِرْطَالَةٌ, supposed to be loan\-word fr. Hellenist. Gk. κάρταλος, κάρταλλος (e.g. LXX [2 K 10:7](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:7), Heb. דּוּדִים), all \= *basket, fruit\-basket*, etc., *canistrum* (so MV BeRy SS); but κάρταλλος itself is possibly a Pers. or Shemit. loan\-word; cf. esp. Frä77 f.; further conject. in Thes BeRy.)

* **Associated Passages:** Ezra 1:9; 2 Kings 10:7


## גרל (id: BDB01926)

גרל (cf. Ar. جَرَلٌ, \& pl. أَجْرَالٌ, *stones*, or *stony place* planted with trees; جَرِلٌ *stony;* جَرْوَلٌ, جُرَوِلٌ, *stony ground, stones;* hence foll., because stones were used in casting lots; cf. Gk. ψῆφος *pebble, vote;* κύσμος *bean, lot*).

> \[Str1486] גּוֺרָל:77 **n.m.**:[Lv 16:8](https://ref.ly/Lev16:8) \& († [Jos 21:10](https://ref.ly/Josh21:10), but v. infr.) **f.** lot: (NH *id.* \& denom. Hiph. הִגְרִיל *cast lots*)—abs. ´ג [Jos 16:1](https://ref.ly/Josh16:1) \+ 51 t.; cstr. גּוֺרַל [Jos 18:11](https://ref.ly/Josh18:11) \+ 2 t., גֹּרַל [Nu 36:3](https://ref.ly/Num36:3); sf. גּוֺרָלִי [Psalm 16:5](https://ref.ly/Ps16:5), גֹּרָלִי Ju [1:3](https://ref.ly/Judg1:3), גּוֺרָֽלְךָ [Pr 1:14](https://ref.ly/Prov1:14), גֹּרָֽלְךָ [Dn 12:13](https://ref.ly/Dan12:13), גּוֺרָלֶָךָ Ju [1:3](https://ref.ly/Judg1:3), גּוֺרָלֵךְ [Je 13:25](https://ref.ly/Jer13:25), [Is 57:6](https://ref.ly/Isa57:6), גּוֺרָלוֺ [1 Ch 26:14](https://ref.ly/1Chr26:14), גּוֺרָלָם [Jos 18:11](https://ref.ly/Josh18:11) \+ 2 t.; pl. גּוֺרָלוֺת [Jon 1:7](https://ref.ly/Jonah1:7) \+ 8 t., גֹּרָלוֺת [Lv 16:8](https://ref.ly/Lev16:8); —*a lot* cast for the decision of questions [Pr 18:18](https://ref.ly/Prov18:18), designation of persons, etc., for service or punishment, assignment of property, etc.; prob. stones put into the bosom\-fold of a garment, [Pr 16:33](https://ref.ly/Prov16:33) (´בַּחֵיק יוּטַל אֶת־הַגּ), or into a vessel, and shaken until one springs out (עָלָה, יָצָא or—late—נָפַל); this was regarded originally as divine decision (cf. on Urim \& Thummim p. 22, supr.); for the agent, the usual verbs are הִשְׁלִיךְ, יָרָה, נָתַן, and הִפִּיל. Particularly:—**1\.** *lot* for dividing land, esp. that which Joshua cast at Shiloh ´לִפְנֵי י [Jos 18:6](https://ref.ly/Josh18:6) (יָרָה) v:[8](https://ref.ly/Josh18:8), v:[10](https://ref.ly/Josh18:10) (both הִשְׁלִיךְ; all three sq. לְ of Pers. for whom; all JE); cf. [19:51](https://ref.ly/Josh19:51) (P); so of lot coming up, or forth, עָלָה [Jos 18:11](https://ref.ly/Josh18:11) (cstr.; P), also sq. לְ Pers. [19:10](https://ref.ly/Josh19:10) (P), \& foll. c. יָצָא [16:1](https://ref.ly/Josh16:1) (JE), [Nu 33:54](https://ref.ly/Num33:54), [Jos 19:1](https://ref.ly/Josh19:1), [19:17](https://ref.ly/Josh19:17), [19:24](https://ref.ly/Josh19:24), [19:32](https://ref.ly/Josh19:32), [19:40](https://ref.ly/Josh19:40), [21:4](https://ref.ly/Josh21:4) (all P); *be divided by lot* ´יֵחָלֵק בְּג [Nu 26:55](https://ref.ly/Num26:55), or *according to lot* תֵּחָלֵק ´עַל־פִּי הַגּ v:[56](https://ref.ly/Num26:56); *apportion by lot* הִתְנַחֲלוּ ´בְּג [33:54](https://ref.ly/Num33:54), [34:13](https://ref.ly/Num34:13), ´נִחַל בְּג [Jos 19:51](https://ref.ly/Josh19:51); *give by lot* ´נָתַן בְּג [Nu 36:2](https://ref.ly/Num36:2) ´נָתַן בַּגּ [Jos 21:8](https://ref.ly/Josh21:8) (all P) [1 Ch 6:50](https://ref.ly/1Chr6:50); *have by lot* ´בַּגּ ´הָיָה ל [Jos 21:4](https://ref.ly/Josh21:4), \& (היה om.) v:[5](https://ref.ly/Josh21:5), v:[6](https://ref.ly/Josh21:6) (all P) cf. [1 Ch 6:46](https://ref.ly/1Chr6:46), [6:48](https://ref.ly/1Chr6:48); also [Jos 21:10](https://ref.ly/Josh21:10) (P; רִאישֹׁנָה ´הַגּ, only here f.; LXX—not LXXL—om. ראישׁנה, \& so) ‖ [1 Ch 6:39](https://ref.ly/1Chr6:39); cf. further [Is 34:17](https://ref.ly/Isa34:17) (הִפִּיל לְ). **2\.** *lot* for assigning to service, duty or punishment, **a.** concerning the goats on day of atonement, [Lv 16:8](https://ref.ly/Lev16:8) (נָתַן עַל) v:[8](https://ref.ly/Lev16:8), v:[8](https://ref.ly/Lev16:8) (sq. לְ); cf. v:[9](https://ref.ly/Lev16:9), v:[10](https://ref.ly/Lev16:10) (עָלָה עַל; all P). **b.** assigning priests to their courses, singers, musicians \& porters to their duties, by lot ´חָלַק בְּג [1 Ch 24:5](https://ref.ly/1Chr24:5); ´הִפִּיל ג v:[31](https://ref.ly/1Chr24:31), [25:8](https://ref.ly/1Chr25:8), [26:13](https://ref.ly/1Chr26:13) (sq. לְ of position) v:[14](https://ref.ly/1Chr26:14); of lot coming forth (יָצָא לְ Pers.) [24:7](https://ref.ly/1Chr24:7) (cf. v:[7](https://ref.ly/1Chr24:7); v:[18](https://ref.ly/1Chr24:18)) [25:9](https://ref.ly/1Chr25:9) (cf. v:[9](https://ref.ly/1Chr25:9)–[31](https://ref.ly/1Chr25:31)) [26:14](https://ref.ly/1Chr26:14) cf. וַיֵּצֵא גּוֺרָלוֺ v:[14](https://ref.ly/1Chr26:14). **c.** *cast lots* for dwelling in Jerus. [1 S 14:41](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:41) ff. where divine agency in detection is prominent but word גּוֺרָל not used). **g.** in allotting slaves [Jo 4:3](https://ref.ly/Joel4:3) אֶל־עַמִּי ´יַדּוּ גּ; [Na 3:10](https://ref.ly/Nah3:10) (יַדּוּ עַל); so Ob:[11](https://ref.ly/Obad1:11) ´וְעַל־יְרוּשָׁלִַם יַדּוּ ג—all c. vb. ידד q.v. **h.** in distributing plundered garments [Psalm 22:19](https://ref.ly/Ps22:19) (הִפִּיל על). **i.** to determine favourable day for a scheme, הִפִּיל [Est 3:7](https://ref.ly/Esth3:7) (לִפְנֵי הָמָן) [9:24](https://ref.ly/Esth9:24) (sq. inf.; v. also פּוּר). **3\.** *thing assigned, apportioned, allotted* esp. of land assigned by lot, *allotment* [Jos 15:1](https://ref.ly/Josh15:1), [17:1](https://ref.ly/Josh17:1), [21:38](https://ref.ly/Josh21:38) (all P), [17:14](https://ref.ly/Josh17:14), [17:17](https://ref.ly/Josh17:17) \= *portion, share* (‖נַחֲלָה JE); cf. גֹּרַל נַחֲלָתֵנוּ [Nu 36:3](https://ref.ly/Num36:3) (P), גּוֺרַל נַחֲלָתָם [Jos 14:2](https://ref.ly/Josh14:2) (P; but perh. rd. abs. גּוֺרָל cf. [Mi 2:5](https://ref.ly/Mic2:5) ´הִשְׁלִיךְ חֶבֶל בְּג (Hi Che; cf. also Ew); [Psalm 125:3](https://ref.ly/Ps125:3), land, exposed to oppressive exaction; also fig. of ´י as portion, allotment of his people [Psalm 16:5](https://ref.ly/Ps16:5) (so Che, \& on תָּמִיד for תּוֺמִיךְ cf. his crit. n.; v. also Ew; otherwise De al.;—‖מְנָת חֶלְקִי); on the other hand, of idols [Is 57:6](https://ref.ly/Isa57:6) (‖חֵלֶק); also ´עָמַד לְג [Dn 12:13](https://ref.ly/Dan12:13), of *allotted portion, share*, in the Messianic consummation; more generally, *one's portion, lot, fortune* גּוֺרָֽלְךָ תַּפִּיל בְּתוֺכֵנוּ [Pr 1:14](https://ref.ly/Prov1:14) *cast thy fortune into the midst of us, in with us* (‖כִּיס אֶחָד יִהְיֶה לְכֻלָּנוּ). **4\.** *portion* \= *recompence, retribution* [Is 17:14](https://ref.ly/Isa17:14) (‖חֵלֶק), [Je 13:25](https://ref.ly/Jer13:25) (‖מְנָת מִדַּיִךְ), both implying divine agency.

## גרן (id: BDB01933)

גרן (√ of foll.; ? cf. Ar. جَرَنَ, *become accustomed, worn smooth* (of skin, garment, etc.); also *grind grain vehemently;* vid. further جَرِينٌ *what one has ground*, of grain; \& *place in which dates are dried* \= مَجْرَنٌ).

## גָּרַע (id: BDB01937)

\[Str1639] † \[גָּרַע] **vb.** diminish, restrain, withdraw (NH *id.*, Aram. גְּרַע ܓܪܰܥ *shave head*, etc.; cf. جَرِعَ *swallow*)—**Qal** *Impf.* יִגְרַע [Jb 36:7](https://ref.ly/Job36:7), יִגְרָע [Ex 21:10](https://ref.ly/Exod21:10); 2 ms. תִּגְרַע [Dt 13:1](https://ref.ly/Deut13:1) \+ 3 t.; אֶגְרַע Ez [5:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek5:11) (but cf. *infr.*), וָאֶגְרַע Ez [16:27](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:27); תִּגְרְעוּ [Ex 5:8](https://ref.ly/Exod5:8) \+ 2 t.; *Inf.* לִגְרֹעַ [Ec 3:14](https://ref.ly/Eccl3:14); *Pt.* גְּרוּעָה [Is 15:2](https://ref.ly/Isa15:2) (so Baer; v. d. H. גְּדוּעָה v. גדע), גְּרֻעָה [Je 48:37](https://ref.ly/Jer48:37); —**1\.** *diminish*, usually sq. מִן [Ex 5:8](https://ref.ly/Exod5:8), [5:19](https://ref.ly/Exod5:19) (the tale of bricks), [Dt 4:2](https://ref.ly/Deut4:2), [13:1](https://ref.ly/Deut13:1) (in both, word of ´י; opp. הוסיף) cf. [Ec 3:14](https://ref.ly/Eccl3:14), and also [Je 26:2](https://ref.ly/Jer26:2) (c. acc.); c. acc. also [Ex 21:10](https://ref.ly/Exod21:10) (food etc.) cf. Ez [16:27](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:27); *Pt. pass. diminished, clipped*, of beard, [Je 48:37](https://ref.ly/Jer48:37) and [Is 15:2](https://ref.ly/Isa15:2) (so Baer, rightly, cf. his note and v. גדע). **2\.** *restrain* [Jb 15:4](https://ref.ly/Job15:4) (c. acc. שִיחָה meditation, devotion), [Jb 15:8](https://ref.ly/Job15:8) (c. acc. חָכְמָה, sq. אֵלֶיךָ, i.e. *unto, for thyself*, monopolize; De Di Da render *draw unto thyself*, appropriate; Schult. comp. Ar. *swallow*, cf. Ew *saugst zu dir ein Weisheit*). **3\.** *withdraw* [Jb 36:7](https://ref.ly/Job36:7) (obj. עַיִן, *eye,* sq. מִן); Ez [5:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek5:11) absol. (si vera l.); Co. reads אֶתְגָּרֶה, for אֶגְרַע, on intern. grounds; others: *withdraw* (thy needs) Sm (cf. [16:27](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:27)); *withdraw* (mine eye) Ges Ke. **Niph.** *Pf.* וְנִגְרַע consec. [Lv 27:18](https://ref.ly/Lev27:18); וְנִגְרְעָה [Nu 36:3](https://ref.ly/Num36:3); *Impf.* יִגָּרַע [Nu 27:4](https://ref.ly/Num27:4), [36:4](https://ref.ly/Num36:4), יִגָּרֵָעַ [Nu 36:3](https://ref.ly/Num36:3); 1 pl. נִגָּרַע [Nu 9:7](https://ref.ly/Num9:7); *Pt.* נִגְרָע [Ex 5:11](https://ref.ly/Exod5:11); —**1\.** *be withdrawn* c. מִן [Nu 36:3](https://ref.ly/Num36:3), [36:3](https://ref.ly/Num36:3), [36:4](https://ref.ly/Num36:4) (inheritance, נחלה); [Nu 27:4](https://ref.ly/Num27:4) a name out of a family; [Ex 5:11](https://ref.ly/Exod5:11) (מֵעֲבֹדַתְכֶם דָּבָר); impers. [Lv 27:18](https://ref.ly/Lev27:18) (*abatement shall be made*). **2\.** *be restrained* sq. inf. [Nu 9:7](https://ref.ly/Num9:7). *Pi.* *Impf.* יְגָרַע [Jb 36:27](https://ref.ly/Job36:27) *withdraw* \= *draw up* c. acc. נִטְפֵי־מָיִם cf. Di (on other hand, De al. *draw down, let down*).

> \[Str4052] † \[מִגְרָעָה] **n.f.** recess, rebatement (RV), i.e. ledge, only pl., נָתֵן מִגְרָעוֺת לבית [1 K 6:6](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:6).

* **Associated Passages:** Deuteronomy 4:2; Ezekiel 5:11; Jeremiah 48:37; Ecclesiastes 3:14; 1 Kings 6:6; Numbers 36:4; Exodus 5:19; Isaiah 15:2; Deuteronomy 13:1; Numbers 9:7; Leviticus 27:18; Job 36:7; Job 36:27; Jeremiah 26:2; Numbers 27:4; Job 15:4; Ezekiel 16:27; Numbers 36:3; Exodus 21:10; Job 15:8; Exodus 5:11; Exodus 5:8

## גָּרַף (id: BDB01939)

\[Str1640] † \[גָּרַף] **vb.** sweep away (orig. *shovel* or *scoop up, away*, cf. NH *id.*, Ar. جَرَفَ; Aram. גְּרַף, ܓܪܰܦ, Eth. in deriv.)—גְּרָפָם (נַחַל קִישׁוֺן) Ju [5:21](https://ref.ly/Judg5:21).

> \[Str106] † \[אֶגְרֹף] **n.\[m.]** fist (LXX πυγμή, so Vulgate; but Targum *club, staff* (?), \& so SS, cf. esp. [Ex 21:18](https://ref.ly/Exod21:18); —NH אֶגְרוֺף)—וִהִכָּה אִישׁ אֶת־רֵעֵהוּ בְּאֶבֶן אוֺ בְאֶגְרָֹף [Ex 21:18](https://ref.ly/Exod21:18); (cstr.) לְהַכּוֺת בְּאֶגְרֹף רֶָשַׁע [Is 58:4](https://ref.ly/Isa58:4).

> \[Str4053] † \[מֶגְרָפָה] **n.f.** shovel (NH *id.;* Aram. מַגְרוֺפִיתָא; Ar. مَجْرَفَةٌ (v. Lane)) only pl. מֶגְרְפֹתֵיהֶם [Jo 1:17](https://ref.ly/Joel1:17) (Thes *clods*, after Jewish interpreters, so AV RV, cf. however MeJoe1 100 f; LXX Me ארותיהם *their cribs*, of cattle; mng. of clause dubious).

* **Associated Passages:** Judges 5:21; Exodus 21:18; Isaiah 58:4; Joel 1:17


## גָּרַר (id: BDB01942)

\[Str1641] † \[גָּרַר] **vb.** drag, drag away (NH *id.*, Ar. جَرَّ Aram. גְּרַר, ܓܰܪ As. *garâru, run*, ZimBP 102; etc.)—**Qal** *Impf.* sf. יְנֹרֵהוּ Hb [1:15](https://ref.ly/Hab1:15), יְגוֺרֵם [Pr 21:7](https://ref.ly/Prov21:7)—*drag away*, fishes in net, fig. Hb [1:15](https://ref.ly/Hab1:15) (Chaldeans, their opponents); violence, the wicked [Pr 21:7](https://ref.ly/Prov21:7). **Niph.** (rd. **Qal** ? Sam. יָגוֺר cf. Di) *Impf.* יִָ\+גּר [Lv 11:7](https://ref.ly/Lev11:7) c. acc. cogn. גֵּרָה לאֹ־יִָ\+גּר \= (the) *cud he cheweth not*, cf. also [Dt 14:8](https://ref.ly/Deut14:8) Sam. LXX (Di). **Po‘al** *Pt.* (denom.; so NH Qal \& Niph.) מִגֹרָרוֺת בַּמְּגֵרָה [1 K 7:9](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:9) *sawn with the saw*. **Hithpo.** סַעַר מִתְגּוֺרֵָר [Jer 30:23](https://ref.ly/Jer30:23) *a* (sweeping) *roaring whirlwind* (‖ [23:19](https://ref.ly/Jer23:19) has מִתְחוֺלֵל). On יִתְגּוֺרָרוּ [Ho 7:14](https://ref.ly/Hos7:14), v. I. גור, גדד.

> \[Str1625] † I. גֵּרָה **n.f.** cud (Ar. جِرَّةٌ, so called fr. sound of rumination ?)—גֵּרָה [Lv 11:3](https://ref.ly/Lev11:3) \+ 10 t.—*cud*, only in legislation of clean and unclean animals, and almost always c. (י—ֵ, ת—ַ, ה—ָ) מַעֲלֵה [Lv 11:3](https://ref.ly/Lev11:3), [11:4](https://ref.ly/Lev11:4), [11:4](https://ref.ly/Lev11:4), [11:5](https://ref.ly/Lev11:5), [11:6](https://ref.ly/Lev11:6), [11:26](https://ref.ly/Lev11:26) (P) [Dt 14:6](https://ref.ly/Deut14:6), [14:6](https://ref.ly/Deut14:6), [14:7](https://ref.ly/Deut14:7); יִָ\+גּר ´ג (cf. גרר Niph.) [Lv 11:7](https://ref.ly/Lev11:7), [Dt 14:8](https://ref.ly/Deut14:8) has גֵּרָת without vb., but read there as [Lv 11:7](https://ref.ly/Lev11:7) (so Sam. LXX Di).

> \[Str1626] †II. גֵּרָה **n.f.** a weight, 20th part of shekel, gerah (As. *girû*, ZehnpfundBAS i. 506\)—גֵּרָה [Ex 30:13](https://ref.ly/Exod30:13) \+ 4 t.—Only in definition of שֶׁקֶל, c. num. עֶשְרִים [Ex 30:13](https://ref.ly/Exod30:13), [Lv 27:25](https://ref.ly/Lev27:25), [Nu 3:47](https://ref.ly/Num3:47), [18:16](https://ref.ly/Num18:16), Ez [45:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek45:12).

> \[Str1620] † \[גַּרְגַּר] **n.m.** berry (NH *id.*), גַּרְגְּרִים בְּראֹשׁ אָמָיר [Is 17:6](https://ref.ly/Isa17:6).

> \[Str1621] † גַּרְגְּרוֺת **n.f.pl.** neck (on format. cf. BaNB 204\)—גַּרְגְּרֹתֶָיךָ [Pr 1:9](https://ref.ly/Prov1:9), [3:22](https://ref.ly/Prov3:22), גַּרְגְרוֺתֶָךָ [Pr 3:3](https://ref.ly/Prov3:3), גַּרְגְּרֹתֶָךָ [6:21](https://ref.ly/Prov6:21)—always in fig., instruction of parents an ornament, a chain *upon neck* [Pr 1:9](https://ref.ly/Prov1:9), *bind them upon thy neck* [6:21](https://ref.ly/Prov6:21); so of mercy \& truth [3:3](https://ref.ly/Prov3:3), cf. v:[22](https://ref.ly/Prov3:22).

> \[Str4050] † מְגֵרָה **n.f.** saw (NH *id.*, perh. from its harsh *dragging* sound)—מְגֵרָה [2 S 12:31](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:31) a \+ 2 t.; pl. מְגֵרוֺת [1 Ch 20:3](https://ref.ly/1Chr20:3) (\< מַגְזְרוֺת [2 S 12:31](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:31) b)—*saw*, used in cutting stone מְגֹרָרוֺת בַּמְּגֵרָה [1 K 7:9](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:9); used in torture (or as tools for enforced labour) of captives [2 S 12:31](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:31) a [1 Ch 20:3](https://ref.ly/1Chr20:3) a.

> \[Str1642] † גְּרָר **n.pr.loc.** south of Gaza, usually identif. with mod. *Umm Jerâr* BDPal 159 GuêrinJudêe ii. 257 ff.; but *Wady Jerūr* W. of *‘ Ain Ḳadīs* acc. to TrumbullKadesh Barnea 61 ff. cf. GutheZPV viii 1885, 215; [Gn 20:2](https://ref.ly/Gen20:2), [26:6](https://ref.ly/Gen26:6), [26:20](https://ref.ly/Gen26:20), [26:26](https://ref.ly/Gen26:26), [2 Ch 14:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr14:12), [14:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr14:13); גְּרָ֫רָה [Gn 10:19](https://ref.ly/Gen10:19), [26:1](https://ref.ly/Gen26:1); נַ֫חַל גְּרָר Gn 26:l7; וַיָּגָר בִּגְרָר [Gn 20:1](https://ref.ly/Gen20:1).

## גרש (id: BDB01949)

גרש (Ar. جَرَشَ *bray, pound, grind, coarse*, Aram. גְּרַס (rare) *crush*, ܓܪܺܝܣ, *crushed*, ܓܰܪܶܣ *crush, destroy;* cf. following).



## גָּרַס (id: BDB01950)

\[Str1638] † \[גָּרַס] **vb.** be crushed (Aram. ܓܪܶܣ \[*be crushed,] perish*)—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 fs. גָּֽרְסָה [Psalm 119:20](https://ref.ly/Ps119:20) fig. נַפְשִׁינ לְ ´ג *my soul is crushed* (i.e. perisheth: cf. Syr.) with longing *for* (cf. כָּלָה לְ). **Hiph.** *Impf.* וַיַּגְרֵס בֶּחָצָץ שִָׁ\+נּי [La 3:16](https://ref.ly/Lam3:16) *and he hath crushed my teeth with gravel*, fig. of divine chastisement.

> \[Str1643] † גֶּ֫רֶש **n.\[m.]** a crushing, but only concrete of that which is crushed, *groats, grits* (cf. Ar. جَرِيشٌ, NH גְּרִיס, Aram. (rare) גִּרְשָא, גְּרוּסְיָא ܓܳܪܣܳܐ)—אָבִיב קָלוּי בָּאֵשׁ גֶּרֶש כַּרְמֶל [Lv 2:14](https://ref.ly/Lev2:14) *young ears parched with fire, groats (\&) fresh fruit* (v. Sifra ad loc.); (וּמִשַּׁמְנָהּ)מִגִּרְשָהּ [Lv 2:16](https://ref.ly/Lev2:16).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 119:20; Leviticus 2:14; Leviticus 2:16; Lamentations 3:16


## גָּרַשׁ (id: BDB01952)

\[Str1644] † \[גָּרַשׁ] **vb.** drive out, cast out (NH *id.*, Aram. גְּרַשׁ Pa. Ithp. of divorce, MI19 ויגרשה)—**Qal** *Impf.* וַיִּגְרְשׁוּ [Is 57:20](https://ref.ly/Isa57:20); *Inf.* sf. מִגְרָשָׁהּ Ez [36:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:5) infr.; *Pt. act.* גֹּרֵשׁ [Ex 34:11](https://ref.ly/Exod34:11); *pass.* f. גְּרוּשָׁה [Lv 21:7](https://ref.ly/Lev21:7) \+ 4 t.;—*cast our, thrust out*, ´י, the Amorites [Ex 34:11](https://ref.ly/Exod34:11) (JE; c. מִפְּנֵי); waters, mire and dirt רֶפֶשׁ וָטִיט [Is 57:20](https://ref.ly/Isa57:20); pass. only of divorced woman [Lv 21:7](https://ref.ly/Lev21:7) (sq. מֵאִישָׁהּ; elsewh. with אַלְמָנָה *widow*), v:[14](https://ref.ly/Lev21:14), [22:13](https://ref.ly/Lev22:13) (all H), [Nu 30:10](https://ref.ly/Num30:10) (P) Ez [44:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek44:22). On מִגְרָשָׁהּ Ez [36:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:5) as Aram. *Inf.* cf. Ges§ 45 1\. c. Köi 166; Co del., on intern. grounds, cf. LXX **Niph.** *Pf.* נִגְרָשׁ [Is 57:20](https://ref.ly/Isa57:20); 3 fs. וְנִגְרְשָׁה consec. [Am. 8:8](https://ref.ly/Amos8:8); נִגְרַשְׁתִּי [Jon 2:5](https://ref.ly/Jonah2:5); —*be driven away*, sq. מִנֶּגֶד עֵינֶיךָ, i.e. from the presence of Yahweh [Jon 2:5](https://ref.ly/Jonah2:5); *be driven, tossed*, as the Nile [Am 8:8](https://ref.ly/Amos8:8); of the sea [Is 57:20](https://ref.ly/Isa57:20) rel. cl., אֲשֶׁר om., *like the sea that is tossed*. **Pi.** *Pf.* 3 fs. וְגֵרְשָׁה consec. [Ex 23:28](https://ref.ly/Exod23:28); גֵּרַשְׁתָּ [Gn 4:14](https://ref.ly/Gen4:14), sf. 3 mpl. וְגֵרַשְׁתָּמוֺ consec. [Ex 23:31](https://ref.ly/Exod23:31); וְגֵרַשְׁתִּ֫י [Ex 33:2](https://ref.ly/Exod33:2), sf. גֵּרַשְׁתִּיהוּ Ez [31:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek31:11) (so Targum Vulgate, not LXX; cf. Co's conj.); וְגֵּרַשְׁתִּיו consec. [Nu 22:11](https://ref.ly/Num22:11); 3 pl. sf. גֵּרְשׁוּנִי [1 S 26:19](https://ref.ly/1Sam26:19); *Impf.* יְגָרֵשׁ [Ex 11:1](https://ref.ly/Exod11:1), וַיְגָ֫רֶשׁ [Gn 3:24](https://ref.ly/Gen3:24) \+ 6 t.; sf. וַיְגָרֲשֵׁהוּ [Psalm 34:1](https://ref.ly/Ps34:1), יְגָֽרֲשֵׁם [Ex 6:1](https://ref.ly/Exod6:1); 2 ms. תְּגָרֵשׁ [Psalm 80:9](https://ref.ly/Ps80:9), וַתְּגָ֫רֶשׁ [Jos 24:12](https://ref.ly/Josh24:12); אֲגָרֵשׁ Ju [2:3](https://ref.ly/Judg2:3), וָֽאֲגָרֵשׁ Ju [6:9](https://ref.ly/Judg6:9); sf. 3 ms. אֲגָֽרְשֶׁנּוּ [Ex 23:29](https://ref.ly/Exod23:29), [23:30](https://ref.ly/Exod23:30), [Nu 22:6](https://ref.ly/Num22:6); אֲגָֽרֲשֵׁם [Ho 9:15](https://ref.ly/Hos9:15); וַיְגָֽרֲשׁוּ Ju [11:2](https://ref.ly/Judg11:2); יְגַֽשרשׁוּהַ [Zp. 2:4](https://ref.ly/Zeph2:4), וַיְגָֽרֲשׁוּם [Ex 2:17](https://ref.ly/Exod2:17); 2 mpl. תְּגָֽרֲשׁוּן [Mi 2:9](https://ref.ly/Mic2:9), וַתְּגָֽרֲשׁוּנִי Ju [11:7](https://ref.ly/Judg11:7); *Inf.* cstr. לְגָרֵשׁ [1 Ch 17:21](https://ref.ly/1Chr17:21) \+ ‖ [2 S 7:23](https://ref.ly/2Sam7:23) Gei We Dr, for MT לארצך; לְגָֽרֲשֵׁנוּ [2 Ch 20:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr20:11); abs. *id.* [Ex 11:1](https://ref.ly/Exod11:1); *Imv.* גָּרֵשׁ [Gn 21:10](https://ref.ly/Gen21:10), [Pr 22:10](https://ref.ly/Prov22:10); —*drive out, away*, Hex all JE, incl. poem Dt 33; (´י subj.), Adam fr. garden [Gn 3:24](https://ref.ly/Gen3:24) cf. Cain [Gn 4:14](https://ref.ly/Gen4:14) (sq. מִן loc., \& so 21 t.), David (men subj.) [1 S 26:19](https://ref.ly/1Sam26:19), id. (Abimelech subj.) [Psalm 34:1](https://ref.ly/Ps34:1) (title), Hagar (Abr. subj.) [Gn 21:10](https://ref.ly/Gen21:10) cf. scorner, [Pr 22:10](https://ref.ly/Prov22:10) daughters of Reuel (subj. shepherds) [Ex 2:17](https://ref.ly/Exod2:17); Moses and Aaron fr. LXX; cstr. מִגְרְשֵׁי [Nu 35:4](https://ref.ly/Num35:4) \+ 2 t.; sf. מִגְרָשֶׁיהָ [Jos 21:13](https://ref.ly/Josh21:13), [21:13](https://ref.ly/Josh21:13) \+ 42 t.; מִגְרָשֶׁהָ [Jos 21:11](https://ref.ly/Josh21:11) \+ 46 t. in Jos 21 (generally \+ or . \+ but also ֭ v:[21](https://ref.ly/Josh21:21)); מִגְרְשֵׁיהֶם [Nu 35:3](https://ref.ly/Num35:3) \+ 4 t.; מִגְרְשֵׁיהֶן [Nu 35:7](https://ref.ly/Num35:7) \+ 8 t.—*common\-land*, usually pl., and usually as attached to a city or town, esp. as surrounding Levitical cities [Nu 35:2](https://ref.ly/Num35:2), [35:3](https://ref.ly/Num35:3), [35:4](https://ref.ly/Num35:4), [35:5](https://ref.ly/Num35:5), [35:7](https://ref.ly/Num35:7), [Jos 14:4](https://ref.ly/Josh14:4), [21:2](https://ref.ly/Josh21:2) \+ 55 t. ## גשׁם (id: BDB01960)

גשׁם (√ of foll.; cf. Ar. جَسُمَ *be bulky, massive*, جِسْمٌ *body*, Syr. ܓܽܘܫܡܳܐ *id.*)

> \[Str1654] † I. גֶּ֫שֶׁם **n.pr.m.** an opponent of Nehemiah [Ne 2:19](https://ref.ly/Neh2:19), [6:1](https://ref.ly/Neh6:1) (in both called הָעַרְבִּי); v:[2](https://ref.ly/Neh6:2); also גַּשְׁמוּ [6:6](https://ref.ly/Neh6:6); וּ as in Nab. n.pr. cf. Nö in EutNab 73\.

> \[Str1653] † II. גֶּ֫שֶׁם **n.m.**:[Gn 7:12](https://ref.ly/Gen7:12) rain, shower (NH *id.*, conn. wi. above √ dub.)—abs. ´ג [Gn 7:12](https://ref.ly/Gen7:12) \+; ָ\+גּשֶׁם [1 K 18:41](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:41) \+; cstr. גֶּשֶׁם [Jb 37:6](https://ref.ly/Job37:6), [37:6](https://ref.ly/Job37:6) \+; pl. גְּשָׁמִים Ez [10:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek10:9), [10:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek10:13); cstr. גִּשְׁמֵי Ez [34:26](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:26); sf. גִּשְׁמֵיכֶם [Lv 26:4](https://ref.ly/Lev26:4); גִּשְׁמֵיהֶם [Psalm 105:32](https://ref.ly/Ps105:32); —*rain, shower* [Gn 7:12](https://ref.ly/Gen7:12), [8:2](https://ref.ly/Gen8:2) (both J) [Lv 26:4](https://ref.ly/Lev26:4) (H) [Ho 6:3](https://ref.ly/Hos6:3), [Am 4:7](https://ref.ly/Amos4:7), [Je 5:24](https://ref.ly/Jer5:24), [14:4](https://ref.ly/Jer14:4), [Is 44:14](https://ref.ly/Isa44:14), [55:10](https://ref.ly/Isa55:10), Ez [1:28](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:28) [Jo 2:23](https://ref.ly/Joel2:23) (‖מוֺרֶה, מַלְקוֺשׁ); [1 K 17:7](https://ref.ly/1Kgs17:7), [17:14](https://ref.ly/1Kgs17:14), [18:45](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:45) (´ג גדול) [2 K 3:17](https://ref.ly/2Kgs3:17), [Pr 25:14](https://ref.ly/Prov25:14), [25:23](https://ref.ly/Prov25:23), [Ec 11:3](https://ref.ly/Eccl11:3), Ct [2:11](https://ref.ly/Song2:11), [Psalm 105:32](https://ref.ly/Ps105:32), ´וּמֵהַגּ [Ezr 10:9](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:9) *and because of the rains*, cf. v:[13](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:13); ´קוֺל הֲמוֺן הַגּ [1 K 18:41](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:41) cf. v:[44](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:44) [Zc 14:17](https://ref.ly/Zech14:17), [Ec 12:2](https://ref.ly/Eccl12:2); גֶּשֶׁם מִטְרוֺת עֻזּוֺ ´מָטָר וְג [Jb 37:6](https://ref.ly/Job37:6); מְטַר־גֶּשֶׁם [Zc 10:1](https://ref.ly/Zech10:1); cf. נְדָבוֺת ´ג [Psalm 68:10](https://ref.ly/Ps68:10); fig. of blessing Ez [34:26](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:26), בְּרָכָה ´ג v:[26](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:26); of destruction שֹׁטֵף ´ג Ez [13:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:11), [13:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:13) cf. [38:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek38:22).

> \[Str1652] † \[גשׁם] **vb.denom.** (cf. NH Qal Pt. pass. \& Hoph.)—**Pu.** *Pf.* 3 fs. גֻּשְׁמָ֯ה Ez [22:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:24) *be rained upon* (גֻּשְׁמָהּ Baer, but v. Ke Sm). **Hiph.** *cause* or *send rain*, *Pt.* מַגְשִׁמִים [Je 14:22](https://ref.ly/Jer14:22).

> \[Str1656] † \[גּ֫שֶׁם] **n.\[m.]** גֻּשְַׁמָהּ Ez [22:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:24) (or גִּשְׁמָהּ from גֶּשֶׁם ?) acc. to punct.; but cf. sub גשׁם **vb.**

> \[Str1657] † גּ֫שֶׁן **n.pr.loc.** (on etym. cf. Di [Gn 45:10](https://ref.ly/Gen45:10))—**1\.** district in Egypt E. of lower Nile, v. EbGS 500 ff.; NavilleGoshen, 1887, who rds. hierogl. *Kesem*, but cf. GroffJAs xiv. 527 (LXX Γεσεμ (Ἀραβιας))—אֶרֶץ גּשֶׁן [Gn 45:10](https://ref.ly/Gen45:10) (JE) [46:34](https://ref.ly/Gen46:34), [47:1](https://ref.ly/Gen47:1), [47:4](https://ref.ly/Gen47:4), [47:6](https://ref.ly/Gen47:6), [47:27](https://ref.ly/Gen47:27), [50:8](https://ref.ly/Gen50:8), [Ex 8:18](https://ref.ly/Exod8:18), [9:26](https://ref.ly/Exod9:26) (all J); אַ֫רְצָה גּשֶׁן [Gn 46:28](https://ref.ly/Gen46:28) (J), \+גּשְׁנָה v:[28](https://ref.ly/Gen46:28), v:[29](https://ref.ly/Gen46:29) (both J), in both LXX καθ̓ Ἡρώων πόλιν cf. Di. (P has land רעמסס [47:11](https://ref.ly/Gen47:11) etc., cf. Di [Gn 45:10](https://ref.ly/Gen45:10). **2\.** ארץ הַגּשֶׁן [Jos 11:16](https://ref.ly/Josh11:16) (D), אֶרֶץ גּשֶׁן [Jos 10:41](https://ref.ly/Josh10:41) (D), גּשֶׁן [Jos 15:51](https://ref.ly/Josh15:51) (P) (LXX in all Γοσομ) a city named with חֹלֽן \& גִּלֽה cf. Di.—not clearly located, but on southern border of Judah.

> \[Str1658] † גִּשְׁפָּא **n.pr.m.** named after צִיחָא as an officer of Nethinim [Ne 11:21](https://ref.ly/Neh11:21); v. om. B; LXXL \& Codd. Γεσφα; name not elsewhere in OT, not even ‖ 1 Ch 9; possibly corrupted from חֲשֻפָא [Ne 7:46](https://ref.ly/Neh7:46) (following צִחָא; in ‖ [Ezr 2:43](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:43) חֲשׁוּפָא, צִיחָא), LXX Ἁσφα (B; Codd. Ασειφα, etc.); cf. BeRy.

## גשׁר (id: BDB01968)

גשׁר (√ of foll. Cf. As. *gašâru, strengthen, make firm* LotzTPRegister; Ar. جَسَرَ *be bold*, also *arch a bridge*, cf. جِسْرٌ, جَسْرٌ *bridge*, NH גֶּשֶׁר *id.*, Aram. גִּישְׁרָא, ܓܝܺܫܪܳܐ, ܓܶܫܪܳܐ).

> \[Str1650] † גְּשׁוּר **n.pr.m.** (*bridge* ? or *land of bridges* ? cf. WetzstHauran, 82\). **1\.** of people, \= הַגְּשׁוּרִי [Jos 13:13](https://ref.ly/Josh13:13) cf. [1 Ch 2:23](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:23). **2\.** of land מֶלֶח גְּשׁוּר [2 S 3:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:3) [13:37](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:37) cf. v:[38](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:38) (but text in disorder cf. LXX We Dr) [14:32](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:32), [15:8](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:8) בַּאֲרָם ´בִּגְ [1 Ch 3:2](https://ref.ly/1Chr3:2), גְּשׁ֫וּרָה [2 S 14:23](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:23); a territory E. of the upper Jordan, exact limits unknown; acc. to GutheZPV xii. 1889, 232 f. between Gilead and Hermon, in *Jaulân;* acc. to Furrerib. xiii. 1890, 198 \= the *Ledjah*, district E. of Jaulân, with town *Jisre;* against him Gutheib. 285 f.

> \[Str1651] † גְּשׁוּרִי **adj.gent.** \= subst. ´הַגּ; **1\.** people dwelling in Geshûr (supr.) [Dt 3:14](https://ref.ly/Deut3:14), [Jos 12:5](https://ref.ly/Josh12:5), [13:11](https://ref.ly/Josh13:11), [13:13](https://ref.ly/Josh13:13). **2\.** a tribe of, or near, the Philistines [Jos 13:2](https://ref.ly/Josh13:2), also [1 S 27:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam27:8), but del LXX (not LXXL) cf. ## גשׁשׁ (id: BDB01971)

\[Str1659] † \[גשׁשׁ] **vb.** feel with the hand, feel, stroke (NH *id.*, Ar. دَأَبَ, Aram. גְשַׁשׁ Pa., ܕܘܝܒܐ ܕܢܦܫܐ; Eth. جَسَّ or ܓܰܫ *stroke, touch*)—**Pi.** *Impf.* 1 pl. coh. נְגַשֲׁשָׁ֫ה [Is 59:10](https://ref.ly/Isa59:10), נְגַשֵָּׁשָׁה *ib., grope, grope for* cf. Che.

> \[Str1660–61,1663] גַּת wine\-press. גַּת **n.pr.** גִּתִּי **adj.gent.**

> \[Str1664–65] גִּתַּיִם **n.pr.loc.** גִּתִּית **adj.** v. sub יגן. p. 387 f

> \[Str1666] † גֶּ֫תֶר **n.pr.m.** (√ unknown) a son of Aram [Gn 10:23](https://ref.ly/Gen10:23) \= [1 Ch 1:17](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:17).
